[2.1.1] idānīṃ parapīṭhe śāntikādihomabalidānaśiṣyalakṣaṇakathanārthaṃ prathamaṃ paṭalam āha: bhagavann ityādi. śrotum icchāmīti saṃbandhaḥ. kiṃ tad ity āha: pratibimbasya kīdṛśam | śāntihomakriyāṇāṃ baliyuktyādikaṃ katham iti. śāntikādihomakriyāyāḥ pratibimbaṃ devatāyogaḥ kīdṛśaḥ. śāntikādihomakriyāyā eva praśnaḥ, tadaṅgasya pṛṣṭatvāt.
baliyuktyādikam iti: balidānakāle ca yuktir devatāyogaḥ kīdṛśīti bhāvaḥ. atha vā: śāntikahomādikriyā kīdṛśī, pratibimbaṃ devatāyogaḥ tatra kīdṛśaṃ, balidānaṃ ca kīdṛśam, ādiśabdāc chiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ ca kīdṛśam iti yojyam.
[2.1.2] etad eva visṛjann āha: śṛṇv ityādi. bimbādi-agracetasam iti: devatābimbasvarūpaṃ cittaṃ śṛṇu. śroṣyasīti bhāvaḥ. mantra mudrasyeti: mantraṃ mudrāṃ ca śṛṇv iti yojyam. kasyety āha: homasyeti. mantrety asya nirdeśaḥ: japeti. tasya mantrasya japam. dhyānābhibhāvanam iti: devatābimbasya nirdeśaḥ. homakāle devatāyogamantramudrāḥ śṛṇv iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.3] kiṃ cāgnikarmādi pūrvasya jape jñāsyanti buddhimān iti. mantrajāpād agnikarma homakarma pūrvaṃ jñātavyaṃ buddhimateti bhāvaḥ. homāt pūrvaṃ tāvaj japādikaṃ kartavyam eva. atha vā japāt pūrvaṃ homādikaṃ jñeyam iti bhāvaḥ. bhūmiśodhitamātreṇa vedilakṣaṇa tatparam iti. homārthaṃ prathamaṃ bhūmiśodhanaṃ kāryam. tatparam iti:
tataḥ paraṃ viditalakṣaṇaṃ kuṇḍaṃ khaned iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.4] aṣṭāṅgulād ārabhya yāvat sahasrahastiketi: yāvat sahasrāṅgulam iti bhāvaḥ. atha vāṣṭāṅgulamānād ārabhya yāvad dhastamānam. hastamānāc ca yāvat sahasrahastamānaṃ kuṇḍapramāṇam uktam. aṣṭāṅgulam adhikaṃ gṛhyeti kvacit pāṭhaḥ. aṣṭāṅgulamānādhikaṃ gṛhyamānaṃ yāvat sahasrahastamānaṃ gṛhyata iti vyākhyā. mānam āha: aṣṭamaṃ ripughātaṃ tv iti.
aṣṭāṅgulamānaṃ kuṇḍaṃ ripughātārthaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ. tatrābhicārahoma iti bhāvaḥ. daśamaṃ pauṣṭikas tatheti: daśāṅgulaṃ pauṣṭikahomārtham.
[2.1.5] dvādaśaṃ vaśasaṃyuktam iti: dvādaśāṅgulaṃ vaśahomārtham. caturdaśaṃ śāntim eva ceti: caturdaśāṅgulaṃ rogādyapanayahomārtham. ṣoḍaśākrāntasaṃyuktam iti: ṣoḍaṣāṅgulaṃ putrapautrādivṛddhihomārtham. aputreṇāpi putrārthaṃ homaḥ kārayitavyaḥ.
[2.1.6] aṣṭādaśasthānasya gokulādibhi vardhata iti: aṣṭādaśāṅgulaṃ sthānaṃ gokulahastyaśvādikapauṣṭikaśāntihomārtham. viṃśa-m-aṅguṣṭha vedīnām iti: viṃśatyaṅgulaṃ vedīnāṃ kuṇḍam ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.7] ākrāntasya tṛtīyena dvibhir bhāgaṃ tu khānitam iti: ākrāntasyeti kuṇḍapramāṇasya. tṛtīyeneti: bhāgatrayaṃ kṛtvā. bhāgadvayapramāṇena kuṇḍasya khananam. kuṇḍamukhasya parimāṇaṃ tribhāgaṃ kṛtvā bhāgadvayena khananam iti bhāvaḥ. sarveṣām eva kuṇḍānāṃ sāmānyena khananalakṣaṇam idam. khānitā-r-aṣṭabhāgaṃ tv iti: khātasyāṣṭamena bhāgena.
kiṃ tad ity āha: oṣṭhenāpi tu kuṇḍiketi. kuṇḍasyauṣṭham iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.8] oṣṭhasya ardhabhāgaṃ tu nemi bāhyaṃ tu kārayed iti: oṣṭhād bahir nemi-r-aṣṭādhikaṃ yuktaṃ padmeti nemer aṣṭamabhāgikaṃ padmam oṣṭhagatam. patrasya r-aṣṭamam iti: padmasyāṣṭamabhāgikaṃ padmapatram ity arthaḥ. atha vā: nemi-r-aṣṭādhikam iti nemer navamo bhāgaḥ. tatpramāṇaṃ nemigatam aṣṭadalaṃ padmam iti.
[2.1.9] bāhyānām iti: nemer bahiḥ padmaprākāra ekāṅgulaḥ. vajrāṇām iti: kuṇḍamadhye padmopari yoginīnāṃ cihnāni likhanīyāni. atha vā vajrasattvasya cihnaṃ vajram.
[2.1.10] trivedīm abhicāre tv ityādinā kuṇḍānāṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇam āha. trivedīm abhicāre tv iti: tryasram ity arthaḥ. paripūrṇaṃ vaśasaṃyuktam iti: vartulam ity arthaḥ. atha vā pauṣṭikam eva ceti: pauṣṭikakuṇḍam api vartulam ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.11] ardhacandram uccāṭā iti: ardhacandrākāram. aṣṭāṃśa karṣastambhanam ity: ākṛṣṭistambhanayor aṣṭāṃśaṃ kuṇḍam. kramakrameṇa yoktavyaṃ śāntipauṣṭivaśādikam iti: ukteṣu kuṇḍeṣu śāntyādikaṃ krameṇa draṣṭavyam iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.12] kuṇḍamadhye tv ityāder (9c) nirdeśam āha: pañcetyādi. pañcabuddhasvarūpāṇāṃ yoginīnāṃ yāni cihnāni yathādiśaṃ nyaset. tad evāha: yathādiśadiśaṃ nyased iti. tatra kuṇḍamadhye padme sūryāsane vajraṃ pūrvottarapaścimadakṣinadaleṣu khaṭvāṅgam eva. catur ityādi: oṣṭhasya catasṛṣu dikṣu krameṇa cakraratnapadmakhaḍgān likhet. etad evāha:
buddhacihnena laṃkṛtam iti.
[2.1.13] ardhacandropari vajraṃ varaṇḍakakoṇeṣu likhanīyam. etad evāha: candretyādi. yathā-m-āsana-m-abhisthānaiḥ kailāsam ucchritaṃ śubham iti: ācāryasyāsanāgravāmapārśve kailāsaḥ kāryaḥ.
[2.1.14] kiyatpramāṇa ity āha: pauruṣārdhasaṃyuktam iti. pauruṣaṃ hastamātraṃ tasya cārdhaṃ vitastiḥ. tiryagucchrayābhyāṃ sārdhahastapramāṇa ity arthaḥ. aṅguṣṭhā dvādaśena tv iti: tatra ca sopānaṃ dvādaśāṅgulaṃ draṣṭavyam iti bhāvaḥ. dale aṣṭābhi sthānasya krama devābhi sthāpayed iti:
kuṇḍasya sarvathā bahir aṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ tasya ca daleṣu pūrvādyaṣṭasu krameṇa devīnāṃ cihnāni likhet. tatra pūrvadale ḍākinīcihnaṃ mukhe hastadvayaṃ praviṣṭam. uttare dīpinīcihnaṃ mūrdhāñjalirūpam. paścime cūṣinīcihnaṃ rudhirāñjalipānarūpam. dakṣiṇe kāmbojīcihnaṃ musalākāram.
aiśānādidaleṣu siṃhīvyāghrījambukyulūkīnāṃ cihnam aṅkuśam eva kevalaṃ likhet. cihnāni kiṃvarṇānīty āha: ākrāntasyetyādi. ākrāntānām āropitānāṃ cihnānām. yoginām iti: yoginā. viśeṣeṇa tv iti: śāntikādikarmaviśeṣeṇa. tad ayam arthaḥ: cihnānāṃ varṇabhedaḥ karmabhedato draṣṭavyaḥ.
[2.1.15] oṃ hili hili oṃ kili kili oṃ vettāli hūṃ iti mantraḥ.
[2.1.16] upacāreti: hotavyavastuprokṣaṇādika upacāraḥ.
[2.1.17] bhūmiśodhanād ārabhya jñānaḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ dhyāyād ity āha: bhāvayed ityādi. jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayet. ātmadehena nirmitam iti: ātmānam eva jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. kavacanam āha: kalanam ityādi. ātmasaṃsthāna iti: ātmamūrtau jñānaḍākinīrūpāyām. ātmānaṃ sthāneti pāṭhe 'py ayam evārthaḥ.
[2.1.18] jihvā āpam idaṃ caiveti: āpam idam ity amṛtaṃ jalam ity arthaḥ. tadviśuddhaṃ suṃbījaṃ, tac ca jihvāyām ity arthaḥ. jihvā āpaṃ caiveti pāṭhe 'pi sa evārthaḥ. ākāśaṃ karṇanāsayor iti: ākāsaṃ yuṃbījaṃ, tac ca karṇadvaye nāsāpuṭadvaye ca. pṛthvī ambum idaṃ yuktam iti: pṛthvīviśuddhaṃ huṃbījaṃ, tac cāmbunoś cakṣuṣor iti yāvat.
agni brahmam idaṃ tatheti: agniḥ kṣuṃbījaṃ, tac ca brahmasthāne tālunīty arthaḥ. vajraḍākinīvettālīcaṇḍālīghorīṇām etāni bījāni krameṇa jñeyāni.
[2.1.19] moha sārathi grīvebhya iti: mohaviśuddhaṃ smryuṃbījaṃ, tac ca sārathibhūtaṃ grīvāyām. calitā vāyus tadviśuddhaṃ ymryuṃbījaṃ, tac ca hṛdaye. kleśā ura sarvāṇām iti: uraśabdena bāhuḥ. kleśaviśuddhaṃ hmryuṃbījaṃ bāhumūlayoḥ. paśubhyām iti: paśuśabdenolūkī, tasyāḥ kṣmryuṃ bījaṃ, tac ca nābhimūle.
siṃhīvyāghrījambukyulūkīnāṃ krameṇaitāni bījāni.
[2.1.20] prakaraṇāyātaṃ kalanaṃ pratinirdiṣṭaṃ: kalana aṅgam aṣṭena aṣṭa devasya jñāpayed iti. aṣṭānāṃ devīnām aṣṭabhir bījair aṣṭāṅgakalanam iti bhāvaḥ. katham ity āha: tripatākenetyādi. vṛddhāṅguṣṭhena kanīyasim ākramya śeṣāṅgulimokṣe kṛte tripatākāmudrā bhavati. tayā pratibījaṃ nyasanīyam.
[2.1.21] jñānaḍākinīyogena hotavyam iti smārayann āha: nirmitam ityādi vyākhyātam.
[2.1.22] khaṭvāṅgavajrahastānām iti: dakṣiṇe vajraṃ vāme khaṭvāṅgam ity arthaḥ. samāhiteti: laukikavimukho yogī, evaṃviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāṃ jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayed iti saṃbandhaḥ. ātmadehenātmaśarīreṇātmānaṃ jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. śvāsaniścalam ity vyākhyātam.
[2.1.23] atha bhūmiśodhanaṃ kāryam iti: ādau devatāyogas tato bhūmiśodhanaṃ, tataḥ kuṇḍakhananam iti kramaḥ. tataś ca devatāyogād anantaraṃ bhūmiśodhanaṃ kāryam.
[2.1.24] tad evāha: oṃ hana hana krodha hūṃ phaṭ.
[2.1.25] savyahasta prasāryaṃ ca vajrabhāvana sphālanam iti: dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ prasārya tatra pañcasūkaṃ vajraṃ dhyātvā sveṣṭadevatāyogī tena vajrānvitena hastenā rasātalaṃ vajramayaṃ pañcadevīviśuddhyā pañcasthānasphālanād dhyāyād iti mudrā. mantraḥ prāg uktaḥ.
[2.1.26–27] dvau hastau saṃpuṭamuṣṭīnāṃ madhyamā dvau prasārayed iti: muṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā
madhyamādvayaṃ sūcyākāreṇa prasārya saṃyojya ca dhārayed iti.
[2.1.28] eṣa mudreti: eṣā mudrā. sarvavastuśodhanam iti: ghṛtādisarvavastuśodhanam. mantraḥ pṛthak pṛthak. anayaikayā mudrayā vakṣyamāṇaiḥ pṛthakpṛthaṅmantrair ghṛtādiśodhanaṃ kāryam.
[2.1.29] mantrān āha: oṃ śriṃ svāhetyādi.
[2.1.30] ghṛtaśodhanam ādiṃ kṛtvā yāvat samicchodhanam iyam eva mudrā.
[2.1.31–39] agnyākarṣaṇam āha: agnimadhya ityādi. kuṇḍamadhye 'gniṃ prajvālya tasya madhye ca padmaṃ (42b) tanmadhye ca raṃkārapariṇataṃ sūryasthaṃ kapilajaṭādharaṃ (42c) guḍavarṇaṃ vāmadakṣiṇakuṇḍikākṣasūtradhāriṇaṃ (42d) jvālākulam vahniṃ (42f) cintayitvā tādṛśam vahnim ākṛṣya tatra praveśayet.
[2.1.40] etad evāha: vāmetyādi. kuṇḍasyauṣṭhe kuśaviṇḍikāyāṃ vāmahastamuṣṭiṃ dattvā madhyamāṃ tarjanīṃ ca samutthāpya madhyamātṛtīyaparvarekhāyāṃ tarjanīṃ kuñcitāṃ niveśyānayā mudrayā ...
[2.1.41] ... oṃ āgaccha āgaccha agni jaḥ svāheti mantreṇa ca karṣayet.
[2.1.42] agnirūpam āha: bhāvayed ityādi subodham.
[2.1.43] pūjayed ityādinā vahnipūjām āha. etad uktam: prāguktanyāyenāgnim ākṛṣyārghapādyādikaṃ tasmai dattvā tena jñānāgninā kuṇḍamadhye bhāvitaṃ samayāgniṃ samarasīkṛtya puṣpadhūpādibhiḥ pūjayed iti. yathāsaṃbhāvya tatra ceti: yathāvibhavaṃ pūjāṃ vidhāya juhuyād iti pareṇa saṃbandhaḥ. tribhiḥ sarveṣām iti:
sarvavastūni vāratrayaṃ juhuyād iti bhāvaḥ. kvety āha: agnimadhye tu jihviketi. agnijihvāyām ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.44] dvāv ityādi: añjaliṃ kṛtvā padmākāreṇa vikāsya sarvāṅgulimadhye madhyamā†kāreṇa†dvaye samutthāpite vahnihṛdayamudreyam.
[2.1.45] oṃ agni juhu juhu svaḥ svāheti mantreṇa vahnaye mudrām imāṃ darśayet.
[2.1.46–47] paścāt kiṃ kuryād ity āha: tricchoṭyaṃ dāpya yatneneti. mudrāṃ baddhvā mantram imam uccārayañ choṭikātrayaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ. evaṃ kṛte kuṇḍavahnāv ākṛṣṭo vahnir jvālākāreṇa līyate samarasībhavatīty arthaḥ. atra śodhanam ārambha agnimadhye tu sarvavid iti:
agnimadhye sveṣṭadevatābhāvanākāle kuṇḍamadhyaṃ hotavyavastūni ca prokṣaṇena śodhayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.48] kathaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād ity āha: oṃ hūṃ ṭaṭṭi phaḍ ityādi.
[2.1.49] udakaṃ sarṣapāḥ puṣpaṃ vajram †...† iti. etad uktam: pāṇinā vajraṃ gṛhītvā sarṣapapuṣpasahitena jalena pūrvoktaprokṣaṇamudrāyuktena cānena mantreṇa sarva(?)śodhanaṃ kāryam iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.50] śodhanamudrāmantra iti: mudrā prokṣaṇārthaṃ pūrvokteti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.51] oṃ vajraprākāretyādi subodham.
[2.1.52] aiśānakoṇam ārabhya bhrāmayed itīśānyetyāder arthaḥ. (!?)
[2.1.53–54] dvau hastau prasārya spṛśed agraṃ tu nonyavad iti: hastadvayaṃ prasārya mastakopari parasparasyāṅgulīr ā mūlaṃ niścārya dhārayed iti pañjaramudrā.
[2.1.55] oṃ vajra svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.1.56–57] vāmahastaṃ muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭham adhomukhaṃ dhārayed iti vāmetyāder arthaḥ.
[2.1.58–59] prākārādikaṃ bhūmiśodhanakāle devatāyoge ca draṣṭavyam.
[2.1.60] agniṃ hutvā sveṣṭadevatāṃ juhuyād iti darśayann āha: agnimadhya ityādi. kuṇḍagatāgnimadhye padmaṃ, tadupari sūryamaṇḍalaṃ, ...
[2.1.61] ... tadupari hūṃkārapariṇataṃ pañcasūkavajraṃ, tadvaraṭake hūṃkāraṃ vīkṣya, tatsarvapariṇatāṃ raktādivarṇāṃ dakṣiṇavāmabhujakhaṭvāṅgavajradharāṃ (?) bhāvayet.
[2.1.62] ātmaśvāsena niḥsṛtam iti: ātmanaḥ śarīrān nirgatā jñānasattvī. karmasyaiva tu varṇānām iti: śāntikādikarmānurūpavarṇā jñānasattvī. samayajñānaḍākiny api karmānurūpavarṇeti jñeyam. prāg uktaṃ ca: raktavarṇādīti (61b). ādiśabdād itare 'pi varṇā grāhyāḥ. yogapīṭhiketi: sattvaparyaṅkasthā.
evaṃviśiṣṭāṃ jñānasattvīṃ svaśarīrān niścārya samayajñānaḍākinyā †...mantraṃ† bhāvayet. sā cāgnimadhyagatā jñānaḍākinī svadehasvabhāvā.
[2.1.63] ata āha: yata ityādi. pūjayed ityādi subodham.
[2.1.64–65] oṃ ṭha ṭha ṭha svāheti: ṭhaśabdena hūṃkāratrayam. oṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ svāheti mantraḥ syāt.
[2.1.66] daṇḍam iti sruvam.
[2.1.67] dvau hastau saṃpuṭamuṣṭīnāṃ madhyamā dvau prasārayed iti (27) yā sarvavastuśodhanamudrā, tasyā mantro 'yaṃ: oṃ hūṃ svāheti.
[2.1.68] etad evāha: mūlamudrāmantra iti. bhūmiśodhanānantaraṃ sarvavastuśodhanaṃ mūlamudrayā pṛthakpṛthaṅmantrair agnimadhye sveṣṭadevatābhāvanākāle cānena mantreṇa pūrvamudrayā sarvavastuśodhanaṃ kāryam.
[2.1.69] sarvapūjāmantrān āha: oṃ ityādi.
[2.1.70–75] dakṣiṇaṃ (?) hastaṃ prasārya gandhalepanadānābhinayena gandhamudrā.
[2.1.76–77] dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ prasārya aṅguṣṭhatarjanyau saṃyojya śeṣāḥ prasārayed iti puṣpamudrā.
[2.1.78–79] dakṣiṇahastamuṣṭer aṅguṣṭhotthānena dīpamudrā.
[2.1.80–81] puṣpamudrāyām iti: puṣpamudrāyāṃ madhyamāṅgulisamutthānād dhūpamudrā.
[2.1.82–83] hastadvayaṃ prasārya madhyamādvayam aṅguṣṭhadvaye spṛśed iti balimudrā. yathākramaṃ mantrair mudrāṇāṃ yogaḥ.
[2.1.84] sakṛj japya sakṛd dāpya agnibhyāṃ mukhajihviketi: sakṛd āhutimantraṃ japtvā pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyāt. agnimukhajihvāyām oṃ hūṃ 3 svāheti mantraṃ japtvā dadyād ity arthaḥ. sakṛj jāpyāhutyā mukhajihvikair iti pāṭhāntaram. ihāpy ayam evārthaḥ. saptādhikaṃ tu saṃgṛhya madhye madhyamapiṇḍiketi: saptāhutayo dātavyāḥ.
madhya ity agneḥ, sveṣṭadevatājihvāmadhye. madhyamapiṇḍiketi: āhutisaṃjñā.
[2.1.85] āhutyāvṛtta śeṣās tu śeṣākāreṇa tatparam iti: āhutau vṛttāyāṃ śeṣaṃ vastu hotavyam. prathamaṃ vahnimukhe pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā hutaṃ yat tasmāc cheṣam iti boddhavyam. tasminn api pūrṇāhutidānapūrvakaṃ hute tadanyac cheṣam ity arthaḥ. etenaitad uktam: agnimukhe prathamam ekāhutiṃ hutvā sarvaṃ vastu juhuyāt.
sarvavastuhomānte punaḥ pūrṇāhutir ekā. tadanantaraṃ vahnau sveṣṭadevatāmukhe pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā taccheṣaṃ vastu juhuyāt. tato homāntaṃ pūrṇāhutiḥ. punaḥ sveṣṭadevatāṃ kṣamayitvā vahnidevatāṃ punar vibhāvya tanmukhe pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā śeṣaṃ juhuyāt. tato 'pi homānte pūrṇāhutiḥ. tadanantaraṃ dānapatihastena vahnau pūrṇāhutir ekā dāpayitavyeti.
itthaṃ saptāhutayo niṣpadyanta iti catuṣpīṭhe vyavasthitam. hutavṛtteti devānām ātmadehasya līyata iti: home vṛtte yā bhāvitā sveṣṭadevatā sātmani praveśayitavyeti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.86] saṃpūrṇaṃ sarvam āhutyā pūrṇamantraṃ tu mudraketi: sarvaṃ pūrṇāhutimantramudrādikaṃ kartavyam iti bhāvaḥ. ghṛta saṃpūrṇabhāṇḍena agnimadhye tu dāpayed iti: home vṛtte sveṣṭadevatātmadehe līyata ity (85d) uddiṣṭārthasyānena nirdeśaḥ kriyate.
sarvathā home vṛtte saptāhutyavasāne vahnimadhye sveṣṭadevatāṃ bhāvayitvā tanmukhe ghṛtasaṃpūrṇāṃ pātrīṃ sruvaṃ ca kṛtvā āhutir ekā dātavyā. tataś ca sveṣṭadevatāyāṃ pūrṇāhutidvayam. tataḥ punaḥ sveṣṭadevatānantaraṃ vahnau pūrṇāhutir ekā deyā. vahner apy āhutidvayaṃ syāt. prāk sveṣṭadevatāvahnyor ekaikāhutiḥ kṣiptā.
[2.1.87] ātmabhāvaṃ tu m-ākarṣa ātmabhāvasya līyatām iti: katham ātmani sveṣṭadevatā līyata iti darśayati. ātmaśvāsenātmani sā praveśayitavyeti bhāvaḥ. paścād ātmadehena bhāvayantu vicakṣaṇa iti: paścād ātmani praviṣṭām ātmadehasadṛśīṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.88] agnidevasya madhye tu bhāvayeta vicakṣaṇa iti: ātmani sveṣṭadevatāpraveśānantaram agnau prokṣaṇādikaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye cāgniṃ prāguktanyāyena vibhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. śeṣā tribhi m-āhutyā pūjābalidāpitair iti: dhyāte vahnau śeṣaṃ havyaṃ pūrṇāhutidānapūrvakaṃ vāratrayaṃ hutvā baliṃ pūjādikaṃ dattvā vahniṃ kṣamayet.
pūrvaṃ saptāhutayaḥ, paścād āhutidvayaṃ, adhunā āhutayas tisraḥ. samudāyena dvādaśāhutayaḥ. maṅgaletyādi: maṅgalagītaṃ vakṣyati (4.4.93). agre dvau hastāv ityādinā (44a) mudroktā.
[2.1.89] aparai hutakarmabhedaṃ tu pravakṣyāmi samāsata iti: aparaṃ śāntikādikarmabhedaṃ saṃkṣepena pravakṣyāmīti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.90] kṣīrapeyaṃ tv iti: kṣīrapāyasam. kṣīramaṇḍa iti kecit.
[2.1.91] agniprajvālanāya samidham āha: pañcetyādi. āmravaṭanyagrodhaparkaṭyaśvatthakāṣṭhāni grāhyāṇi. caturdaśāṅguṣṭhakrāntavad iti: caturdaśāṅgulam indhanam. śāntike kālasaṃprāpta iti prāg ukto vidhiḥ.
[2.1.92] adhunā puṣṭikam āha: tilakṛṣṇādītyādi. kṛṣṇatila ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.93] ghṛtakṣīrasya peyeneti: ghṛtamiśrakṣīrapāyasam. ākrāntā daśa-m-aṅgulam iti: daśāṅgulam indhanam. ākrānta daśamas tatheti pāṭhāntare 'pi tathaiva.
[2.1.94] vaśam āha: tilaraktasyetyādi. kṛṣṇasyeti: kṛṣṇatilaḥ, raktapuṣpam. aśurīti: rājikā. latāphalam iti kecit. madhu sarpiṣayojitam iti subodham. madhusarpeṇa yojitam iti pāṭhāntare madhughṛtābhyāṃ yojitam ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.95] nāgakāṣṭham iti: nāgakesarakāṣṭham. raktakarmādīti: vaśam ākṛṣṭiṃ ca sādhayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.1.96] phalakālaketi: mahākālaphalam.
[2.1.97] kālavṛkṣasyeti: hariṭhetiprasiddhasya. kubjasyeti: kujākaṇḍetiprasiddhasya. bhallātaguḍim eva ceti: bhallātakakāṣṭham. aṣṭāṅguṣṭhakrāntasyeti: aṣṭāṅgulamānam indhanam abhicāre.
[2.1.98–99] kāñcanasya tv iti: dhuttūravṛkṣasya. uccāṭane 'py aṣṭāṅgulamānam indhanam.
[2.1.100–101] gṛhakākasya kāṣṭhādīti: kākagṛhakāṣṭham ity arthaḥ. m-utsiṣṭā dantakāṣṭhakair iti: khāditadantakāṣṭhaiḥ. devadāruvāsānāgakesarakāṣṭhāni kālavṛkṣakubjabhallātakakāṣṭhāni kāñcanavāsākāṣṭhāni kākagṛhakāṣṭhadantakhāditakāṣṭhāni vahniprajvālanārthaṃ, śeṣaṃ hotavyam.
[2.1.102–105] sundarīti: pṛthvī. bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti: punaḥ pṛthvī, antarikṣaṃ svargaś ceti. pṛthvīṃ kevalāṃ sādhayati lokatrayaṃ veti bhāvaḥ.
[2.1.106] athetyādinā śiṣyalakṣaṇam āha.
[2.1.107] paripūrṇaṃ sarvam aṅgasyeti: paripūrṇāvayavaḥ. atha vā: aṅgaśabdena śabdaśāstrādikam. tad evāha: vinītetyādi. vinīta iti jñātaśabdaśāstrādiḥ. śāntacāriketi: jitendriyaḥ. abhirati tribhi dharmasyeti: śrāvakapratyekamahāyāneṣu rataḥ. mahāyānamati sadeti: mahāyānābhyāse vyāsaktamatiḥ. tathā cāha:
śrāvakasaṃvarastho mahāyānakṛtacittotpāda iti.
[2.1.108] mantraprayogaḥ śāntikādimantraprayuktiḥ. tantraprayogaḥ śāntikādicakralikhanādikam. abhirato yogadhyānaṃ ceti: dhyāne rataḥ. śrutapāṭhāditatparam iti: pravacanaśravaṇe tatpāṭhe ca nimnaḥ.
[2.1.109] gurubhakteti: kāyavākcittair gurusevakaḥ. guṇānantaraṃ doṣam āha: na tv ityādi subodham.
[2.1.110–111] evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭāya doṣavarjitāya ca tattvaṃ kim api deyam iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā tathāvidha iha tantre śiṣyaḥ kārya iti.
[2.1.col.] ity uktakrameṇa homādikaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaṃ parapīṭham. iti parapīṭhe catuṣpīṭhanibandhe prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ.

[2.2.1] idānīṃ jalahomapaṭalam āha: śrutam ityādi. śrotuṃ jalahomaṃ kautūhalaṃ me vartata iti bhāvaḥ. asyaiva nirdeśam āha: katham ityādi. jalahomasya lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ kīdṛśaṃ tattvata iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.2.2] śṛṇv ityādi: tattvam iti jalahomasvarūpam. yatheti nidarśane. jalahomatattvaṃ yathā tathā śṛṇv ity arthaḥ. tad evāha: samyak lakṣya jalādikam iti: jalam ādito nirīkṣya samyag yatnato homaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. prāṇakarahitaṃ susthānabhavaṃ svacchaṃ nirīkṣya jalaṃ grāhyam. vitānavitatāś caiva nānāvastrapralambakā iti:
yatra homaḥ kartavyas tatra vitānaṃ vistārayet, nānādhvajāṃś ca.
[2.2.3] samantād ityādi: vijane pradeśe gomayopalipte vitānādiyukte gandhena samantāc caturhastaṃ caturasraṃ maṇḍalaṃ kāryam. suvistaram ity upadeśāc caturhastatvaṃ sūcitam. madhye jalabhāṇḍādīti: gandhamaṇḍalamadhye mṛnmayaṃ caturdaśāṅgulaṃ mukhapramāṇasya tribhāgikasya bhāgadvayena khātapramāṇaṃ śāntikakuṇḍavad (cf.
2.1.7ab) bhāṇḍaṃ kālimādirahitaṃ ca sthāpayed iti śeṣaḥ. sitagandhādi liptayed iti: bhāṇḍaṃ śvetacandanena liptaṃ kuryāt.
[2.2.4] punaḥ kīdṛśam ity āha: udaka kṣīrasaṃmiśram iti. prāguktena pariśuddhena jalena kṣīramiśreṇa paripūrṇaṃ tad bhāṇḍaṃ maṇḍalamadhye sthāpayitvāṣṭasu dikṣv aṣṭau gandhodakapūrṇān sapallavamukhān (5ab) sthāpayed iti bhāvaḥ. ata āha: kalaśānām ityādi.
[2.2.5] trimukhaṣaḍbhujādirūpajñānaḍākinīrūpeṇātmānaṃ jhaṭiti niṣpādya vakṣyamāṇaṃ kāryam ity āha: jhaṭitetyādi.
[2.2.6] pūjayed ityādi: ātmanā puṣpadhūpādibhir vakṣyamāṇaṃ bhāṇḍādimadhyagatadevatāsamūhaṃ pūjayed iti bhāvaḥ. bhāṇḍamadhye devatābhāvanāṃ vaktum āha: paścād ityādi. ātmani jñānaḍākinībhāvanānantaraṃ bāhye bhāṇḍe saṃsthānaṃ devatārūpaṃ jalahomārthaṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.2.7] hastadvayam ityādi: hastadvayaṃ sampuṭīkṛtya kamalākāreṇa kiṃcidvikāsāt kamalamudrāṃ ...
[2.2.8] ... oṃ kamalābhe svāheti mantreṇa yuktāṃ bhāṇḍāya darśayet.
[2.2.9–10] tato jalamadhye padmaṃ dṛśyate. etad evāha: jalamadhya ityādi. nyased iti: mudrādarśanamātreṇa padmam utpadyata iti padmanyāsaḥ.
jalamadhye padmaṃ, tatra ca candramaṇḍalaṃ, tatra hūṃkārabījaṃ, tadbījaniṣpannāṃ dvibhujāṃ sattvaparyaṅkāṃ (11cd) vajralāñchitadaṇḍadvayaṃ hastadvayena bibhratīṃ, sitavarṇāṃ (12ab), kruddhahasitasaśṛṅgāradakṣiṇavāmamūlānanāṃ (12c) jñānaḍākinīṃ vicintyātmasakāśāj jñānasattvīṃ tatsadṛśīṃ niścārya tasyāṃ praveśayet. śvetetyādinā tad evāha:
dhṛtaśvetacchattrāṃ jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayet. katham ity āha: bījamadhyaketi. candre bījaṃ dṛṣṭvety arthaḥ.
[2.2.11] tena bījena jñānaḍākinīrūpaṃ jalamadhye niṣpādya paścād ātmaśvāsenātmaśarīrāj jñānasattvīṃ niścārya praveśayed iti bhāvaḥ. jñānasattvīrūpam āha: dvibhujam ityādi.
[2.2.12] dvipāṇasyeti: pāṇidvaye daṇḍadvayam ity arthaḥ. hasitakrodhaśṛṅgārety anena mukhatrayaṃ gamyate. samayasattvīrūpam apy evam. samayasattvyāṃ jñānasattvyai mudrāṃ darśayitvā tāṃ praveśayed ity āha: darśayed ityādi.
[2.2.13] mudrām āha: dvau hastāv ityādi. hastadvayena muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā parasparasya pṛṣṭhena pṛṣṭhaṃ yojayitvā prasāritena tarjanīdvayena granthiṃ kuryād iti samayamudrā.
[2.2.14–15] oṃ samaye tiṣṭha hūṃ phaḍ ity anena mantreṇa tāṃ darśayet.
[2.2.16] pūrvādikalaśeṣu yoginīnyāsam āha: kalaśa ityādi. pūrvakalaśe vajrī. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[2.2.17–18] pṛthak pṛthak cihnaṃ tu darśayed iti: pūrvādiṣu kalaśeṣu svasvabījaniṣpannaṃ vajryādikaṃ yoginīsamūhaṃ vīkṣya vakṣyamāṇāṃ mudrāṃ pratyekaṃ yoginībhyo yathākramaṃ darśayed iti bhāvaḥ. samayamudrāvad iti: yathā pratyekaṃ yoginījñānasattvyai pūrvoktā samayamudrā darśayitavyā tathaiva tābhyo vakṣyamāṇā mūlamudreti bhāvaḥ.
tataḥ pūjāṃ kuryād ity āha: puṣpetyādi. vicakṣaṇa iti: pūjyādibhedarahitaḥ.
[2.2.19] mūlamudrām āha: vajretyādi. vāmamuṣṭiṃ hṛdi sthāpayitvā dakṣiṇamuṣṭiṃ tadupari dadyād iti mūlamudrā.
[2.2.20] oṃ jrūṃ hūṃ svāheti mantreṇa tāṃ darśayet.
[2.2.21] mūlamantra iti: mūlamudrāmantra ity arthaḥ. japyaś cāyam. lakṣatraye japte pūrvaseveti draṣṭavyam. tato 'nena mantreṇa hotavyam.
[2.2.22] sitapuṣpādikundasyeti: yat kiṃcit sitapuṣpaṃ kundapuṣpam avaśyaṃ yojyam. dūrvān āgraṃ tu dāpayed iti: puṣpamūle dūrvāgraṃ prakṣipya tena puṣpeṇa pūrvoktaṃ mantram uccārya bhāṇḍamadhyadhyātāyā devyāḥ kirīṭivajraṃ tāḍayan juhuyāt. tad evāha: hanayed ityādi subodham.
[2.2.23] sakṛd ityādi: mantram uccāryoccāryaikaikavāraṃ puṣpam ekaikaṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.2.24] dūrveṇa samanvitam iti: raktakaravīrapuṣpaṃ dūrvānuviddhaṃ juhuyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.2.25] samidhā nāgapuṣpeṇeti: nāgakesarapuṣpāṇy eva samidha iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.2.26] yuñjayed ityādi: padmasamidbhir home kṛte vividhāni karmāṇi kuryād ity arthaḥ. tad evāha: ayutam ityādi.
[2.2.27] culukam ityādi: pūrvoktāṃ bhagavatīṃ tādṛśabhāṇḍamadhyasthāṃ dṛṣṭvāṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ jalaculukān uccārya mūlamantraṃ tasyai dadyād iti bhāvaḥ. pauṣṭikam iti: dhanādivṛddhir bhavati.

[2.3.1] devatāyogaṃ spaṣṭayituṃ samayaṃ ca kathayituṃ tṛtīyaṃ paṭalam āha: śṛṇu vajretyādi. yena saṃsārapāragamanaṃ tasyādvayajñānasya lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇv iti bhāvaḥ. devatāyogo hi pūrvam eva pṛṣṭaḥ, tadaṅgopāṅgopadeśāya punar adhyeṣaṇā na kṛtā.
āvartyate sa evārthaḥ punar arthāntarāśritaḥ |
iti vacanāt. devatāyogaspaṣṭīkaraṇe śūnyatāṃ tāvad āha: bhāvetyādi. bhāvaḥ sattā. abhāvo 'sattā. tābhyāṃ vinirmuktam. pramāṇabādhitatvād bhāvāś cen na santi, tadabhāvo 'pi katham? dharmyabhāvāt. śuddhāśuddhiṃ tu varjayed iti: śuddhir niḥkleśatā. aśuddhiḥ sakleśatā. tābhyāṃ varjitam ity arthaḥ.
prakṛtipariśuddhatvād advayajñānasyāgantukatvāc ca kleśānām. bhāvābhāvādivarjitam api rūpam advayasya nāsti. bhāvādikaṃ cen nāsti, tadvarjitatvaṃ katham? tathā cāha:
astīti nāstīti ime 'pi cāntaṃ śuddhīty aśuddhīti ime 'pi cāntam |
tasmād ubhāv antaṃ vivarjayitvā madhye 'pi sthānaṃ na karoti paṇḍitaḥ ||
iti.
[2.3.2] īdṛśam advayaṃ jñānaṃ yat tad eva sarvadharmā ity āha: adaityetyādi. sarve dharmāḥ padārthā advayasaṃjñāḥ. yatas te dvayavarjitā iti bhāvaḥ.
advayāḥ sarvadharmā vai dharmalakṣaṇavarjitāḥ |
iti vacanāt. itthaṃ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśūnyā iti svabhāvaśūnyaṃ jagat. ānimittaṃ ca:
na svato nāpi parato na dvābhyāṃ nāpy ahetutaḥ |
utpannā jātu vidyante bhāvāḥ kvacana kecana ||
iti vacanāt. apraṇihitaṃ ca. na hi niḥsvabhāve jagati praṇidhānam upapadyate. devatāyogārambhamadhyāvasāneṣu śūnyatā bhāvyeti darśayati: rūpetyādi. rūpaskandhādīnāṃ śūnyatā, sā cādau madhye 'nte ca bhāvyety arthaḥ.
[2.3.3] aśucyetyādi: aśucyam aśucisalobhatvam. śucīti tadviparītam. madhyam iti: śucyaśucirahitam. itthaṃ samatā samarasatā. taddarśanaṃ kevalaṃ syād ity arthaḥ. atha vā śucīti pavitram. aśuci tadanyat. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat. etac ca śuddhāśuddhiṃ tv ity (1d) asya nirdeśaḥ. sevyetyādi: sevyaṃ gamyam. asevitam agamyam. madhyaṃ taddvayarahitam.
itthaṃ samajñānasamudbhavam: jñānasya cittasya samatā samudbhavatīty arthaḥ.
[2.3.4] samasaṃsthitadehānām iti: pūrvoktanyāyenaiva kāyasya samatā syād ity arthaḥ. samasaṃsthitavākyavad iti: vāco 'pi samatety arthaḥ. tad ayam arthaḥ: yathā cittaṃ svasaṃvinmātraṃ, tathā kāyo 'pi. yathā kāyo 'pi paramāṇuśo vibhajyamāno na ko 'pi, tathā cittam api.
yathā cittakāyau, tathā vāg api, bahirarthaṃ prati śabdasya paramārthato 'vācakatvāt. itthaṃ kāyādīnāṃ samatā. tathā cāha: utpādayantu bhavantaś cittaṃ kāyākāreṇa kāyaṃ cittākāreṇa cittaṃ vākpravyāhāreṇeti. etena kiṃ syād ity āha: sametyādi. samadarśanāt samatājñānaṃ saṃbhaved ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.5] bhagavann ityādi, samatādṛṣṭiṃ tv iti: samatājñānaṃ. kīdṛśam iti: katham utpadyata ity arthaḥ. advayarūpāś ca dharmāḥ katham ātmarūpadevatārūpeṇa sākṣātkartavyā iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.6] praśnaṃ visraṣṭum āha: śṛṇv ityādi. yathātattvam aviparītam. advayojñānalakṣaṇam iti: advayajñānaṃ yathotpadyate, tac chṛṇv iti bhāvaḥ. varjitam ityādi subodham. tribhir ityādi: tribhiḥ svabhāvādibhiḥ śuddhā ityādimantram uccārayed ity arthaḥ. kadety āha: sarvayogādi agrata iti.
sarvayogānām ādau vakṣyamāṇaṃ mantratrayam uccārayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.7] tān evāha: svabhāvetyādi. svabhāvena sadasannityānityāstināstiheyopādeyagrāhyagrāhakādilakṣaṇena śuddhāḥ śūnyā riktāḥ. ke ta ity āha: sarvadharmā iti. utpādasthitibhaṅgānāṃ svalakṣaṇānāṃ dhāraṇād dharmāḥ skandhādayaḥ. te ca svabhāvena prakṛtyā śuddhāḥ. aham api tathety āha: svabhāvaśuddho 'ham iti.
prakṛtipariśuddho 'py āgantukamalāpagamāya śodhyata iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.8] vajretyādi: vajram advayajñānam, abhedyatvāt. tadvac chuddhāḥ.
[2.3.9] yogo devatālambanaṃ, tena śuddhāḥ, parityaktaprākṛtarūpāḥ. svahṛdi svabījaṃ vīkṣya tannirgatena raśmiprasareṇa gurusveṣṭadevatāvabhāsanaṃ kṛtvā puṣpādimudrābhiḥ puṣpādidānābhinayaṃ ca kṛtvā pāpadeśanāpuṇyānumodanāpuṇyapariṇāmanātriśaraṇagamanātmaniryātanāpraṇidhānamārgāśrayamaitryādibhāvanāṃ vidhāya paścāt triviśuddhim uccārayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.10] ebhi m-uccārya m-añjalyam anuttarasyaiva kārayed iti: anuttarajñānārthaṃ hṛdy añjaliṃ nidhāya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā triviśuddhim uccārayed iti bhāvaḥ. sthānasampattim āha: manonukūletyādi. yaḥ kaścit pradeśo yadi mano'nukūlo bhavati, tatra dhyānam ārabhetety arthaḥ. atha vā parvatādau.
[2.3.11] patāketi: cihnarahitā. matsyapakṣyādicihnāṅko dhvajaḥ.
[2.3.12] samantād daśabhiḥ sthānair gandhamaṇḍalādibhis tatheti: tādṛśe pradeśe samantād daśa diśo vyāpya gandhena maṇḍalam ekam eva dhyānādikāle kuryād ity arthaḥ. ūrdhvam api gandhacchaṭākṣepān madhyalepāc ca daśadigvyāptiḥ. pūjādyanantaraṃ jñānaḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ dhyātvā vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryād ity āha: jhaṭitetyādi.
jhaṭitety ekākinī jñānaḍākinī bhāvyety arthaḥ.
[2.3.13] pūjā kurvantam ātmana iti: pūjādikam ātmanā kṛtvety arthaḥ. kartavyam āha: hastam uttānakam ityādi. uttānadakṣiṇahaste jvaladākāraṃ vajraṃ pañcaśūkaṃ dhyātvā ...
[2.3.14] madhye karāsphālaṇaṃ kuryāt tataḥ pūrvasyāṃ diśi tato vāmāvartenottarādiṣv itthaṃ rasātalaparyantaṃ vajramayaṃ dhyātvā oṃ hūṃ phaḍ iti mantram uccārayan prāguktanyāyena prākārādikaṃ dhyāyāt. ādau paścimābhimukhenaiva dhyānam ārabdhavyam ity upadeśaḥ. enam eva mantram uccarati: hūṃkāreṇetyādi. ghāta antimeti phaṭkāro ghātaḥ.
tata oṃ hūṃ phaḍ iti mantrapariṇataṃ catūratnamayasumerūpari sthitaṃ kāñcanamayam āgāraṃ saptaratnakhacitaṃ svecchāvistaraṃ dhyātvā tatra bhagavatīṃ dhyāyād iti śeṣaḥ.
[2.3.15] etad evāha: merumūrdhaṃ tv ityādi.
[2.3.16] mudrām āha: baddhvetyādi. sarvamudrāṇāṃ madhye prathamaṃ samayamudrāṃ baddhvā darśayed iti śeṣaḥ. kāñcanāgāre bhagavatīṃ dhyātvā tasyai mudrāṃ darśayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.17] kathaṃ badhnīyān mudrām ity āha: vajramuṣṭītyādi. vāmamuṣṭiṃ hṛdi nidhāya dakṣiṇabāhum utkṣipya samayamudrāṃ darśayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.18] asyā mantram āha: yathetyādi. yatheti yasyā ity arthaḥ. vajramayaṃ bījaṃ hūṃkāraḥ. huta antimeti: svāhākāro 'nte kārya ity arthaḥ. oṃ hūṃ svāheti samudāyaḥ. asyetyādi: asyāḥ samayamudrāyā mantro darśita ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.19] idānīm āsanam āha: siṃha-m-āsanetyādi. pañcasu sthāneṣu siṃhāsanāni pañca, teṣu ca pañca sūryamaṇḍalāni bhāvayet.
[2.3.20] madhyāsane jñānaḍākinī, śeṣeṣu vajraḍākinyādayaḥ. aparāṇy apy āsanāni vakṣyati. āsanabhāvanāpūrvakaṃ devatā bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. ātmaśvāsena niḥsṛtyeti: samayasattvīṃ bhāvayitvā jñānasattvīm ātmanaḥ śarīrān niḥsārya samayasattvyāṃ praveśayed iti bhāvaḥ. jñānaḍākini madhyata iti: madhyasiṃhāsane jñānaḍākinī bhāvyeti bhāvaḥ.
tasyā rūpam āha: trimukham ityādi.
[2.3.21] keśa vicchārya pañcabuddhābhi maṇḍitam iti: keśān vitatīkṛtya pañcabhir buddhair maṇḍitā, spharadbhir ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.22] hasitakrodhaśṛṅgāreti: śṛṅgāri mūlamukhaṃ, hasitaṃ vāmaṃ, kruddhaṃ dakṣiṇam.
[2.3.23] khaṭvāṅga ūrdhvaśaś caiveti: ūrdhvakhaṭvāṅgaṃ bhāvayet.
[2.3.24] madhyamaṃ budha iti: etādṛśīṃ bhagavatīṃ jñānaḍākinīm ākāśaviśuddhyā madhyāsane dhyāyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.25–34] ātma āpetīti: ātmaśabdaḥ svabhāvavācī. āpetīti jalam. jalasvabhāvety arthaḥ. evam agnyādisvabhāvā ghoraḍākinyādayo vāmāvartenottarādiṣu draṣṭavyāḥ.
[2.3.35] ātmapañcetīti: etāḥ pañcabhūtasvabhāvāḥ pañca ḍākinya iti. upasaṃhāra iti bhāvaḥ. pañcapañceti: pañcajñānaviśuddhā etāḥ pañceti bhāvaḥ. pūrvokteṣu siṃhāsaneṣu bhāvyāḥ. atha vā ekaikayoginyaḥ pañcajñānaviśuddhāḥ.
[2.3.36] iśānyasya tv ityādi: aiśānyāṃ siṃhī siṃhamukhadhāriṇī sitapītobhayabhūmivarṇā. nāgendro hastī sa evāsanam.
[2.3.37] vajrāṅkuśa tarjanyeti: dakṣiṇe bhuje vajralāñchito 'ṅkuśaḥ. vāme tarjanī.
[2.3.38] āgneyyāṃ saptaratnaghaṭitāsanasthā sitanīlobhayabhūmivarṇā vyāghrī.
[2.3.39] aṅkuśavirajapāṇim iti: aṅkuśavirājito dakṣiṇaḥ pāṇir ity arthaḥ. tarjani pāśavad iti: pāśayuktā tarjanī vāma ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.40] raktakṛṣṇasya varṇiketi: raktakṛṣṇobhayabhūmivarṇā.
[2.3.41] aṅkuśapāśatarjanyeti: aṅkuśo dakṣiṇe, pāśāṅkitā tarjanī vāme.
[2.3.42] vāyavyāṃ lūkikā devīti: ulūkī pītaraktobhayabhūmivarṇā ...
[2.3.43] ... sarpāsīnā sarpābharaṇā ca. etad evāha: nāgam āsanam ākramya sarpābharaṇabhūṣitam iti. aṅkuśa pāśatarjanīti pūrvavat. siṃhyādaya etāḥ svanāmaprasiddhānanāḥ.
[2.3.44] aṣṭa ḍākini madhyaṃ tv iti: aṣṭau yoginya etā madhyapuṭe sthitā ity arthaḥ. bāhyānāṃ tu caturthaketi: bāhyapuṭe catasro ḍākinyo vaktavyā iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.45] tā evāha: pūrvetyādi. pūrvadvāre raudrīnāmnīti saṃbandhaḥ. rājendreti: vajrapāṇisaṃbodhanam.
[2.3.46] mukhe prakṣipya hastānām iti: hastadvayam añjalirūpaṃ mukhe kṣiptvā sthitety arthaḥ. aṭṭahāsīti: mukhe prakṣiptāñjalir aṭṭahāsaṃ kurvatī tiṣṭhatīty arthaḥ.
[2.3.47] uttare dīpinīti: uttaradvāre dīpinī. añjalya dvayahastaṃ tu śira ūrdhvaṃ tu dīpavad iti: añjaliṃ kṛtvā śirasa upari dīpākāreṇa darśayantī sthiteti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.48–50] raktasūtrasya hastānāṃ añjalyā rudhiraṃ pibed iti. sūtrasyeti: sūtrākāreṇa dhārayed ity arthaḥ. raktāñjaliṃ gṛhītvotthāpya dhārayā pibed ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.51–52] tarjanīmuṣalahastānām iti: muṣalaṃ dakṣiṇe, tarjanī vāme. mohayantīti: vaśīkurvatīty arthaḥ.
[2.3.53] sarva sarvavid iti: itthaṃ sarvaṃ maṇḍalaṃ yogī bhāvayed ity arthaḥ. svasvāsanopari paryaṅkākāreṇa vāmapādākrāntaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kiṃcit prasārayed iti sattvaparyaṅka iti sattvaparyaṅkasthā yoginyaḥ. siṃhāsanavat pūrvam eva nāgendrāsanādīni pretāsanāni ca sūryākrāntāni bhāvyāni. teṣu svasvabījapariṇatā yoginyaḥ.
ebhi bimbādi agrasya samayaṃ cāpi darśayed iti: āsāṃ yoginīnām agrataḥ samayamudrāṃ svāṃ svāṃ darśayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.54] mantramudrayor mantraṃ tāvad āha: aniletyādi. saptiśabdena saptamaḥ sa evārthaḥ. anilasya saptamaḥ sakāraḥ, analasya ca hakāraḥ. sa eva vajrībījaṃ, bījaikadeśatvāt. tasya cāmṛtasvarūpatvād ardhacandreṇa sādharmyam. tataś ca vajrībījaśabdenārdhacandraḥ. tena bhedayed analasya saptamaṃ hasya śirasi taṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ. etenaitad uktam:
anilānalasaptamayor madhye anilasaptamo vajrībījam. tenānalasaptamaṃ bhedayed iti. bindunādasamākrāntam iti: hakārasyārdhacandropari binduḥ. nāda ūkāro 'dhastāt. tena hūṃ iti mantraḥ syāt. dhārāvarṣa iti: amṛtadhārāṃ varṣatīti dhārāvarṣa ity andhitā saṃjñā hūṃkārasya.
[2.3.55] praṇametyādi subodham. oṃ hūṃ svāheti mantraḥ. ayaṃ ca jñānaḍākinyāḥ. anena mudrā darśayitavyā. hūṃkārabījamātrāt tūtpadyate jñānaḍākinī. evam uttaratrāpi praṇavādisvāhāntena bījena mudrā prayoktavyā. bījamātrebhyo yathāsvaṃ yoginya utpadyanta iti. svarapūrvādibījasyeti: svaraḥ pūrvo 'kāraḥ. sa cādi, bījānām iti bhāvaḥ.
jñānaḍākinyā bījaṃ vihāya, anyāsāṃ yāni bījāni teṣāṃ madhye prathama ity arthaḥ. praṇamā huta antimeti vyākhyātam. oṃkāra ādau, svāhākāro 'nte mantroddhāraparisamāpte.
[2.3.56] vajrīnāmaṃ tu pūrvasyeti: vajraḍākinyāḥ pūrvadiksthitāyā akāro bījam iti bhāvaḥ. oṃ a svāheti samudāyaḥ. svaravarge dvitīyasyeti: svaravarṇasya dvitīya ākāraḥ.
[2.3.57] uttarasyāṃ diśi ghorībījam idam ākāraḥ. oṃ ā svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ. asyetyādi: asya ākārasya prayuktasya bījasya pārśve daṇḍo 'para ākāra ity arthaḥ. tena ā3 iti bījam.
[2.3.58] paścimasyāṃ vettālyā bījam idam. oṃ ā3 svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ. asyetyādi: ābījasyopari bindur deyaḥ. tena āṃ iti bījam. oṃ āṃ svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.59] napuṃsā pūrva bījasyeti: napuṃsakākṣarāṇi catvāri ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ iti, eṣāṃ pūrvaṃ prathamam akṣaraṃ ṛbījam. oṃ ṛ svāhetisamudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.60] napuṃsaketyādi: napuṃsakānāṃ dvitīyaṃ bījaṃ ṝ. oṃ ṝ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.61] napuṃsaketyādi: napuṃsakānāṃ tṛtīyaṃ bījaṃ ḷ. oṃ ḷ svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.62] caturthe napuṃsābījasyeti: napuṃsakānāṃ caturthaṃ bījaṃ ḹ. oṃ ḹ svāhetisamudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.63] lūki hūṃkāreti: ulūkī hūṃkārapradhānadhvaniḥ. tasyā bījam idaṃ mantraś cāyam ity arthaḥ. pañcamasyetyādi: pañcamasya vargasya dvitīyam akṣaraṃ phaḥ.
[2.3.64] mūlanādaṃ tu bījānām iti: mūlaṃ binduḥ. nāda ukāraḥ. tadyuktaḥ phakāraḥ. phuṃ iti bījaṃ ḍākinyā bījam. oṃ phuṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.65] pañcamasya tu vargasyeti: dvitīyam akṣaram iti śeṣaḥ. ekāre bindu yojitam iti: pheṃ iti bījam. oṃ pheṃ svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.66] pañcamasya tu bījasyeti: pañcamavargasya dvitīyam akṣaram ity anuvartate. phoṃ iti bījam. oṃ phoṃ svāheti samudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.67] repha-aṅkuśabindukam iti: phriṃ iti bījam. oṃ phriṃ svāhetisamudāyo mantraḥ.
[2.3.68] idānīṃ mudrām āha: ubhāv ityādi. vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā madhyamāṅgulidvayaṃ prasārya tadaṅgulidvayam agrāgrābhyāṃ cumbayitvā mudrā kāryā. tāṃ ca jñānaḍākinyai darśayet. oṃ hūṃ svāhety asyā mantraḥ.
[2.3.69] hastāv ityādi: vajrāñjaliṃ kṛtvā vṛddhāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ madhyamādvayaṃ muhuḥ spṛśaṃś cālayed iti vajraḍākinyā mudrā. oṃ a svāhety asyā mantraḥ.
[2.3.70] samāv ityādi: muṣṭidvayaṃ samaṃ baddhvā vṛddhāṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ prasārayed iti ghorīmudrā. oṃ ā svāhety asyā mantraḥ.
[2.3.71] atretyādi: enām eva mudrāṃ baddhvā kanīyasīdvayaṃ prasārayed iti vettālyā mudrā. oṃ ā3 svāhety asyā mantraḥ.
[2.3.72] atretyādi: enām eva mudrāṃ baddhvā madhyamāṅgulidvayaṃ prasārayed iti caṇḍālyā mudrā. oṃ āṃ svāhety asyā mantraḥ. dakṣiṇamudrā vyākhyāteti: dakṣiṇe caṇḍālyā mudrety arthaḥ. etan mudrācatuṣṭayam iti: catasṝṇāṃ yoginīnāṃ mudrācatuṣṭayam iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.73] vajretyādi: vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā tarjanīdvayaṃ prasāryānyonyasyāgreṇāgraṃ cumbayed iti koṇasthānāṃ siṃhyādīnāṃ catasṝṇām ekaiva mudrā, mantras tu bhinnaḥ. oṃ ṛ svāheti mantreṇa siṃhyāṃ darśayen mudrām, oṃ ṝ svāheti vyāghryām, oṃ ḷ svāheti jambukyām, oṃ ḹ svāheti ulūkyām.
[2.3.74] dvau hastāv ityādi: hastadvayaṃ prasārya mukhe prakṣipya oṃ phuṃ svāheti mantram uccārya paścāt phaḥkāraṃ krama krameṇoccārayan mudrāṃ tyajed iti pūrvadvārapālikāyā mudrā.
[2.3.75] mūrdhnītyādi: mūrdhni sampuṭāñjaliṃ kṛtvā dhārayed iti dīpinīmudrā. oṃ pheṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.76] praṇāmetyādi: yathā praṇāmārtham añjaliḥ kriyate tathā kartavyaḥ. sa ca mukhe sthāpayitavyo hūṃkāroccāraṇapūrvakam iti cūṣiṇīmudrā. asyā oṃ phoṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.77] āvartetyādi: hastadvayena kamalāvartanaṃ kṛtvā punar añjaliṃ vidhāya bhagasthāne sthāpayed iti kāmbojikāmudrā. asyā mantraḥ oṃ phriṃ svāheti.
[2.3.78] idānīṃ gandhādīnāṃ mantrān mudrāś ca darśayann āha: praṇametyādi. dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ prasārya gandhamrakṣaṇābhinayena dhārayed iti gandhamudrā. oṃ hūṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.79] amṛtetyādi: amṛtā sakāraḥ. rephabindūnām iti: sakārasyādho repha upari binduś ca, tena sraṃ iti syāt. oṃ sraṃ svāheti mantrasamudāyaḥ. gandhamudrāyā evāṅguṣṭhatarjanīyogāt puṣpamudrā.
[2.3.80] vargapūrvakapūrvasyeti: vargapūrvakaḥ kavargaḥ. tasya pūrvakaḥ kakāraḥ. asyādha ukāra upari binduḥ, nādabinduṃ tv iti (83b) vakṣyamāṇavacanāt. tena kuṃ iti syāt. oṃ kuṃ svāheti mantrasamudāyaḥ.
[2.3.81] puṣpamudrāyā eva madhyamāṅgulisamutthānād dhūpamudrā.
[2.3.82] navāntabījeti: navamaṃ ca tad antabījaṃ ceti navāntabījaṃ kṣakāraḥ. yakāram ārabhya gaṇyamānatvena navamatvam. asyāpy adha ukāra upari binduḥ. tena kṣuṃ iti syāt. dakṣiṇahastamuṣṭiṃ kṛtvāṅguṣṭhotthāpanād dīpamudrā. oṃ kṣuṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.83] pūrvavargatrikaṃ gṛhyeti: pūrvavargaḥ kavargas tattrikas tṛtīyo gakāraḥ. nāda upadeśād dhrasva ukāraḥ. bindur anusvāraḥ. gakārasyādha upari ca ukāro binduś ca. tena guṃ iti syāt.
[2.3.84] dvāv ityādi: hastadvayaṃ prasāryāṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ madhyamādvayaṃ ca cālayed iti balimudrā. oṃ guṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.3.85] mantramudrāyogena yathāyogaṃ gandhādipūjāṃ kuryād iti. pūjayed ebhi tattveneti: anena balitattvena pūjayet. sarvadevatā iti śeṣaḥ. balim āha: adaityābali pūrvavad iti. yatra pañcāmṛtāni pañcāṅkuśā āmamatsyamāṃsapalāṇḍvādikaṃ ca so 'daityābaliḥ. pūrvoktamantramudrābhyāṃ tair vastubhiḥ puṣpadhūpādibhiś ca devatāyogī baliṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ.
etad evāha: pañcetyādi. pūrvāṇīti: pañcāmṛtatvena prasiddhāni. gokudahanāḥ pañcāṅkuśābhidhānāḥ. abhimatāṃ siddhiṃ kuṣṇantīty aṅkuśāḥ.
[2.3.86] śeṣaṃ subodham. mudrāmantraṃ tv iti: pūrvoktau balimudrāmantrau. balyādikāle viṃśatividhāṃ pūjām āha: paścād ityādi.
[2.3.87] hūṃkāragītikākāram iti: hūṃkāroccāraṇasamaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. pūrvayogiṇitoṣiketi: etat pradhānaṃ yoginītuṣṭikaram iti bhāvaḥ. vajramuṣṭidvayam ityādinā viṃśatividhāṃ pūjāṃ darśayati. vajraghaṇṭādhāraṇābhinayena vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ kaṭidvaye nidhāya hūṃkāroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ kaṭyaṅgaśiraś cālayet.
[2.3.88] etad evāha: hūṃkāreṇetyādi.
[2.3.89–91] phomākāreṇeti: kāhalābhinayena.
[2.3.92] vādyākāreṇeti: kaṃsatālādyākāreṇa.
[2.3.93–96] dhvajākāreṇeti: vāmahastam ūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā tadupari dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ prasārayet.
[2.3.97–98] patākākāreṇeti: dakṣiṇabāhumātraṃ prasārayet.
[2.3.99] chatrākāreṇeti: hastadvayena cchatradhāraṇābhinayaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.100] mauñjyākāreṇeti: ūrdhvamardalavādanābhinayena.
[2.3.101–106] mardalākāreṇeti: tiryaṅmardalavādanābhinayaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.107] pūjayet sarvavastūni aṣṭa yogini tattvata iti: ebhir vajravajraghaṇṭādyabhinayavastubhis tattvataḥ prādhānyād aṣṭa yoginīḥ pūjayet. tad eva gītikākāraṃ hūṃkāram ātmanirmitam iti: hūṃkārapradhānaṃ gītaṃ, yat
paṇamaha akāraṇa rūvu secchavi sattuha uttāru |
tennā hūṃ tennā hūṃ tennā te te hūṃ (4.4.93)
ityādi. tad eveti prasiddham. ātmanirmitam ity ātmanā gātavyam ity arthaḥ. etena gītapūjā darśitā.
[2.3.108] stutipūjāṃ darśayann āha: vajja hu ityādi. puṃdevatāyāḥ strīdevatāyāś ca sādhāraṇī stutiḥ. vajja hu iti: he vajra, abhedyadharmakāyātmakatvāt.
surataṃ sattvārthakriyā, tatra samarthāni māṃsadivyaprajñādharmabuddhacakṣurabhidhānāni pañca locanāni, tair darśanaṃ jagad arthopalambho yasya, sa suratasamarthalocanadarśanaḥ, tasya saṃbodhanaṃ suraasamatthaloaṇadaṃsaṇae.
na kṣaratīty akṣaram advayajñānaṃ, tasya bhāvaḥ svabhāvo niḥsvabhāvatādikaṃ, sa svabhāvo yasya, so 'kṣarabhāvasvabhāvas, tasya saṃbodhanam akkharabhāvasahāvu. svabhāvaśūnye jagati svacittaṃ vilāsayati krīḍayatīti śūnyavilāsanas, tasya saṃbodhanaṃ suṇṇuvilāsaṇae.
[2.3.109] dharmāṇāṃ bhāvābhāvābhyāṃ vimuktā majjā tattvaṃ, tena viśeṣeṇa jñāta iti bhāvābhāvavimuktamajjāvijñātas tasya saṃbodhanaṃ bhāvābhāvavimuttiamajjavijāṇiae. bhañjia akkharu savva iti: sarvāṇi vācakāni śabdarūpāṇi bhaṅktvā tyaktvety arthaḥ. tataḥ kiṃ kṛtam ity āha: sesuvicintiae iti:
śeṣeṇāvācyena rūpeṇa vicintito yas tasya saṃbodhanaṃ sesuvicintiae iti.
[2.3.110] dvandvāliṅganaṃ prajñopāyasamāpattiḥ, tena yogaḥ samādhis, tena salilaṃ bodhicittaṃ tad eva kāryaṃ, tena vyavaharatīti dvandvāliṅganayogasalilakāryakas, tasya saṃbodhanaṃ dondāliṅgaṇajouśalilākajjiae iti. dharmam advayaṃ nairātmyaṃ suṣṭhu prāpya mokṣaṃ viśeṣeṇa prāpto yas tasya saṃbodhanaṃ mokkhavipāviae iti.
[2.3.111] sukhākārasaṃsāravimohitānāṃ sattvānām arthe mokṣeṇa mokṣāsaṅgena vivarjitas tasya saṃbodhanaṃ suhasaṃsāravimohiamokkhavivajjiae iti.
prajñayā na bhave sthānaṃ kṛpayā na śame sthitir
iti vacanāt. dondāliṅgaṇetyādinā svaparārthopāyasampad uktā. dhammu adaia ityādinā svārthasampat. suhasaṃsāretyādinā ca parārthasampat. evaṃviśiṣṭaṃ bhagavantaṃ saṃbodhya yaḥ kaścit stuvann āha: savvetyādi. sarvasvabhāvena sarvaprakāreṇa kāyavākcittair ity arthaḥ, yoginyo mattās tvayā nartitāś ceti savvasahāveṃ joiṇi mattaviṇacciae ity asyārthaḥ.
[2.3.112] pañca vītyādi: kṣayānutpādajñānaṃ buddhānāṃ tattvaṃ, pañcāpi yoginyo buddhatattvam iti viśeṣeṇa lakṣitās tvayeti draṣṭavyam iti pañca vi yoiṇi buddhatattvavilakkhiae ity asyārthaḥ. anena dharmakāyaś ca bhagavataḥ sūcitaḥ. māyayā bahuvidharūpayā sattvāḥ pācitās tvayeti śeṣaḥ. itaś ca nirmāṇakāyo darśitaḥ.
[2.3.112] vajreṇa śūnyatayā, suṣṭhu sāritam adhigataṃ, kāryaṃ nirvikalpatālakṣaṇaṃ yena, sa vajrasusāritakāryas tasya saṃbodhanaṃ vajjasusāriakajjeti. saṃbodhya kim uktam ity āha: dhammetyādi.
jīvitamaraṇasukhaduḥkhādilakṣaṇadhāraṇād dharmāḥ sattvā viśiṣṭena krameṇa dharmadeśanārūpeṇa kliṣṭamohān nivartitās tvayeti śeṣa iti dhammavimohiae ity asyārthaḥ. saṃbhogakāyaś ca darśitaḥ. idānīṃ sattvān saṃbodhyāha: ṇamatetyādi. he sattvās! tam evaṃbhūtaṃ bhagavantaṃ namata! sāradharmaś ca yo bhagavāṃs taṃ namateti pūrveṇaiva saṃbandhaḥ.
sārudhammu jjo iti vyākhyātam. yoginyaś ca yena mocitās taṃ namateti yojyam. joiṇimokkhiae iti sunītam.
[2.3.114] saṃskṛtastavam āha: namo 'stv ityādi. prajñopāyasamāpattir yogaḥ, sa evādhipaḥ śreṣṭhatvāt. tena sattvān mocayatīti yogādhipasattvamocakaḥ. yad vā: he yogādhipeti bhinnaṃ padam. evaṃviśiṣṭa bhagavaṃs tubhyaṃ nama iti bhāvaḥ. ātmeti: dharmadhātuḥ. tasmāj jātā ity ātmajāḥ. sarva ātmajā yasya, sa sarvātmajaḥ. ekabhāvata ity ekacetasā.
[2.3.115] saṃsāra evārṇavo bālair duravagāhatvāt. moho jñeyāvaraṇam. saṃsārārṇavasthitānāṃ mohacchedaka, namo 'stu. ta iti śeṣaḥ. tattvenaikaḥ śreṣṭhatvāt tattvajñas tvatsadṛśo nāparo 'stīti tattvaiketi saṃbodhanam.
[2.3.116] itiviśeṣateti: viśeṣata ity arthaḥ. stavagītastotraśabdena stutigītam. tenopahāraṃ pūjāṃ kuryāt. tattvākhyānāt sadguṇākhyātiḥ stutiḥ. stutirūpaṃ gītaṃ stutigītam. paṇamahetyādigīte (4.4.93) tattvasyālpatvād gītamātraṃ, tataś cānayor bhedaḥ.
[2.3.117] stutyanantaraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ vādayet. etad evāha: hūṃkāretyādi. hūṃkārāntaritayā ceti. hūṃkāroccāraṇaviśeṣitāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ vādayet. hūṃkāroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ ghaṇṭāvādanam ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.118] atheti: ghaṇṭānādānantaram. akārṣīd iti: kuryād ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.119] trividhaṃ praṇidhānam āha: vajrāñjalītyādi. vajrāñjaliṃ hṛdi dhārayitvā ...
[2.3.120–122] ... oṃ svabhāvaśuddhā ityādi mantratrayaṃ paṭhet.
[2.3.123] kamalāvartaṃ tu m-āvartya dakṣiṇe padma cintitam | vāme vajram utsṛjya mahāmudrāṃ tu darśayed iti: praṇidhānāntaraṃ kamalāvartaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇahaste padmabhājanaṃ cintayitvā vāmahastasya vṛddhāṅguṣṭhena kanīyasīm ākramya vāmahastaṃ padmabhāṇḍe niyojayed iti mahāmudrāṃ darśayet.
[2.3.124] asyā mantraḥ: oṃ ha ho hrī svāheti.
[2.3.125] ata āha: mahāmudrāmantra iti.
[2.3.126] madanāsvādane 'py anyatra mudrāmantrāv etāv eva. ata evāha: samaṃ tāpi ca yogīnāṃ sarvadevābhi cintayed iti. samam: ekakālam. tāpi ceti: taṃ madanaśodhanam. sarvadevābhīti: sarvadevatāyogaś ca jhaṭiti yoginā kārya iti bhāvaḥ. laukikādīn tu jñānīnāṃ bāhyamaṇḍala kalpitam iti:
laukikāḥ śreṣṭhāś ca jñāninaḥ kṣetrapālāḥ, tebhyo balidānārthaṃ bāhyamaṇḍalaṃ kalpitam. ātmano bahirbhūtatvād bāhyam. tatra raktacandanena kuṅkumena vā trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā, tanmadhye vartulaṃ, tanmadhye ca catuḥsūtrayogena navakoṣṭhakaṃ maṇḍalam.
tatra pūrvādikoṣṭhakeṣu yathākramaṃ kṛṣṇarudramahārudradevadattakṛṣṇakarālabībhatsanandātītavināyakā ete kṣetrapālāś, cāmuṇḍīghorīprabhṛtibhir dvātriṃśadyoginībhiḥ pratyekaṃ catasṛbhiś catasṛbhiḥ parivṛtāḥ svāyudhodyatapāṇayaḥ. madhyakoṣṭhake ca trayodaśātmikā jñānaḍākinī. iti bāhyamaṇḍalaṃ bhāvyam.
[2.3.127] pṛthag gandhena m-āliptam ityādi: pṛthag ity anyatrāparibhuktena gandhādinā. pṛthag yogaṃ tu dhyāpayed iti: balidānakāle jñānaḍākinīmaṇḍalātmako bhaved iti bhāvaḥ. pṛthaktvaṃ bāhyamaṇḍalamadhyasthajñānaḍākinīmaṇḍalāt.
[2.3.128] pañcāmṛtasamāśritam iti: pañcāmṛtaṃ śuktyādibhājanagataṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ. ata āha: śuktādiṃ tu dhyāpayed iti: śuktaśabdena śuktiḥ. hakāram ityādi: hakāraṃ pañcāmṛte dhyāyāt, sa ca tasya prākṛtaṃ varṇaṃ harati. hokāraś ca dhyātas tasya prākṛtaṃ gandhaṃ nāśayati.
[2.3.129] hrīkāraś ca dhyāto 'tra prākṛtaṃ vīryaṃ hanti. ebhis tribhir akṣarair varṇādinā trividhais tribhir uccārya trīn lokeśvarādīn yathākramaṃ dhyāyād iti bhāvaḥ. hakāreṇa lokeśvaraṃ, hokāreṇa vairocanaṃ, hrīkāreṇākṣobhyaṃ pañcāmṛte dhyāyād ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.130] tataḥ pariśuddhaṃ varṇādikaṃ syād ity āha: padmeśvaram ityādi. padmeśvaro 'mitābhaḥ, sa eva lokeśvaraḥ. sa suvarṇavarṇāṃ rūpakāntiṃ karoti. pañcāmṛtasyeti śeṣaḥ. suvarṇakāntiś ca lokeśvaro dhyeyaḥ. gandhodakaṃ bhāveti: gandhodakam iva sugandhaṃ bhāvaṃ prakṛtiṃ niṣpādayati. ko 'sāv ity āha: surocanaṃ ceti. vairocana ity arthaḥ.
śuklaś cāyam. āsvāda vajreśvara vajrapāṇīti: āsvādaṃ rasaṃ viśuddhaṃ niṣpādayati, vajreśvarety akṣobhyaḥ. sa ca vajrapāṇir vajrahastaḥ. kṛṣṇaś cāyam. prākṛtaṃ varṇādikaṃ hatvā viśuddham anyad eva niṣpādayanti vāgādayaḥ. etad evopasaṃharann āha: trayābhi devā trividhena jñāpayed iti. tisro devatās trividhena varṇena pītādinā bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.131] idānīṃ yoginīpūjārthaṃ madanaśodhanaṃ darśayati: vāme candra pāṇiṃ tv ityādi. vāmapāṇitale candraṃ cintayitvā tanmadhye 'mṛtākṣaraṃ sakāram. sarvavinmātrā praṇavas tena yuktaṃ cintayet. tad yathā soṃ iti. aṅguṣṭhā kṣitim eva ceti: aṅguṣṭhaḥ kṣitisvarūpaḥ.
[2.3.132] anāmā sumero iti: anāmā sumerusvarūpā. amṛtā iti: amṛtaṃ madanam ity arthaḥ. balineti: prajñopāyasvarūpakṣitisumerusvarūpābhyām aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ balibhyāṃ mathanetīti mathnāti. balitvaṃ cāmṛtatvotpādanāya. evaṃ kṛte madanam amṛtatāṃ yātīti balam etad vastuśaktir eṣety arthaḥ. m-ambho trividhena jñāpayed iti:
tad ambho 'mṛtaṃ ha-ho-hrīty anena pūrvoktakrameṇa dhyāyād ity arthaḥ. vāmahastatale madanaṃ kṛtvā tadupari soṃkāraṃ vicintyāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ pramathya hakārādinā ca lokeśvarādirūpaṃ cintayitvā pratyekayoginīnāmagrahaṇena tābhyo jyeṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ pṛthak pṛthaṅ madanachiḍriṅgāṃ dadyāt.
[2.3.133] ata āha: pṛthag ityādi. chidriṅgā dravavikiraṇam. aṅguṣṭheti: jyeṣṭhānāmikābhyām. paścād ityādi: tadanv ātmabhāvena jñānaḍākinīsvabhāvenātmacittaṃ kuryāt.
[2.3.134] pañcasphoṭaṃ tu stūpasyeti: sphuṭanti naśyantīti sphoṭāḥ skandhāḥ, pañcabhiḥ sphoṭaiḥ stūpaṃ śarīram ity arthaḥ. tat snāpayet trividhena chidriṅgātrayeṇety arthaḥ. hastagatamadanachidriṅgābhir yoginīḥ pūjayitvā jñānaḍākinīyogavān ātmānam api chidriṅgātrayeṇa pūjayed iti bhāvaḥ. ātmani chidriṅgāsthānam āha: jihvāgra ityādi.
jihvāgre, bindv iti ūrṇāmadhye, ūrdhvānām iti mūrdhni. eṣu triṣu sthāneṣu chiḍriṅgām ekaikāṃ dattvātmānaṃ pūjayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.135] gaṇamaṇḍale madanadānagāthām āha: pecchia ityādi. dharmāḥ śodhitā dṛṣṭāḥ pecchia sohia dhammu ity asyārthaḥ. anabhiniviṣṭatvāt. etena kim ity āha: ṇahisaṃbhāvaṇu mellu iti. nāstikasaṃbhāvanām unmuñca, yataḥ santy eva sarvadharmāḥ kiṃ tu pariśuddhā iti bhāvaḥ. tataś ca brāhmaṇādibhedo 'yathārtha eva. etad evāha: bamhaṇu ityādi.
brāhmaṇakurkuracaṇḍālā ekasvabhāvena khāditāḥ. ekena svabhāvena gṛhītā ity arthaḥ. yato brāhmaṇādīnāṃ śukraśoṇitādikaṃ rāgadveṣādikaṃ cāviśiṣṭam eveti vāstavo bhedo nāsti. kiṃ tarhi? kālpanikaḥ. bamhaṇuśabdena brahmakṣatravaiśyāḥ. kukkuruśabdena sāmānyasiddhās tiryañcaḥ. caṇḍāluśabdenāntajāḥ sarve. śūdro 'nukto 'pi draṣṭavyaḥ.
so 'pi varṇatvena vyavasthito yataḥ. śuddham aśuddhaṃ vā na kiṃcid asti, tataś ca sarvaṃ vikalpam pibeti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.136] ebhir ityādi: anayā gāthayā paṭhitayā madanapātraṃ yogiyoginībhyāṃ deyam ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.137] kamalāvartaṃ tv ityādi (123a) pūrvam uddiṣṭaṃ nirdiśann āha: kamalāvartaṃ tu pūrvasyeti. cittasphaṭikacetaseti: sphaṭikavad vikalpamalarahitena cetasā. trisūcyetyādi subodham.
[2.3.138] dānānantaraṃ madanagrahaṇagāthām āha: svāgadu ityādi. svenātmanā, āgato 'vabuddhaḥ, svasaṃvedyatvāt. ko 'sāv ity āha: dhammu iti. dharmaḥ prakṛtiḥ skandhādīnāṃ padārthānām. sa punas tathatā. eṣā cādvayajñānam. tac ca tathāgataḥ. dharmaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭa ity āha: aṇagghu ityādi. na kvacid vidyate 'rghaḥ pūjā yasya, so 'narghaḥ.
tatpūjāyogyaṃ vastu triṣu lokeṣu nāstīty arthaḥ. bodha eva tasya satpūjeti bhāvaḥ. rāamalāgatae iti: rāgas tṛṣṇā. sa eva malaś, cittamalinīkaraṇatvāt. tasmād āgata īṣadgato bhraṣṭaḥ. viśuddharāgasadbhāvān na sarvathā bhraṣṭaḥ. nanu kathaṃ dveṣādikleśān hitvā rāgamātram uktam? satyam, sukhā vedanā saṃsārasya pradhānahetuḥ. tasyāś ca hetū rāgaḥ.
rāgaviśuddhyā saṃsāraḥ sāṅgaḥ samagro viśuddhaḥ syād iti. gāhetyādi: yato 'pagatarāgamalaḥ, tato grāho 'bhiniveśaḥ, agrāhas tadviparītaḥ, tābhyāṃ varjitaḥ saddharmaḥ. abhiniviṣṭo 'smy anabhiniviṣṭo 'smīty apy abhiniveśavarjita iti bhāvaḥ. evaṃviśiṣṭaṃ dharmaṃ taṃ namateti saṃbandhaḥ. kimartham ity āha: tattiae iti. tattvacintārtham.
tatpraṇāmena tattve mano vartatām iti hetuneti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.139] anayā gāthayā pratigrāhako gṛhṇīyād iti madanam. ata āha: asya gāthenetyādi. dānagrahaṇahetum āha: yogasiddhyarthahetuneti. yogo 'viparītabhāvaḥ, samasamayatvam ity arthaḥ. tasya niṣpattis tayā yo 'rthaḥ khādanapānādilakṣaṇaḥ, sa eva hetuḥ. gāthayā madanapātradānagrahaṇayoḥ. yathetyādi: yathā m-ācāreti:
yathālābhaṃ yogī samācaret tan madanam iti bhāvaḥ. sarvam etac ca yogināṃ śuci pavitram.
[2.3.140] yathāsukham iti vaktavyam iti: madanaṃ dattvā yathāsukham iti yoginā vaktavyam.
[2.3.141] bāhyamaṇḍala kalpitam ity (126d) uddiṣṭasya nirdeśam āha: raktamaṇḍaletyādi. kuṅkumena raktacandanena vā caturasraṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā tadbahis trikoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. bāhyadevatāvāhanamudrāmantraṃ cāha: vajramuṣṭītyādi. vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ kṛtvā tayoḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhena saṃyojya ...
[2.3.142] ... dakṣiṇakaniṣṭhayā vāmakaniṣṭhāṃ saṃveṣṭya tarjanīdvayam utthitaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇatarjanīm ākuñcya tatpṛṣṭhe vāmatarjanīṃ niveśya mudrāṃ kuryāt.
[2.3.143] asyā mantraḥ: oṃ ityādi.
[2.3.144] ata āha: āvāhanamudrāmantra iti.
[2.3.145] raktetyādi subodham. balidravyam āha: ohāretyādi. āmamāṃsādikam ohārabaliśabdenocyate. ādiśabdāt palāṇḍulasonādikam. āmamāṃsamatsyapalāṇḍuprabhṛtiprabhūtataraṃ vastu balibhājane dātavyam.
[2.3.146] etat sarvaṃ khādyam. etad evāha: khādyeti. sarvāṇi dravyāṇīti madanam. tac ca sarvaṃ yathālābhaṃ deyam. tad dhi bahuprakāram. tathā cāha:
dhānyavalkādisaṃdhānaṃ jñeyā lokaprasiddhitaḥ |
surā maireyam ikṣvādirasasaṃdhānam iṣyate ||
iti. pañcāmṛtasuyuñjitam iti: tatra pañcāmṛtaṃ deyam. tac ca suyuñjitaṃ nyastasvasvabījatvāt. vāmabhāgānītyādi: balibhāṇḍasya vāmabhāge vāmahastaṃ dattvā dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ samutthāpya vajramuṣṭiṃ baddhvā tarjanīṃ prasārya darśayet. etad evāha: dāyinaṃ krodha darśayed iti. krodhā tarjanī.
atha vā baliṃ bhāgadvayatvena parikalpya vāmabhāgaṃ maṇḍalacakrāya dakṣiṇaṃ ca dikpālebhyo nivedayed iti. tatra bhāṇḍaśabdena maṇḍalacakraṃ, krodhaśabdena dikpālāḥ.
[2.3.147] oṃ ekavṛkṣa ityādinā dhanus tathetyantena (152d) mantreṇa lokapālān āvāhayet. bhājana iti jalādhāre. mātaṅgāṃ tv iti caṇḍālagṛhe. ekavṛkṣādinivāsinaḥ kṛṣṇarudrādayaḥ pūrvoktakoṣṭhabhāviteṣu saparivāreṣv āvāhya praveśanīyāḥ.
[2.3.148] tatra pūrve kṛṣṇarudraḥ. uttare mahārudraḥ. paścime devadattaḥ. dakṣiṇe kṛṣṇaḥ. aiśānyāṃ karālaḥ. agneyyāṃ bībhatsaḥ. nairṛtyāṃ nandātītaḥ. vāyavyāṃ vināyakaḥ. ete trinetrāś caturbhujā yathoktāyudhā vastrābharaṇabhūṣitā yathāsaṃkhyaṃ kṛṣṇapītasitaraktavarṇāḥ pūrvādidiksthāḥ, kṛṣṇapītakṛṣṇaraktaraktasitasitapītā aiśānyādisthitāḥ.
[2.3.149] kṛṣṇarudrasyāgrataś cāmuṇḍī, vāmato ghorī, bībhatsā pṛṣṭhataḥ, umādevī dakṣiṇataḥ. etā yathākramaṃ ḍākinyādivarṇāḥ. mahārudrasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthā jayāvijayājitāparājitā dīpinyādivarṇāḥ.
[2.3.150] devadattasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthā bhadrakālīmahākālīsthūlakālīndryaś cūṣiṇyādivarṇāḥ. kṛṣṇasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthāś candrīghorīduṣṭīlambakyaḥ kāmbojyādivarṇāḥ. karālasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthās tridaśeśvarī-...
[2.3.151] ...-kāmbojīdīpinīcūṣiṇyaś cāmuṇḍyādivarṇāḥ. bībhatsasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthā ghorarūpāmahārūpādaṃṣṭrārūpākarālinyaś candryādivarṇāḥ.
[2.3.152] nandātītasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthāḥ kapālamālāmālinīkhaṭvāṅgāyīmaharddhikā bhadrakālyādivarṇāḥ. vināyakasya pūrvottarapaścimadakṣiṇasthāḥ khaḍgahastāparaśuhastāvajrahastādhanurhastā jayādivarṇāḥ. etāś cāmuṇḍyādimaharddhikāntā vilāsinyaḥ, puṣpādipañcopacārahastāḥ. khaḍgahastādyāś catasro yathārutacihnāḥ.
[2.3.153] pañca yogini mahātattve sarvakāmānusādhakā iti: dīpinyādyāḥ kathitāḥ. yogamaṇḍalamahārājñī vajreśvarīti: jñānaḍākinīmaṇḍalam. prabhus tatheti: dikpālāḥ.
[2.3.154] tathāgatamahākāyeti: ete tathāgatarūpā ity arthaḥ. katham ity āha: nirañjayogasṛṣṭiketi: añjanam añjaḥ kleśaḥ, nirgato 'ñjo yasmin sati, sa nirañjaḥ. yogaḥ samādhiḥ. sṛṣṭikāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ. nirañjena yogena sṛṣṭāḥ sādhitā iti nirañjayogasṛṣṭikāśabdārthaḥ. idaṃ vajreśvarī ājñena āvāha sarvasarvinām iti:
idam ity anayā, vajreśvarī maṇḍalanāyikā jñānaḍākinī tasyā ājñā. anayā jñānaḍākinyājñayā pūrvoktamantralakṣaṇayā sarvathā bahirdevatā āvāhya vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.155] tad evāha: oṃ ka kketyādi.
[2.3.156] amṛta mukham āpūryeti: amṛteti baliḥ. pūrvoktena mantreṇāvāhya sarvadevatā maṇḍale dṛṣṭvā, oṃ ka kketyādimantreṇa devatānāṃ mukhaṃ pūrayed iti bhāvaḥ. padakarmādi cintitam iti: etad yac cintitaṃ, tat padakarmaśabdena bhaṇyate. tac ca śreṣṭham. atha vā: amṛtena madanena svamukhaṃ pūrayitvā pīkākāreṇa tan madanaṃ balau nikṣiptavyam.
etad eva padakarmety upadeśaḥ. padakarmaśabdena nṛtyam iti kecit. paścān nṛtyaṃ vajrapadena kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. bhāvayed ityādi: itthaṃbhāvanayā yoginā yoginyā vā balir deya ity upasaṃhāraḥ. tena kiṃ syād ity āha: sarvakarmābhi sidhyatīti. śāntikādikarmety arthaḥ.
[2.3.157] idānīṃ balidānamudrām āha: vajramuṣṭītyādi subodham.
[2.3.158] oṃ bhakṣetyādir asyā mantraḥ. etau mudrāmantrau vistarapakṣe darśitau. pūrvaṃ saṃkṣiptau darśitau.
[2.3.159] yathākarma saṃyujyeti: yasya karmaṇaḥ saṃyogas tatraivānena vidhinā balir deyaḥ.
[2.3.160] śāntītyādi subodham. bali bāhyasya tattveneti: bāhyabalitattvam etad ity arthaḥ. anena tattvena bāhyadevatānāṃ balir deyaḥ. tena ca sarvakarmāṇi sidhyanti. ata āha: bali karmādi sādhayed iti.
[2.3.161] idānīṃ guhyapūjām āha: athetyādi. pūjā viśeṣaṃ tv iti: viśeṣaṃ viśiṣṭā pūjety arthaḥ. sā hi vairocanādibhiḥ kriyamāṇatvād viśiṣṭā. ittham api kathaṃ viśiṣṭety āha: kalpākalpavivarjitam iti. kalpyata iti kalpaḥ, śucitvādinā vidhiḥ. tadviparīto 'kalpo 'vidhiḥ. tābhyāṃ varjito yato yogī bhavati.
vairocanādyabhyāso hi prākṛtavikalpaparihārāya. pūjām āha: aṅkuśādibhīti. aṅkuśās te ca pañca. pañca tattvānīti: pañcabhir vairocanādibhis tattvabhūtair devatārūpatvāt. kārayet śodhayet.
[2.3.162] ke te 'ṅkuśā ity āha: mahāṅkuśetyādi. mahāṅkuśanāmānam iti: mahāṅkuśanāmā nādiḥ. jñānadevata iṣṭavān iti: jñānadevatā vairocanaḥ. mahāṅkuśo vairocana iṣṭa ity arthaḥ. vajrāṅkuśo gādiḥ, sa cākṣobhyaḥ. sthānāṅkuśe ratnavān iti: sthānāṅkuśa ādiśvaḥ, sa ca ratnasaṃbhavaḥ.
[2.3.163] amogha rājamahāṅkuśeti: rājamahāṅkuśo rājāṅkuśa ity arthaḥ. sa ceha hādiḥ, amoghasiddhirūpaḥ. amitābhaṃ cala-m-aṅkuśair iti: calāṅkuśo 'ntaśvaḥ, sa cāmitābhaḥ. ete cāṅkuśā vakṣyamāṇasvasvabījapariṇatavairocanādirūpeṇopayoktavyāḥ. etad evāha: pañca-m-aṅkuśa-m-ādīni sarvatattva prayojayed iti.
pañcasv aṅkuśeṣu sarvatattvaṃ yathāsvaṃ devatātattvaṃ bhāvayet.
[2.3.164] yadepsetyādi: yasmād īpsitaṃ tan mahāṅkuśādikaṃ tattvādhiṣṭhitaṃ sad bhavati. keṣām īpsitam ity āha: sarvadevasyeti. sarvayoginīnām ity arthaḥ. tatas tena sarvayoginīḥ prīṇayet, tarpayed ity arthaḥ. vinā bhedena samayam ācared ity āha: na tv ityādi. kāmaratāś caiveti: kāma icchā, tatra raktāḥ. yāvadicchaṃ yatheṣṭam ity arthaḥ.
tathā nācared
iti bhāvaḥ. abhedena hi samayācāraḥ śreyaskaraḥ. tathā cāha:
sarvaṃ samāsataḥ kāryaṃ yathā bhedo na jāyate |
bhedena samayānāṃ tu na siddhir na ca sādhakaḥ ||
iti. na tu bhogena m-āśritam iti: bhogaḥ saṃskṛto viṣayaḥ. bhujyate viśeṣeṇāsvādyata iti kṛtvā. bhogam āśrito bhogāśritaḥ, bhogāsaktaḥ san na samayam ācared iti bhāvaḥ. saṃskāre kriyamāṇe bhedo jāyata iti. kiṃ ca laukike 'nabhiniveśārthaṃ samayācāraḥ. samaye ced abhiniveśaḥ sa eva doṣaḥ.
[2.3.165] svacchā sarvasarveṇeti: rūpaśabdādiṣv apy anyeṣu sarveṣu dṛśyadraṣṭṛdarśanādyanabhiniveśena sarvathā pravartanam iti bhāvaḥ. evaṃ saty api viśiṣṭā tathāgatapūjā syād ity āha: pūjetyādi. tasyetyādi: tasya gaṇamaṇḍalasya madhye vīrā yoginaḥ pariśuddhaśīlā niravagrahacittā ālikāliṃ prajñopāyasamāpattiṃ kurvīrann iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.166] tena sarvā yoginyas tarpitāḥ syur ity āha: alikālītyādi. tathoddhṛtetyādi: tathā samāpattyā saṃbhūtaṃ padmamadhye 'mṛtaṃ suratadravaṃ sarvaṃ svayam āsvādayet, gaṇe ca ḍhaukayet. svayaṃ pariśodhya padmasthaṃ jihvayāsvādayet. padmabhāṇḍe niveśya tad anyebhyo dadyāt.
[2.3.167] ata āha: śeṣādītyādi. svayam upabhuktāc cheṣaṃ puṣpādikam amṛtaṃ prajñāṃ ca dadyāt. kebhya ity āha: bāhyamaṇḍala kalpayed iti. bāhyamaṇḍalaṃ gaṇamaṇḍalaṃ praviṣṭāḥ sāmayikā ye, tebhyaḥ kalpayen nivedayeta. paścād dhūṃkārapradhānaṃ gītaṃ gāyayet. tatredaṃ gītaṃ: paṇamaha akāraṇu rūvu ityādi (4.4.93). etad evāha: hūṃkāragītikākāram iti.
na kevalam idaṃ, kiṃ tarhi: maṅgaladhvanipūritam iti. maṅgalagītaṃ ca paścād gāyayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.3.168] maṅgalagītam āha: paṇamahetyādi. bhāvavivajjia iti: bhāva utpādas tena vivarjitaṃ taṃ praṇamateti saṃbandhaḥ. utpādaḥ paramārthato nāsti, saṃvṛtyāstīti bhāvaḥ. svārthadvāreṇa viśeṣaṇam uktvā parārthadvāreṇāha: ṇāhia ityādi. nāthitaṃ prārthitam. aṅganti karmavaśāt tāṃ tāṃ gatiṃ gacchantīty aṅgāḥ sattvāḥ.
aṅgyate 'nena sattvārtha ity aṅgam utpādaḥ. nāthitam aṅgānām arthe 'ṅgam aneneti ṇāhia aṅgam uaṅgu iti bhavati. yadi paramārthato nāsty evotpādaḥ, sattvārthe 'pi sa katham ity āha: dondāliṅgaṇetyādi. dvandvāliṅganayoga utpattinirodhau. sa eva māyā, na paramārtha ity arthaḥ. dvandvāliṅganayogamāyayā vibhūṣitaḥ.
vividhair nirmāṇaiḥ sthita ity arthaḥ. sattvārthāya tathābhūta ity arthaḥ. yo hy asyotpādo 'pi sa utpādakāryākaraṇād anutpāda eva, anabhiniveśenotpādāt.
[2.3.169] maṅgalagītikānantaraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ nādayet. etad evāha: vajraghaṇṭābhi nādasya svecchā maṅgalagītiketi. paścāt praṇidhānaṃ kuryād ity āha: vajrāñjalītyādi. vajrāñjaliṃ hṛdi nidhāya ...
[2.3.170] ... sarve sattvā ityādi ...
[2.3.171] ... yogaśāstraṃ kriyāmy aham ityantaṃ paṭhitvā praṇidhānaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ.
[2.3.172] devatāvisarjanam āha: vajramuṣṭidvayaṃ kṛtvā, vāmavajramuṣṭiṃ hṛdi nidhāya, dakṣiṇavajramuṣṭiṃ prasārya bhūmau samāropya, tata oṃ ātmani tiṣṭha hūṃ svāheti (173) mantraṃ paṭhitvā, navamamadhyakoṣṭhagatāṃ jñānaḍākinīṃ saparivārāṃ visṛjet.
[2.3.173] mantram āha: oṃ ātmani tiṣṭha hūṃ svāheti.
[2.3.174] āvāhitasaparivārajñānaḍākinīvisarjanānantaraṃ samayajñānaḍākinīṃ saparivārām ātmaśvāsenātmani praveśayed ity āha: paścād ityādi.
[2.3.175] lokapālādivisarjanam āha: oṃ sarvaduṣṭetyādi.
[2.3.176] bhujadvayenāliṅganāsaktena choṭikātrayaṃ mantrapāṭhasamakālaṃ kṛtvā bāhyalokapālān visṛjed iti.
[2.3.col.] ity uktakrameṇa devatāyogādibalyantaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇam. iti parapīṭhe catuṣpīṭhanibandhe tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ.
