[2.4.1] idānīṃ viśeṣeṇa pañcāmṛtādikaṃ pratipādayitum āha: bhagavann ityādi. pañcāmṛta viśeṣata iti: pūrvam aviśeṣeṇa pratītam, idānīm ākāraprakārabhedataḥ pañcāmṛtaṃ śrotum icchāmīti bhāvaḥ. aṅkuśā kena pañcasyeti: aṅkuśāḥ pañca pūrvoktā ye ke ta iti viśeṣataḥ śrotum icchāmīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ. keneti ke ta ity arthaḥ.
alikāli kathaṃ bhaved iti: prajñopāyayogaḥ kathaṃ bhaved iti.
[2.4.2] advayādvayadharmasyeti: advayā tathatā prajñāpāramitā. dharmaḥ svabhāvaḥ. advayāyā advayadharmo niḥsvabhāvatādilakṣaṇaḥ, tasya bhāvābhāvavikalpitaṃ kathaṃ bhaved iti (1d) pūrvavat praśnaḥ. bhāvābhāvābhyāṃ visadṛśī kalpanā bhāvābhāvavikalpitam. vākyamudrādītyādi: vākyamudrā vākchommakam. cittasyeti: cihnamudrā.
cihnasyeti pāṭhe cittamudrā cihnamudraiveti sa evārthaḥ. yogaśāstranayam iti: yogo bhaiṣajyādisiddhisādhanam. śiṣyante saddharme niyojyante 'nena sattvā iti śāstram. nīyate prāpyate saṃbodhir aneneti nayaḥ. yogaś cāsau śāstraṃ ceti yogaśāstram. yogaśāstraṃ ca tan nayaś ceti yogaśāstranayaḥ.
bhaiṣajyādisiddhyā sattvāḥ pratyayād buddhatvamārge pravartante. pravṛttā abhyāsāt saṃbodhiṃ labhante. pāramparyeṇa yogenaivānena bodhiḥ prāpyata iti so 'pi tasyā nayaḥ. atha vaitadarthaṃ japādikaṃ kurvatāṃ puṇyasaṃbhāras tato mārgapravṛttis tato 'bhyāsāt saṃbodhir iti pāramparyeṇa
yogaḥ saṃbodhinaya iti. eṣa kathaṃ bhaved iti praśnaḥ.
[2.4.3] praśnaṃ visṛjann āha: bhagavān āhetyādi.
[2.4.4] pūrve prathamatas tāvad vajrāmbu pratipādyata iti saṃbandhaḥ. amṛtavajrāmbv iti: amṛtasvarūpaṃ vajrāmbu bodhicittam. pañca-m-ātmeti: pañcātmakam agnyādipañcasvarūpam. prapadyatāṃ pratipādyata ity arthaḥ. pañcātmakatvam āha: agnītyādi. ūṣmāyate sparśeti: ūṣmasparśayogād agnir iti bhāvaḥ. vāyu dhūmāya dṛśyata iti:
dhūmavad bāṣpadarśanād vāyur ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.5] apa dravabhāgaṃ tv iti: nimne dravatīti dravaḥ snehaḥ. bhāgo 'ṃśaḥ. snehāṃśenāpaḥ. apaśabdo jalavācakaḥ. pṛthivyā rūpa dṛśyata iti: kakkhaṭatvarūpadarśanāt pṛthvīti jñāyate. jñānādhiṣṭhita vajreṇeti: etāni catvāri bhūtāni jñāyanta iti jñānāni. teṣām adhiṣṭhitaṃ sthitiḥ. tena caturbhūtāvasthānenākāśaṃ bodhicitte 'stīti gamyate. vajreṇeti:
vajram ākāśam, abhedyatvāt. yasmāt pañcātmakaṃ tataḥ pañca devasya dhyāpayed iti. amitābhāmoghasiddhyakṣobhyavairocanaratnasaṃbhavānāṃ pañcānāṃ devānām ekasminn eva bodhicitte dhyānaṃ vikalpanaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.6] pañcatathāgatadhyānam āha: sakaletyādi. apasaṃsthānam iti: apāṃ saṃsthānaṃ saṃbandho 'syety apasaṃsthānaṃ sakāraḥ. sa eva saha kalābhir vartata iti sakalaś candro, 'mṛtabījatvāt sakārasya. kṣayaṃ tejas tathaiva ceti: kṣiṇoti dahatīti kṣayaḥ kṣakāraḥ. sa eva tejo, vahnisādharmyāt. yathā vāyuś ca nityānām iti:
vāyubījaṃ kathaṃ tarhi tad iti praśne yatheti nidarśane. vāyur yakāraḥ. kuta ity āha: nityānām iti nityam. vāyurūpatvād iti bhāvaḥ. haṃ sārthe pṛthivī sākṣiṇa iti: haṃ hakāraḥ. pṛthivīti pṛthivībījam. sa ca hakāraḥ sākṣīr atītādīnām. kasmin satīty āha: sārtha iti.
samāne 'rthe śūnyatādinā samāne padārthajāte sa hakāraḥ sākṣīḥ pṛthivīvat, pṛthivyutpattibījatvāc ca.
[2.4.7] bindunādena saṃyojyeti: etāni catvāri bījāni bindunā nādena ca yojyānīti bhāvaḥ. tena suṃ yuṃ kṣuṃ huṃ iti syāt. jñānam akṣara pañcadheti: jñānaṃ hūṃkāraḥ. sa cākṣaram. na kṣaratīty akṣaram ākāśam. ākāśabīja ity arthaḥ. pañcadheti: pañca bījāni. tatra madhye tu candrasyeti:
tatra bodhicittamadhye candrabimbaṃ, tatra ca candrabimbe 'mṛtākṣaram akāraṃ madhyagaṃ candrabimbamadhyastham ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.8] pañca pañceti vaktavyam iti: ekaikam eva pañcapañcātmakam amṛtam iti bhāvaḥ. yathā bodhicitte pañcabuddhātmakapañcabhūtabījaiḥ suṃ-yuṃ-kṣuṃ-huṃ-hūṃkārair akṣobhyāmoghasiddhyamitābhavairocanaratnasaṃbhavasvabhāvair adhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā tadupari candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupary akāraṃ cintayet, tathānyeṣv api caturṣv amṛteṣu.
ekaikasyāmṛtasya pañcātmakatvāt pañcaviṃśatyātmakatvaṃ pañcāmṛtasya. pañcāmṛtāni yasmiñ jāte tat pañcāmṛtam. vajrāmbu-ātmaśodhana iti: itthaṃ vajrāmbuśodhane sati bhogād bhāvābhāvavinirmukto yogī syād iti jñeyam. vajrāmbuna upalakṣaṇatvād anyeṣām apy amṛtānām ācaraṇād bhāvābhāvavinirmukta iti siddham.
bhāva utpādo 'bhāvo 'nutpādaḥ, tābhyāṃ varjitaś cirāvasthitatvāt. tasmān mohāni dūrata iti: yata evaṃ tato moho 'śucijñānaṃ dūrato varjanīya iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā tathābhūtena krameṇa bodhicitte bhukte moho dūrato 'vasthita iti. evam anyatrāmṛte draṣṭavyam.
[2.4.9] bāhyāmṛtetyādi: yathā bāhyaṃ vastu vahnipākavikopitaṃ sad amṛtavat sudravyaṃ bhavaty evaṃ kukṣyādyā vairocanādayaḥ. kukṣisaṃbhūtatvāt kukṣir vairocanaḥ. ādiśabdād akṣobhyādayaḥ. kiṃbhūtā ete 'mṛtībhavantīty āha: jñāna-m-agniṃ tu ucchiṣṭā iti. śodhanajñānāgnijuṣṭā jñānāgninā pakvā ity arthaḥ.
yathā bāhyaṃ vastu pakvaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ bhavati tathā vairocanādayo 'pi jñānāgnipakvā muktaprakṛtirūpā viśiṣṭā bhavantīti bhāvaḥ. tathāpi ke ta ity āha: viṭ dravā iti. viḍ vairocanaḥ, dravo 'kṣobhyaḥ.
[2.4.10] agnipācaṃ tu m-agnīnām iti: pūrvaṃ tāvac charīravahninā pakvā idānīṃ jñānāgnineti bhāvaḥ. bodhicittaṃ vairocanam akṣobhyaṃ ca śodhanāgnipakvaṃ bhakṣayet. etad evāha: tasya madhye tu āhuta iti. tasya śarīrasya madhye tenāhutir deyā. vajrāmbu yasya bījena japaṃ caiva sabījateti: vajrāmbu yena bījena śodhitaṃ tad eva bījaṃ japet.
prajñopāyasamāpattau viśuddhyarthaṃ draṣṭavyam. ata āha: sabījateti. saha bījena mantrākṣareṇa vartata iti sabījo yas tasya bhāvaḥ sabījatā samāpattau. vajrābjaśodhanaṃ tatra tatra nirdekṣyati. atha vā yena bījena vajrāmbuśodhanaṃ tenaiva bhakṣaṇam iti granthārthaḥ.
[2.4.11] ata āha: tasya madhya iti. tasya bodhicittasya madhye 'kārasya japaḥ kartavya iti bhāvaḥ. na kevalam akāraḥ kiṃ tarhi kṣuṃkāro 'pīty āha: jvālārkaṃ tv ityādi. ciccākṣaraṃ kṣuṃkāraḥ. jvālāmālākulaṃ bhānusamaprabhaṃ kṣuṃkāraṃ ca japed ity arthaḥ. a kṣuṃ iti japtavyam ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.12] padmeśvaram ityādi ...
[2.4.13] ... vyākhyātam eva.
[2.4.14] amṛtodakanāmasya tribhir devābhi kīrtita iti. ete bodhicittādyā lokeśvarādirūpā amṛtanāmānas te ca traya iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.15] pañcāmṛtapraśnavisarjanānantaram alikālipraśnavisarjanam āha: alītyādi. akrameṇa praśnavisarjanaṃ cānabhiniveśasūcanāya. anupamaṃ sukharasaṃ lāty ādatte 'nenety alir ity asminn arthe 'yam aliśabdaḥ. śabdāpaśabdayor anāsthā, kalpitatvāt. tato 'yam alir upāya ucyate. vajram ity arthaḥ. hevajratantre tv āliśabdasyākāro dīrgha eva.
sa cāliśabdaḥ prajñāvācakaḥ. ayaṃ tv aliśabda upāyābhidhāyī. bhramareti: bhramaṃ rahayaty aneneti bhramaraḥ. bhramarā vajrabhairaveti: bahavo ramante 'neneti vajram. bhairavo bhayado bālānām advayapratītihetutvāt. ali bhramaro vajrabhairava ity upāyaparyāyāḥ. bodhicittasya saṃjñām āha: alim ākāśa paryantaṃ dharmadhātu svabhāvata iti.
avakāśanta utpadyante sattvā asmād iti alir ākāśam. pari samantāt sukham antati badhnātīti paryantam. ati adi bandhana iti dhātuḥ. svalakṣaṇadhāraṇād dharmāḥ sattvās tān dadhāti puṣṇātīti dharmadhātuḥ. svasmin bhavatīti svabhāvaḥ. prathamārthe svabhāvata iti. alir ākāśaṃ dharmadhātuḥ svabhāva iti bodhicittasya paryāyāḥ.
[2.4.16] katham asyeyaṃ vyutpattir ity āha: sakaletyādi. nityam atiśayena caranty akārye saṃsarantīti carāḥ pṛthagjanāḥ. nākārye nityam atiśayena carantīty acarāḥ prekṣāpūrvakāriṇaḥ. dehānām iti dehinaḥ. sakalāḥ sarvadehinaḥ. saha carācarair vartanta iti sacarācarā dharmadhātor evotpadyante.
na kevalam eta evotpadyante kiṃ tarhi guhyadhiyo 'pīty āha: guhyadhīr iti. guhyata iti guhyaṃ tattvaṃ prajñopāyātmakaṃ jñānaṃ, tatra dhīr yeṣāṃ te guhyadhiyo buddhabodhisattvāḥ. te 'py anabhiniviṣṭāḥ sattvārthakaraṇaṃ prati dharmadhātor evotpadyanta iti bhāvaḥ. prasaṅgād bodhicittasyāpi saṃjñākathanam: aṣṭādaśetyādi.
stanādyaṣṭādaśasu sthāneṣu yad amṛtaṃ prāg uktaṃ bodhicittaṃ, tac ca sarati galatīti sarvaṃ, tathābhūtaṃ sad indati sukhadānaṃ praty ādhipatyaṃ karotīndriyam.
[2.4.17] tasya madhye tu m-ākṛṣyeti: tasyeti tat. madhye tv iti madhyaviśrāntatvān madhyam ākṛṣṭam. ālīty aliḥ. etena bodhicittam evāvartitam. madhye tu līyata iti: kamalakuliśavaraṭakamadhye līyate. tatra śliṣṭaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ. aṣṭādaśāṅgasthaṃ yad bodhicittaṃ tad ākṛṣṭaṃ kamalakuliśavaraṭakamadhyagataṃ kāryam iti samudāyārthaḥ.
katham ākraṣṭavyam ity āha: bhairavaṃ vajranādeneti. kuliśāsphālaneneti bhāvaḥ. utpanno dravya yogata iti: dvīndriyayogata utpannaṃ tad amṛtaṃ dravyaṃ bhavati.
[2.4.18] katham ity āha: aniletyādi. atha vā yogata iti (17d) devatāyogataḥ. yogata utpannaṃ bodhicittaṃ dravyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ bhavati. prakārāntareṇāpi śreṣṭhatvam ity āha: aniletyādi. anilo yakāraḥ. analo rephaḥ. arthyata ity artha upādeyaḥ. tāvat sādhanaṃ mṛgyate yāvad artho labhyate. tataḥ sādhanamārgaṇasyārtho 'nto bhavati.
tenārtho 'nta iti paryāyau. saptīnām ādityāśvānām artho 'ntaḥ saptamas turaga ity arthaḥ. tena saptyarthaśabdenādityaturagaḥ saptamaḥ. saptamatvena sādharmyāt saptyarthaḥ sakāro hakāraś ca, yato yakārarephayor yathāsaṃkhyaṃ tau saptamau. vajrībījaṃ sakāraḥ. sa cāmṛtasvabhāvatvād ardhacandrasya paryāyaḥ.
tataś ca vajrībījaṃ sakāro 'rdhacandra iti paryāyāḥ. tena yojayed dhakāram iti yojyam. hakārasya śirasy ardhacandraṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ. bindunādasamutkrāntam iti: ardhacandropari binduḥ. adhastān nāda ūkāraḥ. tābhyāṃ samutkrānta ākrānto hakāraḥ. tena hūṃ iti syāt. ayaṃ ca kuliśavaraṭakamadhye dhyātavyaḥ.
[2.4.19] tatra madhye tu candrasya amṛtākṣara madhyagam ity (7cd) uddiṣṭam akāram uddhāreṇa nirdiśann āha: svaretyādi. svarapūrvaṃ bījam akāraḥ. tac cādibhūtaṃ śreṣṭhatvāt. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[2.4.20] prajñāpraśnavisarjanam āha: kālītyādi. kaṃ sukham āleḍhi samāsvādaty asyām iti kāliḥ. strītvaṃ prajñārūpatvāt. dharmodayāyā abhidhānam etat. puṣpati vajrāsphālanena vikasatīti puṣpam. kāliḥ puṣpam iti paryāyau. ākrānta puṣpavigraha vajriṇām iti:
tatra puṣpābhidhānāyāṃ trikoṇāyāṃ śuklāyāṃ viśvapadmavajragarbhāyāṃ dharmodayāyāṃ vajradharo mahāsukharūpa ākrāmyati viharati. tathā cāha:
sukhasya rakṣaṇād eva sukhāvatīti śabditam |
iti. na kevalaṃ mahāsukharūpīty āha: puṣpavigraheti. puṣpeti bodhicittasyāpi saṃjñā. puṣpaṃ bodhicittaṃ vigrahaḥ śarīram asyeti puṣpavigrahaḥ. tathā cāha hevajre:
śukrākāro bhaved bhagavān
iti. saṃsārotpatti sarveṣu mātaraṃ sarvabhūminam iti: sā ca kāliḥ sarveṣāṃ saṃsārotpattikāraṇam. ata eva mātā. sarvabhūminam iti: sarvāsu gatiṣu.
[2.4.21] jñānāmbusamudro 'sāv iti: asau kāliḥ. jñānānām ādarśādīnāṃ tāpapraśamanahetutvād ambusadṛśānām ādhārabhūtatvena samudraḥ. ata eva saiva prajñā. prapañcarahitaṃ jñānaṃ jñāyate yasyām iti prajñā. prapañcarahitajñānābhyāsād dhi sarvāṇy ādarśādijñānāny utpadyante. makaramīnakair iti: viśuddharāgādirūpā buddhās ta eva makaramīnakāḥ. tathā cāha:
bolakakkolayogena sparśāt kāṭhinyavāsanā |
kaṭhinasya mohadharmatvān moho vairocano mataḥ ||
ityādi. makaramīnakair lakṣitā kāliḥ. tathā cāha:
buddharatnakaraṇḍakam
iti. advayā paṅkamadhye tv iti: advayā bahiḥprajñā, tasyāḥ paṅkajamadhyaṃ paṅkamadhyaṃ kālimadhyam ity arthaḥ. tatra kim ity āha: upāya aṅkureṇa tv iti. tatropāyabhūto 'ṅkuraḥ. sa ca khagamukhā nāḍī.
[2.4.22] aṅkurasvarūpam āha: vikasadbandhūkanibham iti. vikasadbandhūkakusumasadṛśaḥ. madhu-m-amṛta-m-ālayam iti: madhu rajaḥ, amṛtaṃ bodhicittaṃ, tayor ālayaḥ. kālītyādi: ayam apy aṅkuraḥ kālisaṃjñāṃ labhate 'mṛtāsvādāspadabhūtaḥ. tasya bandhūkanirbhāsasyāṅkurasya vivartā vikāsaḥ. tasmin sati kim ity āha: puṣpam ambuṣa iti. puṣpaṃ rajaḥ.
tad evāmburūpam. tat sravatīti bhāvaḥ. khagamukhā nāḍī yā sāṅkurasaṃjñā kālisaṃjñā ca vikasitā satī yugapat krameṇa vā kusumodakaṃ bodhicittaṃ vā sravatīty arthaḥ. pūrvaṃ tāvad amṛtavajrāmbv iti (4a) granthenālim ākāśetyādinā (15c) ca bodhicittam uktam. viṭ dravā ity (9d) anena vairocanākṣobhyau ca. puṣpam ambuṣa ity (22d) anena ca raja uktam.
upadeśataś ca kheṭasya parigrahaḥ. etena pañcaitāny amṛtāni siddhāni. pratyekaṃ pañcātmakatvaṃ śeṣāṇām amṛtānāṃ bodhicittasyeva sādhanīyam. kiṃ ca yatra pañcāmṛtam ucyate tatra kusumasyaiva grahaṇaṃ na tu raktamātrasya, puṣpam ity asya tadvācakatvāt.
[2.4.23] kṣuṃkārasyoddhāram āha: dviguṇetyādi. dviguṇākṣaraṃ kṣakāraḥ, kakāraṣakārayoḥ samudāyarūpatvāt. kṣakāraṃ mūrdhni bindunā mūle ca nādena yojayet. kṣuṃ iti syāt. tad akṣaraṃ bījabhūtaṃ devatānāṃ yoginīnāṃ kulamātā. atha vā devatā vahnidevatā, tasyāḥ kulamātā. tato bodhicitte tad dhyāyāt.
[2.4.24] bodhicittamadhyacandrastham akāraṃ dhyātvā kṣuṃkāram arkatejasaṃ dhyāyād ity āha: candretyādi. tatra madhye tu candrasya amṛtākṣara madhyagam ity (7cd) upasaṃhāravākyam idam. pravṛttikāle cākāraṃ padmakarṇikāyāṃ dhyāyād iti jñeyam. bhāvetyādi subodham.
[2.4.25] bahirupāyo 'pi yaḥ so 'py aliḥ, bahiḥprajñā ca kālir ity āha: alītyādi. tathāgatīti: tathāviparītaṃ gacchati jānāty asyām iti tathāgatī prajñaiva. ubhayor anayor bāhyayor alikālyor alikāliyogajaṃ yad bodhicittam amṛtaṃ vidhinā sevyamānam uttamaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.26] idānīṃ vyaktidvāreṇākāram uddharati: yathetyādi. etac ca vyākhyātakalpam. kiṃ ca ḍakārasyādhastād daṇḍaṃ dattvā mantram uddharet. yathā a iti mantrabījaṃ, na tu a iti ḍakāraṣakārasamudāyaḥ. ḍakāradaṇḍayoḥ samudāyo 'tra tantre akārasya vyaktiḥ. uccāraṇaṃ tu samānam eva. vyaktiḥ paraṃ bhidyate.
[2.4.27] ebhir ityādi: etat pañcāmṛtaṃ tattvam, anenātmanaḥ śarīrasya kṣālanaṃ dehinām. amṛtā ityādi: pañcāmṛtaṃ ye viśeṣata itthaṃ śodhanaviśeṣeṇa jānanti bhakṣayantīti yāvat, te 'mṛtā na mṛtā iti bhāvaḥ. mṛtyumāro na bhavatīti yāvat.
[2.4.28] atha maraṇaṃ syāt. tadāmṛtānāṃ gatidvārāṇi devamanuṣyagatayaḥ śuddhībhavantīti. samālokyeti draṣṭavyam ity arthaḥ. kasyāṃ gatāv ity āha: kāyasya yatra ceti. kāyasya śarīrasya yasyām utpādas tasyām iti bhāvaḥ.
atha vā yogajam amṛtaṃ bodhicittaṃ eva kevalam ājñātam adhiṣṭhitaṃ svecchayā svādayanti ye te 'py amṛtā bhavantīti yogetyādi gāthārdhasyārthaḥ.
[2.4.29] aṅkuśapraśnavisarjanam āha: śṛṇv ityādi. he vajrapāṇe, pañca bhakṣyāṇi pañcāṅkuśān śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. yathātattvam ity aviparītatvena. viśeṣata iti pūrvoktakramād viśeṣeṇety arthaḥ. yenetyādi: yeṣām aṅkuśānāṃ jñānapūrvakabhakṣaṇena gatir mokṣaḥ śīghraṃ dṛṣṭaḥ, ihaiva janmani mokṣasaṃbhavāt, anyatrāsaṃkhyeyakalpadurlabhatvāt tasya.
tān aṅkuśān śṛṇv iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.30] aṅkuśān āha: mahāmatītyādi. mahāmatir gajaḥ. vākyasyeti: vākyo gauḥ. sajātīti śvā. cchṛṣṭavīraketi turagaḥ. sphaṭikena tu bhoktavyam iti: śodhanena bhoktavyam iti bhāvaḥ. mahāṅkuśetīti: mahāsamayaḥ. yoginām iti yoginā.
[2.4.31] vijñānabījaśabdena hūṃkāraḥ, tasya japas tatra mahāṅkuśe kartavyaḥ. etad evāha: jāpānām iti. hūṃkāras tatra madhye dhyeya ity arthaḥ. bhakṣa sphaṭikacitteneti: vikalpamalavirahitena cittena bhakṣayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.32] ekam aṅkuśa vighnasyeti: vighnāṅkuśo hastī. mahāṅkuśavighnāṅkuśayor ekam evādhiṣṭhānam ekabījatvāt. ata āha: tasyetyādi. hūṃkāraṃ vighnāṅkuśe 'pi dhyāyād ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.33] sthānādhipatyam aṅkuśyā iti: sthānādhipatiḥ śvā, svasthāne balīyastvāt. prayojyaṃ tatra kāraṇād iti: tatra prayogaḥ kartavya iti bhāvaḥ. kenety āha: ghriṃkāreṇa tu bījasyeti. ghriṃbījenādhiṣṭhātavyam ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.34] hayāṅkuśam adhiṣṭhetum ākṛti sarva sarvavid iti: hayāṅkuśākṛtiṃ sarvathā sarvavid yogy adhitiṣṭhet. kenety āha: jriṃkāreṇa tv iti. turagāṅkuśe jriṃbījaṃ dhyeyam iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.35] vajrāṅkuśo gauḥ. sa sriṃbījenādhiṣṭhātavyaḥ. tataḥ kiṃ syād ity āha: sriṃkārādhiṣṭhānapūrvakaṃ bhakṣitena gādisamayena vajrakāyo bhavati yogī. ebhiḥ sarvair aṅkuśaiḥ pāpakṣālanaṃ ca bhavati. ete hy atyarthaṃ siddhiṃ kuśnantīty aṅkusāḥ.
[2.4.36] balikālam āha: aṣṭamyām ityādi. devatāyoginā yathāvidhānam āsu tithiṣv ebhir dravyair anyaiś ca balir deyaḥ. katham ity āha: yatneneti. niyamena.
[2.4.37] nityaṃ ca pūjayā siddhiḥ syād ity āha: nityetyādi. yadepsā bhuktimuktivad iti: bhuktir bhogaḥ svargādilakṣaṇaḥ. muktir mokṣo buddhatvam.
[2.4.38] sidhyante sarvakarmāṇīti (37c) yad uktaṃ tad darśayann āha: rājakuletyādi. rājakulaṃ daśadravyakṛtāṃ gulikām āsvādya jñānaḍākinīyogavān praviśati yadi tadā śīghrataraṃ śrīyukto bhavati. priyāpriyatamam iti: anenaiva krameṇāpriyatamaḥ priyo bhavati. icched darśanaṃ cāpi sidhyatīti: īpsitadarśanaṃ sidhyati.
[2.4.39] daridro 'rthārthī yadi tadarddhiḥ sphītā bhavati. gṛhāṇi ca gṛhārthinām iti bhāvaḥ. cyutītyādi sugamam.
[2.4.40] daśa dravyāṇīti: amṛtāni pañca, vairocanaratnasaṃbhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhyakṣobhyāḥ. amoghasiddhiḥ kheṭaḥ. pañca ca gokudahanāḥ. gandhena saha yojitair ebhir dhūpād ātmano jagatsaṃmohako yogī bhavati (41cd). kathaṃ kṛtvety āha: daśa-akṣaretyādi.
vairocanaratnasaṃbhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhyakṣobhyāṇāṃ bījāni huṃ hūṃ kṣuṃ yuṃ suṃ iti yathākramaṃ draṣṭavyāni. gokudahanānāṃ ca sriṃ ghriṃ hūṃ jriṃ hūṃ iti bījāni. oṃ huṃ hūṃ kṣuṃ yuṃ suṃ sriṃ ghriṃ hūṃ jriṃ hūṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[2.4.41] asya mantrasya daśākṣarasya jñānaḍākinīyoginā balidānapūrvakaṃ lakṣaṃ japtvā pūrvasevā kāryā. tatas tena mantreṇāṣṭottaraśatajapto dhūpo grāhyaḥ. uttaratrāpy ayam eva mantraḥ. ata āha: lakṣetyādi. tathaiva pūrvasevā kāryety arthaḥ.
[2.4.42–45] ebhiḥ karmasya yoktavyam iti: ebhir dravyair yuktaṃ candanādikaṃ yathoktasādhakam iti nāsti saṃdehaḥ. etad evāha: ahaṃvākyaṃ na saṃśaya iti. mama vākyam etad ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.46] idānīm yoginīsādhanam āha: bhagavann ityādi. †...† dīpinyādicaturyoginīsādhanam. kimarthaṃ dūtīsādhanam ity āha: dūtītyādi. yoginīnām ātmīkaraṇārtham ity arthaḥ. ātmīkaraṇam eva kimartham ity āha: kim idaṃ siddhi yoginīti. kimartham idam ātmīkaraṇam? yataḥ siddhāsu satīṣu yoginīṣu sarvakarmasādhako yogī bhavati.
[2.4.47] ata āha: sarvetyādi. sarvaṃ karmābhisārapradhānaṃ, tatsiddhyarthaṃ samartho bhavatīty āśayaḥ. tato yatnena dūtīḥ sādhayet.
[2.4.48] sādhanam āha: ekavṛkṣasyetyādi. ekavṛkṣamūle vidhinā balidānapūrvakaṃ mantrī samayārādhanatatparo mantraṃ lakṣaṃ japtvā dūtīṃ vakṣyamāṇāṃ prasādhayed ity arthaḥ. japam evāha: pauruṣāpādetyādi. pauruṣaṃ hastamātram. pādo hastasya caturtho bhāgaḥ. sapādahastamātraṃ paṭaṃ vistārya dūtīṃ sādhayed iti saṃbandhaḥ. rūpādi cintitam iti:
yathācintitaṃ dīpinīrūpaṃ lekhyam iti bhāvaḥ. avadhūtādikarpaṭe likhanīyam ity upadeśaḥ.
[2.4.49] dīpinīrūpam āha: dvādaśetyādi. dvādaśāṅgulapramāṇā pītavarṇam ityādiviśeṣaṇā dīpinī likhanīyā. tayā paṭa ākrānta iti boddhavyam. evaṃvidhaṃ paṭam agrato vistṛtaṃ kṛtvā sādhako dīpinīṃ sādhayed iti jñeyam.
[2.4.50] vidhipūjādīti: raktapuṣpagandhādi (51a).
[2.4.51] śuṣkamāṃsādi dhūpayed iti: pūjāṃ paṭasyāgrataḥ kṛtvā śuṣkamāṃsadhūpaṃ dadyāt.
[2.4.52] tato baliṃ dattvā daśavastubhiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā lakṣamantreṇa kṛtapūrvasevaḥ punar api mantraṃ lakṣaṃ japet. pañca vastūni pañcāmṛtam. pañca dravyāṇi gokudahanāḥ.
[2.4.53] āgatāyā dūtyā mudrāṃ darśayed ity āha: samayetyādi.
[2.4.54] oṃ ityādimantro japyaḥ.
[2.4.55–56] dvau hastetyādi samayamudrā. uccared iti: mudrādarśanakāle 'py amum eva mantram uccārayed ity arthaḥ.
[2.4.57] pañcāmṛtena pūrayitvā kapālaṃ samayodakaṃ dadyād ity āha: pañcetyādi. samayā dāpya vṛtteneti: samayodakadāne vṛtte vāmahastena tāṃ gṛhītvā ...
[2.4.58] ... siddhāsīty abhidhāya dvayobhi samadhiṣṭhayed iti dvīndriyasamāpattiṃ kuryād iti samudāyārthaḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham. bhakṣyādikaṃ sādhakāya sā dadyāt.
[2.4.59] ekayāmam ekapraharaṃ tayā saha rantavyam. kuta ity āha: maithunāpriya yoginīti. yato maithunāpriyā. maithunapriyā na bhavati yataḥ, tataḥ praharād ūrdhvaṃ na kāmayitavyā. yadītthaṃ na sidhyati tadā ghoraḍākinībījena kṣuṃkāreṇa vidarbhya dīpinīmantraṃ japet. kṣuṃ oṃ dīpeśvarī hūṃ sriṃ pheṃ kṣuṃ iti vidarbhaṇaṃ, tathaiva lakṣaṃ japet.
tena bījena pīḍyamānā dīpinī sidhyati. yadi na sidhyati tadā tasyā mūrdhā sphuṭati (60b). etad evāha: yadītyādi.
[2.4.60] bhāryetyādi: anena vidhānena dīpinī bhāryā siddhā syāt. sattvārthakriyātatpara iti: bhakṣyabhojyādidānena sattvārthaḥ kartavya iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.61] śṛṇv ityādinā ḍākinīsādhanam āha. aparair ity aparaṃ sādhanam ity arthaḥ. dvihastaṃ paṭayaunatvam iti: pādonadvihastaḥ paṭaḥ kārya ity arthaḥ. balidānapūjādipuraḥsaraṃ vakṣyamāṇarūpāṃ ḍākinīṃ sādhayed iti.
[2.4.62–66] pṛthivībhājanam ārūḍham iti: pṛthivī śmaśānam. tatra bhājanaṃ citiḥ. ārūḍham iti: āropya. śmaśānaṃ gatvā citāyāṃ vistārya paṭam uktarūpaṃ ḍākinīṃ sādhayed iti bhāvaḥ. kiṃbhūtaṃ pṛthivībhājanam ity āha: samantād ityādi subodham. atrāpi pūrvasevāyāṃ lākṣiko japaḥ. siddhiś ca lakṣataḥ.
[2.4.67] oṃ ḍākinī hūṃ aiṃ phur iti mantreṇa samayamudrāṃ darśayet. ayam eva japyaś ca.
[2.4.68–96] vajrībījaṃ suṃkāraḥ. suṃ oṃ ḍākinī hūṃ aiṃ phuḥ suṃ iti vidarbhaṇam. atha vā suṃ oṃ caṇḍālī hūṃ ghrūṃ phuḥ suṃ iti. ittham api lakṣaṃ japet.
[2.4.97] asiddhā uktakarmeṇa anyatkarmeṇa kārayed iti: jñānaḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ cintayitvā ...
[2.4.98] ... kāmbojīpaṭaṃ vāmapādenākramya pañcaśatajapaḥ kāmbojīmantraḥ kartavyaḥ. tataḥ sidhyati.
[2.4.99] vīramadhye yoginīcakramadhye m-ānayed ānayed ity arthaḥ. anenāpi prakāreṇa yadi na sidhyati tadā kāmbojīpaṭaṃ vīramadhye drutam ānīya jriṃ phaḍ ity uccārayan khaṇḍakhaṇḍaṃ kuryād iti jñeyam. ghātaḥ phaṭkāraḥ. deḍḍhakair iti: gāḍham uccārayan khaṇḍakhaṇḍaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ. uccakair iti pāṭhāntaram.
[2.4.100] sarvaṃ khaṇḍaśo vibhaktaṃ paṭaṃ tatra hūṃkāraṃ dhyātvā bhakṣayed ity āha: bhakṣayed ityādi subodham. sā kāmbojī mriyate pūrveṇa karmaṇā. ittham api sā sādhyayoginī sarvāsāṃ yoginīnāṃ madhya īśvarī nāyikā. asiddhā yadi syād anenāpi vidhinā tadāgnimadhye juhuyāt (101ab). etad evāha: sādhyayoginya ityādi subodham.
[2.4.101] kālavṛkṣo riṣṭikāvṛkṣaḥ. bhallātakakālavṛkṣayoḥ kāṣṭhenāgniṃ prajvālya sarṣaparudhireṇa kaṭutailenāloḍya taṃ khaṇḍaśo vibhaktīkṛtya paṭaṃ rājikāviṣanimbayuktaṃ hūṃkāroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ śatāṣṭakaṃ juhuyād iti (102ab). etad evāha: agnimadhye tv ityādi.
[2.4.102] itthaṃ yadi na sidhyati tadā niyataṃ mriyate. na kevalaṃ kāmbojī kiṃ tarhy anyā api ḍākinyaḥ. pūrvoktasvasvavidhānato 'siddhā yadi tadānena vidhānena sidhyantīty etad eva sakalam āha: mriyanta ityādi. ḍākinya iti: yoginyaḥ. ārtasvarabhi kranditam iti: ārtanādena krandantīty arthaḥ. avaśyaṃ siddha karmasyeti:
ittham avaśyaṃ karma dūtīsādhanalakṣaṇaṃ sidhyatīty arthaḥ.
advayādvayadharmasya bhāvābhāvavikalpitam iti (2ab) praśnasya visarjanaṃ bhāvābhāvavinirmuktas tasmān mohāni dūrata ityādinā (8cd) sūcitaṃ, yogaśāstranayapraśna(2d)visarjanaṃ daśa dravyāṇītyādinā (40c) bhagavan śrotum icchāmi caturyoginīsādhanam ityādinā ca (46ab) kathitam.
[2.4.103] idānīṃ gaṇamaṇḍalapraveśakāle dvārapālaprārthanāgāthām āha: sohaï ityādi. he nīladaṇḍa krodha, śobhate tava samayaceṣṭā. nīladaṇḍa eva nīla ity ucyate, ekadeśenāpi samudāyapratīteḥ, yathāmoghasiddhir amogha iti, avalokiteśvaro 'valokita iti ca. sohaï nīla kodhu tuhu samayaceddhu iti vākyasyārthaḥ. kathaṃ sā śobhata ity āha:
pāṇihi dharaï daṇḍa māṇikkehiṃ baddhu iti. pāṇihi iti pāṇau daṇḍaṃ dhārayati ceṣṭā. kīdṛśaṃ daṇḍam ity āha: māṇikkehiṃ baddhu iti. māṇikyair maṇibhir baddham. kvacit pāṇehiṃ iti pāṭhaḥ. tatra māṇikyaviśeṣaṇam. durlabhatvāt priyatvāc ca māṇikyaiḥ prāṇaiḥ prāṇasadṛśair ity arthaḥ. itthaṃ kiṃ syād ity āha: pecchavītyādi.
prekṣye 'haṃ yoginīmadhyam iti saṃbandhaḥ. he vīra jitakleśa, mellu tyaja. kiṃ tad ity āha: duvāra dvāram. yoginīmadhyapraveśena kiṃ syād ity āha: saṃsāra[-]uttāra jāmīti. saṃsārārṇavottāraṃ yāmi prāpnomi. tataś ca tyaja mām. etad evāha: mella mahuṃ iti. anayā gāthayā maṇḍalapraveśaṃ ca śiṣyaḥ kāryate.
[2.4.104] aṅgamudrā sthāne sthāne kathitā, cihnamudrā ca. ataś ca tayoḥ praśnavisarjanaṃ siddham. idānīṃ vākyamudrāṅgamudrāpraśnavisarjanam āha: bhagavann ityādi subodham. rahasyam ityādi: rahasyaṃ gopyaṃ yogināṃ yoginīnāṃ ca yat tad guhyaguhyaṃ tu jñāpayed iti. vākyamudrālakṣaṇena jñāyata iti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.105] yoginām ityādi: yoginā dakṣiṇena mudrā darśayitavyā, yoginyā ca vāmeneti bhāvaḥ.
[2.4.106] mukhe prakṣipyetyādinā yoginīnām aṅgamudrā, cihnamudrety arthaḥ.
[2.4.107–109] gho ghu ityādi vākyamudrā.
[2.4.110–153] guravāṅguṣṭha iti madhyamāṅguliḥ.
[2.4.col.] ity uktakrameṇa pañcāmṛtādiḍākinīsādhanamudrāntaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaḥ parapīṭhasya caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ. tasminn itiprakaraṇe homādiyoginīsādhanaparyantam ātmanaḥ parabhūtatvāt paraḥ. tad eva siddheḥ pīṭham. tac ca samāptam ity āha: parapīṭhaḥ samāpta iti. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe parapīṭhaṃ samāptam.

[3.1.1] idānīṃ yogapīṭham adhikṛtyāha: bhagavann ityādi. aparair ity aparam. kiṃ tad ity āha: lakṣyalakṣaṇam iti. lakṣyāṇi catvāri vajrādīni, teṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ tattvaṃ śrotum icchāmīti saṃbandhaḥ. bhedena pṛcchann āha: catus tattvam ityādi. vajraṃ, ghaṇṭā, akṣasūtraṃ, pañcagatisaṃsārajñānaṃ ceti catvāri, teṣāṃ tattvam.
kathaṃ jñātvā prayojayed iti: tattvaṃ jñātvā vajradhāraṇādikriyāṃ kathaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.2] saṃsārottāralakṣaṇam iti: lakṣaṇaṃ vajrādisvarūpaṃ śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. tac ca saṃsārād uttīryate 'neneti saṃsārottāraḥ. tad evāha: vajra tattvasyetyādi. pūrvaṃ vajraṃ, dvitīyā ghaṇṭā, ...
[3.1.3] ... tṛtīyam akṣasūtraṃ, caturthaṃ ca jñānalakṣaṇaṃ, pañcagatikasaṃsārajñānalakṣaṇam ity arthaḥ. saṃsārottāram ity (2b) uddiṣṭaṃ nirdiśati: catur ityādi. caturṇāṃ vajrādīnāṃ tattvena vikalpataraṅgān ativāhya sattvasamūho nīyate  saṃsārapāraṃ mokṣam ity arthaḥ. īpsitam iti: yatheccham ity arthaḥ.
[3.1.4] tatra prathamaṃ vajrasya tattvam āha: madhya ityādi. vajramadhyaśūke pṛthivyādarśajñānādisvarūpaṃ vairocanaṃ dhyātvā, pūrvādiśūkeṣu yathākramaṃ jalādisuviśuddhādijñānasvarūpān akṣobhyādīṃś caturo dhyāyāt. sarvapūrva iti: sarvathā pūrvaḥ prathamaḥ kālaḥ sarvapūrvaḥ. ratnasaṃbhavādīn apekṣya sarvapūrvatvam.
[3.1.5] vajrasya śūkādhastān muṣṭiparyante padmam aṣṭadalaṃ, tatra padmapāṇyādīṃś cintayet. etad evāha: padme aṣṭetyādi. padme aṣṭeti: aṣṭadalapadme. saṃbodhyaṅgam iti: saṃbodher aṅgam upāyaḥ. yathābhūmyaṃ yathāsthānam. saṃbodhyupāyabhūtān padmapāṇyādīn pūrvādidaleṣu cintayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.6] padmapāṇim iti lokeśvaram. gaganaṃ gaganagañjam, ākāśagarbham ity arthaḥ. yakṣādhipatiṃ vajrapāṇim. kṣitiṃ kṣitigarbham. sthāpayec cintayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.7] amūn aṣṭau sthāpayed ity upasaṃhāraḥ. madhye tridaśa aṇḍānām iti: vajrasya muṣṭau devānām aṇḍaṃ sthānam. tatra jñānadevaṃ tu cintayed iti jñānaḍākinīm.
[3.1.8] aparasmin muṣṭyante sāṣṭadalapadme pūrvādidigdaleṣu vajrāṅkuśyādayo devyo vidigdaleṣu cundādayo 'pi bhāvyā iti padme aṣṭa devatītyādinoktam.
[3.1.9–11] aparāntasūcīṣu jñānaḍākinyādayo madhyādivibhāgena draṣṭavyāḥ. etad evāha: sūcyām ityādi.
[3.1.12] vajratattveti devasyeti: devatārūpeṇa vajratattvaṃ vicakṣaṇo jānīyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.13] tribhāgetyādi subodham. utpalaṃ kudmalam iti: utpalakudmalākāraḥ śūkasamudāyaḥ.
[3.1.14] diśādītyādinā navaśūkavajratattvam āha. nava śūkeṣu pūrvādiṣu padmapāṇyādaya evam aparāntaśūkeṣv api. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat.
[3.1.15] makarāsyanirgataṃ sūcīti: madhyasūcīṃ vinā pūrvādisūcyo makaramukhanirgatāḥ kāryāḥ. raśmicandrādiyuktāś ca draṣṭavyā iti sāmānyaṃ lakṣaṇam. anyatra vajrasya ghaṇṭāyāś ca lakṣaṇaṃ viśeṣeṇa jñeyam. nava sūcyeti: navāṅgasaddharmaviśuddhyā navaśūkatvaṃ, pañcajñānaviśuddhyā pañcaśūkatvaṃ ceti jñātavyam. ata āha: jñāneti.
ādarśādipañcajñānaviśuddhyā pañcaśūkatvam ity arthaḥ. jagad eva vajram, uttaraṃ viśuddhaṃ sat.
[3.1.16] kuta ity āha: yato tattvānītyādi. yato vajrasya tattvaṃ svarūpaṃ, kiṃ tad ity āha: vajrātma ātma cetaneti. vajrasyātmā svarūpaṃ saḥ, ya ātmā paraparikalpitaḥ. yata ātmā nāmamātrārūḍhaḥ paramārthato 'dvayatathatārūpa eva, tataḥ sa vajraṃ cetaneti jñātavya ity arthaḥ. vajra sārasyeti: vajraṃ cittasya sārabhūtaṃ yogicetasi dṛśyata iti bhāvaḥ.
kāraṇād ityādi: yato yogicittasārabhūtaṃ vajraṃ sakalātmakaṃ ca, ...
[3.1.17] ... tato vajraśuddhiṃ dṛḍhīkuryāt: vajraśuddhāḥ sarvadharmā vajraśuddho 'ham iti. kuta evam ity āha: vajretyādi. sarve dharmā vajram eva. kathaṃ tathā ta ity āha: vajretyādi. sarve dharmā advayajñānanirjātatvād abhedyā vajraṃ ca tadvat. śāntikādikarma vajram. abhisārata iti: dharmadhāturūpatayā sarvagatvāt.
[3.1.18] devatārūpaspharaṇena vajraṃ saṃsārād uttārakam. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.1.19] andhakāraṃ yathā dīpikā ghātayed dhanyāt, tathā viśiṣṭaṃ jñānaṃ vajrasvarūpaṃ kleśāndhakāraṃ hanti. tataś ca jñānavajreṇa dīpikābhūtena diśa ālokam āpnuvanti. jñānena sarvaṃ nirāvaraṇaṃ dṛśyata iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.20] sādhyam api vajrasvarūpam ity āha: vajra sādhitetyādi. kleśabandhanamokṣo 'pi vajram ity āha: vajra dharmasyetyādi.
[3.1.21] prajñopāyābhyāṃ jagat sādhayed ity āha: vajreṇetyādi. vajre dhṛte sarvaṃ laukikalokottaraṃ jagad dhriyata iti vajram ācāryeṇa dhriyate. vairocanādīnāṃ bījāni vā kevalaṃ bhāvayed iti.
[3.1.22] idānīṃ ghaṇṭātattvam āha: śṛṇu vajradhara ityādi. vajradhara iti vajrapāṇe ity asminn arthe. rājeti rājann ity asminn arthe. prajñācoditatattvānām iti: prajñāpāramitāviśuddhyā ghaṇṭātattvaṃ, śṛṇu tāṃ bhava-m-uttare iti: tāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ śṛṇu, tato bhavottāro bhavatīty arthaḥ.
[3.1.23] śūnyāḥ sarvadharmā anabhisaṃskārāḥ sarvadharmāḥ prakṛtinirvṛtāḥ sarvadharmā ityādidhvaninākāśadhātuṃ vyāpya yāvadbodhimaṇḍavyavasthānakālaṃ ca vyāpya prajñāpāramitātattvaṃ ghaṇṭā dhvanantī vivṛṇotīty āha:
ākāśadhātvityādi. ākāśadhātuparyantaṃ nirdiśann āha: daśadig ityādi.
[3.1.24] aṣṭeṣu pārśveṣv aṣṭa devīs tadbījāni vā cintayed ity āha: diśe ityādi.
[3.1.25–26] sauvarṇī vasudhārā. madhurā cundā. kāntī bhṛkuṭī. vajramāleti yathārthanāmnī.
[3.1.27–29] śūkeṣu vajravad vairocanādayo draṣṭavyāḥ: ūrdhvaṃ padmaṃ vajrāṇām iti śūkāḥ. yathāpūrvam iti: vajrasyeva. madhyaṃ jñānadaṇḍarūpam.
[3.1.30] cālayed aṣṭadiśaṃ tv iti: ghaṇṭām ādāya vṛddhānāmikābhyāṃ saṃyamyāṣṭasu pūrvādidikṣu cālayet. aṣṭasu dikṣv aṣṭau dhvanīn kṛtvā ghaṇṭāṃ sthāpayed ity arthaḥ. nādhikaṃ dhvānayatīti jñeyam. kathaṃ cālayed ity āha: ālikāliprayojitam iti. ālir vajram. kālir ghaṇṭā. jyeṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ vajraṃ dakṣiṇenādāya ghaṇṭāṃ nādayed iti bhāvaḥ.
kuta evam ity āha: ālītyādi. prajñopāyayogena hi saṃsāraṃ chinatti.
[3.1.31] vajrāṅkitā ghaṇṭā vajraghaṇṭā. sā copāyam. upaiti vajram upāyam ity upāyaḥ. upaiti prajñāṃ ghaṇṭām ity upāyo vajram. balyādidānakāle na kevalā ghaṇṭā, na kevalaṃ vajraṃ ca, kiṃ tarhi tayor yaugapadyena dhāraṇam. ittham anyonyānugamaḥ. madhyam eva ceti: muṣṭau dhāraṇaṃ pratipādayati. dhāraṇaviśuddhim āha: pāṇau dvāv ityādi subodham.
[3.1.32] upāyaśabdena ghaṇṭāvyutpādadvāreṇa svadehe dhārayed iti. svecchetyādi: dakṣiṇe kare sūryaṃ vāme candraṃ ca cintayitvā yathākramaṃ vajraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ ca dhārayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.33] dhāraṇagāthādvayam āha: hūṃkāretyādi. hūṃkāroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ ...
[3.1.34] ... sohia ityādigāthāṃ vajrollālena paṭhet. vajrasyollālaḥ kiṃbhūtaḥ? tāritāḥ sattvā yena, sa tāritasattva iti parārthaṃ darśayati. vimokkheti: vikalpān mokṣata iti vimokṣo vajrollālaḥ. ata eva sattvān uttārayatīti siddham. kuta evam ity āha: dhammavimokkhe kajju iti. vajrasya dharmaḥ prakṛtiḥ, yato vikalpamokṣaḥ.
mokṣa ādana iti dhātoḥ prayogaḥ. tataś ca vajju susāra varaṃ tu. vajraṃ punaḥ sāreṇa sthirāṅgena śunyatāsvabhāvatvād varam.
[3.1.35] oṃ ityādi gāthā ghaṇṭādhāraṇe pāṭhyā.
[3.1.36] prajñopāyena nirnādeti: prajñā śūnyatā. upāyo bhāvaḥ. ubhayasvabhāvena nadatīti bhāvaḥ. prajñopāyarūpatvaṃ devatārūpatvam anyeṣāṃ kartuṃ nadatīti draṣṭavyam. kuta evam ity āha: prajñām ityādi. prajñopāyarūpatvena sarvadharmāḥ pratibuddhā iti jagāda. vāśabdād gadati gadiṣyati ca. ajñānetyādi: †...†
[3.1.37] tattvaṃ paramārthaḥ. paramārthaviśuddhyā vajraṃ dhārayitavyam. sarvadharmāḥ śūnyā ityādinādaviśuddhyā ghaṇṭā nādayitavyā. śuddhaṃ cittaṃ vajram. ghaṇṭā śarīraṃ kāyaḥ. tannādo vāk. cittakāyavāgviśuddhyā
vajravajraghaṇṭāgrahaṇam iti vajraghaṇṭābhiṣekakāle 'pi draṣṭavyam.
[3.1.38] tattvam ajñātvā yo ghaṇṭāṃ nādayati, sa hastīva ghaṇṭhāṃ dhārayati.
[3.1.39–41] vajrācāryeṇa vajravratam ācaratā vajraṃ dhārayitavyam iti yanmadhyetyādinā (?) pratipāditam.
[3.1.42] akṣasūtratattvam āha: śṛṇv ityādi subodham.
[3.1.43–46] kālabījaṃ riṣṭikābījam.
[3.1.47–49] digvidigviśuddhyāṣṭau sūtrāṇi, teṣu ca padmapāṇyādayo bhāvyāḥ. navamaṃ sūtraṃ madhyaviśuddhyā, tatra sarve buddhā vajradharo vā draṣṭavyāḥ. etad evāha: diśāhītyādi.
[3.1.50] kanyākarttitena sūtreṇākṣasūtraṃ grathanīyam ity āha: saṃpūrṇetyādi.
[3.1.51] evaṃviśiṣṭakanyākarttitena sūtreṇa yad grathitam akṣasūtraṃ, tena japāc chāntikādisiddhayo muktiś ca bhavantīti bhāvaḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.1.52–63] akṣasūtraśodhanamantram āha: akkharu ityādi. akkharu kāraṇu rūvu iti: akṣarāṇy akārādikakārādīni kāraṇāni yasya rūpasya, tad akṣarakāraṇam. akṣarakāraṇaṃ rūpaṃ svarūpaṃ yasya, so 'kṣara kāraṇarūpo mantraḥ, sohia iti śodhitaḥ. manta vīti mantra eva. śodhito mantra eva sāraḥ. śodhanaṃ ca nairātmyādinā. etad evāha: gaṇiasaṃkhu alikku iti.
gaṇitā saṃkhyā yasya, sa gaṇitasaṃkhyo, mantraḥ. alīkaś ca. alīkatvena nirūpaṇam viśodhanam. evaṃviśiṣṭo mantraḥ sidhyati, yoginīnāṃ sāro hṛdayam. bamhaṇetyādi (?) subodham. kiṃ ca brāhmaṇaḥ kāyaḥ. śūdro vāk. kṣatriyaś cittam. eṣām ekatvena sidhyati, yoginījālaṃ mantraḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham. akkharu-ādisvāhānto (63) mantraḥ.
[3.1.64] anenākṣasūtrasya śodhanam. akṣarāṇāṃ mantrāṇāṃ sūtraṇāt saṃkhyākaraṇād akṣasūtraḥ. tatas tena japyamāno mantra īpsitāṃ devatāṃ sādhayen nānyatheti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.65] idānīṃ pañcagatikasaṃsāratattvam āha: śṛṇv ityādi. jñānabhedena karmabhedena dūrasthāḥ sattvāḥ paraspareṇa bhinnāḥ. kiṃ tv anyad ity āha: sāmānyajñānavākyata iti. sāmānyājñānatveneti bhāvaḥ. jñānamātraṃ jagad iti nyāyāt.
[3.1.66] daityādaitya paraṃ daitya iti: †...† daityā iti bhāvaḥ. yakṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ. asurā rākṣasāḥ. siddhakair iti devāḥ.
[3.1.67] avīcyā ekavidhā rūpā iti. avīcyā iti: avīcigatāḥ sattvāḥ. sūkṣmeti sūkṣmāḥ prāṇinaḥ. aṇḍā iti: aṇḍajāḥ. dhāraṇā iti: avadhāryatām.
[3.1.68–70] sāmānyetyādi: jñānamātratvāj jagatāṃ sāmānyaṃ jñānaṃ jagad ity arthaḥ. kiṃ kathaṃ tu bhedaḥ? pañcagatikatveneti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.71–72] devabhedam āha: viṃśetyādi. viṃśeti viṃśatiḥ. triketi: tryadhikā viṃśatir devānāṃ sthānāni. dvigu ūrdhvaṃ tu ūrdhvaśa iti: ūrdhvam ūrdhvaṃ dviguṇam āyur devānām. tatrāyaṃ kramaḥ. ṣaṭ kāmāvacarāḥ: catur mahārājakāyikaḥ, trāyastriṃśaḥ, yāmaḥ, tuṣitaḥ, nirmāṇaratiḥ, paranirmitavaśavarttī ceti. ete ṣaṭ kāmadhātoḥ. ata ūrdhvaṃ rūpadhātuḥ:
saptadaśa sthānāntarāṇi. prathamadhyānasya sthānadvayaṃ bhūmidvayaṃ ca: brahmakāyikāḥ, brahmapurohitāḥ. dvitīye dhyāne trīṇī sthānāntarāṇi, tisro bhūmayaḥ: parīttābhāḥ, apramāṇābhāḥ, ābhāsvarāḥ. tṛtīye dhyāne trīṇi sthānāntarāṇi: parīttaśubhāḥ, apramāṇaśubhāḥ, śubhakṛtsnāḥ. caturthe dhyāne 'ṣṭau sthānāntarāṇi:
anabhrakāḥ, puṇyaprasavāḥ, bṛhatphalāḥ, abṛhāḥ, atapāḥ, sudṛśāḥ, sudarśanāḥ, akaniṣṭhāḥ. mahābrahmāṇo 'saṃjñisattvāś ca navamaṃ bhūmyantaram. sthānaśabdena bhūmaya uktāḥ. etena trayoviṃśati devasthānāni devabhūmayaḥ. nṝṇāṃ pañcāśad varṣaiḥ ṣaṭ kāmāvacarāṇāṃ devānām ahorātram. evaṃbhūtair ahorātrair māsasamvatsarāḥ saṃkhyāyante.
anayā saṃkhyayā pañcaśatasamvatsaram āyur mahārājikānām. ato dviguṇena dinādikrameṇa trāyastriṃśānāṃ samvatsarasahasram āyuḥ. ato dviguṇena dinādikrameṇa yāmānām āyuḥ samvatsarasahasradvayam. itthaṃ dinādidviguṇatvenāyuḥpramāṇaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ trayāṇām api draṣṭavyam. iti ṣaṭ kāmāvacarāṇām āyurniyamaḥ.
rūpadhātusthitānāṃ devānāṃ punar ahorātrasaṃkhyā nāsti. kalpakrameṇa teṣām āyurvṛddhiḥ. ūrdhvaśa iti: ittham uparyuparibhāvenāyurvṛddhis trikaviṃśānāṃ devānām iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.1.73–77] śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.1.col.] ity uktakrameṇa vajraghaṇṭāditattvaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaṃ yogapīṭham. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe yogapīṭhe prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ.

[3.2.1] idānīṃ cakrādinā śāntikādisādhanapaṭalam āha: śṛṇv ityādi. cakrasādhya viśeṣata iti: cakreṇa sādhyaṃ śāntikādikarma tac cakrasādhyam. viśeṣata iti: pūrvoktaśāntyādikarmaṇo viśeṣata iti bhāvaḥ. sādhyam evāha: śāntītyādi.
[3.2.2] navakoṣṭhādicakrasyeti: aṣṭābhir arair varaṭakena ca yuktam aṣṭāram eva navakoṣṭhaṃ cakraṃ bhavati. bāhya nimnaṃ tu kārayed iti: bāhyato nemir bhāvyā. karmavarṇādi cakrasyeti: karmāṇurūpaṃ varṇabhāvanam iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.3] mantram āha: oṃ ityādi.
[3.2.4] asya bījaṃ tu sarveṣām iti: etāni bījāni madhyādikoṣṭheṣu cintanīyāni. koṣṭheṣu pūrvādiṣv aṣṭasu tākārādīny aṣṭāv akṣarāṇi, oṃkārahākārau madhye. ata āha: praṇamā anta madhyaketi. praṇameti: praṇava oṃkāraḥ. anteti: mantrānto hākāraḥ. madhyaketi: madhyagau tau dvau bhāvyāv iti bhāvaḥ. yojayen madhya nāmaṃ tv iti:
oṃkārahākārayor madhye sādhyanāmeti bhāvaḥ. rakṣāvākyaṃ tv iti: ādiśabdo 'tra draṣṭavyaḥ. tena rakṣādivākyayuktaṃ sādhyanāma praṇavahākārayor madhye bhāvyaṃ jñeyam. oṃ devadattaṃ rakṣa hetyādiyogaḥ.
[3.2.5] etac cakraṃ kathaṃ bhāvayitavyam ity āha: samatetyādi. samatayā śūnyatājñānaṃ samatājñānaśūnyeṣv ity asyārthaḥ. na prādeśikī śūnyateti bhāvaḥ. ātmanaḥ prākṛtaṃ rūpaṃ śūnyatājñānāmbunā kṣālayet. samatā sarvavit sthānair iti: samatayā sarvavedanaṃ sarvajñajñānaṃ śūnyatājñānam ity arthaḥ. sthānair iti sthānasya.
śūnyatājñānabhāvanaiva bhāvanāsthānasya rakṣā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ. śūnyatābhāvanāpūrvakarakṣācakrabhāvanayā rakṣā syād iti bhāvaḥ. rañjayed iti: vaśyabhāvanākāle svaparasantānaṃ rañjayed ity arthaḥ. raktayed iti pāṭhe cāyam evārthaḥ. atha vā samatā sarvavid ityāder ayam arthaḥ: śūnyatābhāvanānantaraṃ rakṣācakreṇa vajrapañjarādinā sthānarakṣā kāryeti.
[3.2.6] bhāvayed ityādi: itthaṃ cakre yoginā bhāvite bhayaṃ sarva upadravā dūrasthā bhavantīti bhāvaḥ. abhāvena tu sattvānām iti: sattvānāṃ bhāvānām abhāvaṃ śūnyatāṃ cintayitvā paścād yogabhāvaḥ. etad evāha: yogabhāveti. yogo devatāyogas, tasya bhāvo bhāvanā. katham evam ity āha: bhayena tv iti. rogādibhayena.
rogādibhayāc chāntikādisiddhyarthaṃ tadanuguṇavarṇādiyuktadevatāyogaḥ kārya iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.7] devatāyogam āha: candretyādi. padmopari candramaṇḍale tārārūpam ātmānaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. śūnyatābhāvanānantaraṃ padmopari candrabimbe tāṃbījapariṇatatadadhiṣṭhitavaraṭakotpalapariṇatāṃ tārāṃ bhāvayed iti samudāyārthaḥ.
[3.2.8] utpattyanantaraṃ ṣaḍaṅganyāsam āha: oṃ ityādi. oṃ tāre svāheti ...
[3.2.9] ... śirasi.
[3.2.10] oṃ tuttāre svāheti ...
[3.2.11] ... cakṣuṣoḥ.
[3.2.12] oṃ ture svāheti ...
[3.2.13] ... nāsāpuṭadvaye.
[3.2.14] oṃ tu svāheti ...
[3.2.15] ... karṇayoḥ.
[3.2.16] oṃ re svāheti ...
[3.2.17] ... jihvāyām.
[3.2.18] oṃ tāriṇi svāheti ...
[3.2.19] ... hṛdaye.
[3.2.20] idānīṃ bhujādiniyamam āha: dvibhujam ityādi. rakṣāyāṃ śyāmaiva bhagavatī, mantraś ca śyāmaḥ.
[3.2.21] dakṣiṇena kareṇābhayapradā, vāmena gṛhītanālaṃ karṇāsaktadalam utpalaṃ dhārayantīṃ bhāvayet. aṃśunā sarva iti: svaśarīraraśminā rakṣāprastāve śyāmena cakraraśminā ca vyāpyamānaśarīraṃ sādhyaṃ cintayet. mantraraśminā ca tathābhūtena vyāpyamānaṃ sādhyaṃ cintayan mantraṃ japed ity āha: mantrajāpa viśeṣata iti.
[3.2.22] oṃ tāre tuttāre ture devadattaṃ rakṣa svāheti japaḥ.
[3.2.23] iti rakṣākarmikamantra iti: rakṣā bhaviṣyadbandhanādibhyaḥ, śāntis tu vartamānabandhanādipraśamaḥ. śeṣaṃ sustham. pūrvokte tu cakre mantranyāso vāmāvartena, nemyāṃ punar akārādayaḥ svarāḥ †...†.
[3.2.24–25] mandaprajñāya medhāvīti: mandabuddhir medhāvī bhavaty etaccakrabhāvanāmantrajapābhyām. rakṣā likhita dhārayed iti: bhūrjādau likhitasyāṣṭāracakrasya dhāraṇād rakṣā syāt.
[3.2.26] etad eva nirdiśati: devyā ityādi. iti rakṣāya sarveṣāṃ yogatattvāni sādhayed iti: sarveṣāṃ rakṣārthaṃ yogatattvāni devatāyogaṃ cakraṃ ca sādhayed itīty uktakrameṇa. tārāyogī rakṣārthaṃ cakraṃ mantraṃ ca bhāvayej japec cety arthaḥ.
[3.2.27] idānīṃ prajñācakram āha: aṣṭau dalam idaṃ cakram iti. aṣṭāraṃ cakram ity arthaḥ. nemi bāhyaṃ tu veṣṭayed iti: bāhye nemyāṃ rekhādvayena veṣṭayed bhāvayed ity arthaḥ. pūrva antādi madhyasyeti: pūrvacakrasyevāsya cakrasya madhya anta ādiś ca varṇau hākārapraṇavāv ity arthaḥ. japa sarvebhi sthāpayed iti:
japākṣarāṇi śeṣāṇi sarveṣv areṣu draṣṭavyāni.
[3.2.28] sitavarṇādi cakrasyeti: sitaṃ cakram iti bhāvaḥ. nemyāṃ svarān bhāvayed ity āha: svaretyādi. pra-jñe-ma-hā-pra-jñe-hūṃ-svā-bījāni sarveṣv areṣu, oṃ-hā-bīje madhye, svarā nemyām iti samudāyārthaḥ. ihāpi śūnyatābhāvanāpūrvakaṃ devatāyogī cakraṃ bhāvayan vakṣyamāṇānuśaṃso bhavatīti darśayati: samatetyādi.
[3.2.29] pūrvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktam iti: pūrvaṃ yad uktam ātmapīṭhatṛtīyapaṭale rūpaṃ śūnyādītyādinā (1.2.3a) granthenendriyaviṣayavijñānāni śūnyānīti tenaiva krameṇātrāpy ātmānaṃ viśodhayed iti bhāvaḥ. pūrva yukteneti: pūrvam eva devatābhāvanātaḥ śūnyatābhāvaneti bhāvaḥ. bhāvayed asyeti: idaṃ cakraṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.2.30] oṃ prajña ityādimantrasyākṣarāṇi cakramadhye oṃ-hā-kārau vāmāvartenāreṣu pra-kārādayaḥ.
[3.2.31–32] prathamaṃ sarvatra bhāvanāyāṃ svāsanasthaṃ svabījaṃ vīkṣya tadraśminā gurubuddhādīn avabhāsya tadbījavinirgataraśmisaṃbhūtena puṣpādinā ca saṃpūjya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ śūnyatāṃ vibhāvya devatāṃ bhāvayed iti sāmānyo yogaḥ. prajñāpāramitābhagavatyā hūṃkāro bījam.
kiṃ ca, hūṃbījajātaṃ sanālapadmasthaṃ prajñāpāramitāpustakaṃ hūṃkārādhiṣṭhitaṃ, tanniṣpannā bhagavatī sitavarṇā vyākhyānamudrayā sthitahastadvayā kaphoṇidvayābhyantarotthitasanālakamaladvayasthitaprajñāpāramitāpustakadvayadhāriṇī. tām eva tadrūpeṇātmānam eva paśyed iti prajñām ātmyeti. prajñāpāramitārūpeṇātmaiveti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.33] asyā bhāvanāyā anuśaṃsām āha: japitam asyetyādi. amuṃ mantraṃ japataḥ prajñā śākheva vardhate. yathā vṛkṣasya śākhā bahuprakārā tathā prajñāpi taṃ mantraṃ japato bhavati. śeṣaṃ subodham. kvacit sākṣeti pāṭhaḥ. tatra sākṣāt pratyakṣata eva prajñāvṛddhiḥ. dṛṣṭapratyayā prajñety arthaḥ.
[3.2.34] prayogāntaraṃ darśayati: navetyādi. aṣṭāraṃ prathamapuṭaṃ, dvādaśāraṃ dvitīyapuṭaṃ, ṣoḍaśāraṃ tṛtīyapuṭam. etad eva navakoṣṭhādi garbhasya garbhapuṭaṃ varaṭakena saha navakoṣṭhaṃ bhavati. madhya dvādaśam eva ca: garbhapuṭabahiḥpuṭayor madhyavartitvād dvādaśāraṃ puṭaṃ madhyaṃ, madhyapuṭabahiḥsthitatvāt ṣoḍaśāraṃ bāhyam iti kathayati.
nimna paryantam iti: ṣoḍaśārasya paryante nemiś caturthī.
[3.2.35] madhye ātmacetasyeti: garbhapuṭasya madhye ātmacittaṃ hūṃkāro jñānaḍākinīsvarūpas tasyādau praṇavaḥ. huta-antimā svāhākāraḥ. oṃ hūṃ svāheti mantro garbhapuṭavaraṭake. aṣṭametyādi: aṣṭānāṃ vajraḍākinīghorīcaṇḍālīvettālīsiṃghīvyāghrījambukyulūkīnāṃ yoginīnām akṣarāṇi bījāny aṣṭau krameṇa suṃ kṣuṃ yuṃ huṃ smyruṃ ymyruṃ hmyruṃ kṣmyruṃ iti.
tatra suṃkārādīni catvāri pūrvādyareṣu vāmāvartena, smryuṃkārādīni dakṣiṇāvartena aiśānyādividigareṣu draṣṭavyāni. bindunādaṃ tu yojayed iti vyākhyātavat.
[3.2.36] dvādaśa svarabījasyeti: dvādaśāre madhyapuṭe dvādaśa svarāḥ. te napuṃsakavarjitāḥ. ata āha: napuṃsakādibhir varjitam iti. ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ iti napuṃsakākṣarāṇi. akārādayaḥ svarāḥ krameṇa pūrvādyareṣu draṣṭavyā iti siddham. yathāvargatṛtīyasyeti: kavargacavargaṭavargā iti vargatṛtīyaśabdārthaḥ, yatheti yasyety arthe.
etena vargatrayeṇa pañcadaśākṣarāṇi. ekam akṣaram adhikam iti: tavargād ekam akṣaram adhikaṃ grāhyaṃ, sa ca takāraḥ. samudāyena ṣoḍaśa bhavanti.
[3.2.37] sthāpayed bāhyasthānaṃ tv iti: bahiḥ ṣoḍaśāre puṭe tāny akṣarāṇi bhāvayed iti. etāni bindunādābhyāṃ ca yojyānīty āha: bindvityādi. ghātākṣara nimnānāṃ veṣṭayet sarva sarvavid iti: ghātākṣaraṃ phaṭkāraḥ. tena nemyāṃ veṣṭayet. ghātākṣaraṃ samagrāyāṃ nemyāṃ veṣṭanena sthitaṃ cintayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.2.38] asya cakrasya varṇam āha: prathamam ityādi. prathamaṃ navakoṣṭhaṃ sitam. dvitīyaṃ dvādaśāraṃ pītam. tṛtīyaṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ raktam. caturthī nemī kṛṣṇā. yāvac cakraṃ tāvad vṛddhiṃ bhāvayed ity upadeśaḥ.
[3.2.39] etac cakraṃ nemīcaturthapuṭaṃ Jñānaḍākinīmūrtyā bhāvayed iti jñeyam. etad eva sūcayann āha: pūrvalakṣaṇetyādi. bhāvayed iti: devatām iti śeṣaḥ. asya cakrata iti: asya cakrasya bhāvanārthaṃ jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.2.40] samāhitam ityādi: samāhito yogī, bhāvabhāvenātiśayabhāvanayety arthaḥ. jvalitam ityādi: jvalitaṃ sac cakraṃ sarvaprakāreṇāvidyādīndhanaṃ dahati vahnivad ity arthaḥ. dahyanti sarvamārāṇāṃ jñānacakrasya bhāvanā |
[3.2.41] asya jñānacakrasya bhāvanātaḥ sarve mārā dahyanti naśyanti. kleśāvṛtānām api cakram idaṃ bhāvayatāṃ buddhatvaphalaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.42] bhāvanāntaram āha: samatājñānetyādi. ātmānaṃ khasamaṃ vibhāvya Jñānaḍākinīṃ bhāvayan vakṣyamāṇaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. tad evāha: sūryetyādi. hṛdi sūryamaṇḍalaṃ dṛṣṭvā ...
[3.2.43] ... tatra bījaṃ bhāvayet. bījam evāha: yathetyādi. vargasya prathamasya pūrvam akṣaraṃ kakāraḥ.
[3.2.44] aṅkuśena tu pīḍasyeti: aṅkuśa ikāraḥ. sa copari. mūla rephaṃ tv iti: adho rephaṃ dadyāt. amṛtāmṛtam. candra ity arthaḥ. ūrdhvaṃ ca binduḥ. tataḥ kriṃ iti bījaṃ syāt.
[3.2.45] evaṃbhūtaṃ bījaṃ sūryaprabhākulaṃ bhāvayed iti. rājā prasiddhaḥ. rājapuro iti: rājapuram. nagaram ity arthaḥ. svahṛdi sūrye kriṃbījaṃ vīkṣya tadraśmibhir ātmānaṃ rañjayitvā paraṃ ca sādhyaṃ rājādikaṃ rañjayati. tato rājā sapura eva vaśībhavatīti bhāvaḥ. manyaketi: yogī.
[3.2.46] idānīṃ nārīvaśīkaraṇam āha: dviguṇetyādi. dviguṇākṣaraṃ kṣakāraḥ. nāda ukāraḥ. yady api dīrgho 'pi nāda ity ucyate, atropadeśād dhrasva eva. tena kṣuṃ iti syāt. yathā hrasvo dīrgho 'pīkāro 'ṅkuśa iti. prastāvena dvayor eka upadeśād gṛhyate. amṛtā-antikānāmeti: amṛtasyānto nāśas tena vyavaharatīty amṛtāntikaḥ kṣuṃkāraḥ, asyāgnisvarūpatvāt.
anye punar evaṃ vyācakṣate: amṛtā-nāmā svāhākāraḥ. sa cāntiko 'vasāne yojya ity arthaḥ. kiṃ cānyathāpi praṇavasvāhākārāv upadeśād draṣṭavyau. tata oṃ kṣuṃ svāheti mantraḥ. raktavarṇajñānaḍākinīyogināṃ lakṣaṃ mantram amuṃ japtvā pūrvasevāṃ kṛtavatāṃ vaśyaṃ bhavati. ata āha: vaśyetyādi.
paścān nāmagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ yadarthaṃ raktavarṇajñānaḍākinīyogī japati, taṃ niyataṃ vaśīkaroti. oṃ kṣuṃ devadattaṃ me vaśam ānaya svāheti vidarbhaṇaṃ sādhyasādhanakāle.
[3.2.47] punar apīdānīṃ kriṃkārasya prayogāntaram āha: yaś cintayatītyādi. yo nārīṇāṃ dharmodayāyāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ hṛdaye kriṃkāraṃ bhāvayati, sa nārīpuruṣān vaśayati. pūrvasevāyāṃ kriṃkāro 'pi devatāyoginā lakṣaṃ japyaḥ.
[3.2.48] prayogāntaram āha: pūrvalakṣaṇetyādi. śūnyatānantaraṃ pītajñānaḍākinīyogī pītacandramaṇḍale vakṣyamāṇaṃ bhāvayed iti śeṣaḥ. bhāvanām āha: amṛtā sakāraḥ. sarvavinmātrair iti: sarvavinmātro praṇavaḥ. ubhayayogena soṃ iti syāt. raśmijvālām anekaśa iti: soṃkāro raśmisamākulaḥ. ayam api pūrvasevāyāṃ lakṣaṃ japyaḥ.
jñānaḍākinīyogī strīṇāṃ nābher adhaś candrasthaṃ raktasoṃkāraṃ vīkṣya, yonau ca kriṃkāraṃ bhāvayan drāvayati.
[3.2.49] dravyākṛṣṭim āha: varṇapītetyādi. varṇaśabdena rakto varṇaḥ, pīteti pīto varṇaḥ, dravyākṛṣṭāv iti draṣṭavyam. etad evāha: śvāsākarṣitetyādi. etenaitad uktaṃ bhavati: svahṛccandramaṇḍalopari pītasoṃkāraṃ dhyātvā ratnādau cākraṣṭavyadravye [rakta?]soṃkāraṃ vīkṣya svaśvāsena svahṛccandrasthasoṃkāraraśminā ca ratnādikam ākarṣayatīti.
[3.2.50] prayogāntaram āha: sādhakasya tv ityādi. sādhakaśabdena sādhya eva. dehānām iti hṛdaye. sādhyahṛdaye sūryasthaṃ kriṃkāraṃ raktavarṇaṃ cittasvarūpaṃ yuṃkāraveṣṭitaṃ cintayet.
[3.2.51] candramaṇḍalopari raktavarṇaṃ jñānaḍākinīrūpam ...
[3.2.52–53] ... aṅkuśagrāhivāmakaraṃ sūryasthakriṃkārāgradakṣiṇatarjanīkam ātmānaṃ cintayitvā, sādhyasya galam aṅkuśena viddhvā sādhyahṛdgataṃ kriṃkāraṃ kriṃkārayuktena dakṣiṇatarjanyagreṇa cālyamānaṃ cintayan kṣuṃbījaṃ japet. oṃ kṣuṃ devadattasya mana ākarṣaya svāheti japaḥ. dviguṇetyādi vyākhyātam eva.
[3.2.54] vaśa lakṣeṇeti pūrvasevāyāṃ, sādhyasādhanakāle tv aniyamaḥ. ebhir ityādi: kṣuṃkāreṇa japtena kriṃkāreṇa bhāvitena kriṃkārasahitena tarjanyagreṇa manaś cālayet. itthaṃ sādhyasya manaś calati. ākṛṣṭaṃ syād ity arthaḥ. lakṣam ekena sidhyatīti: pūrvasevāyāṃ lakṣajapo 'yaṃ kriṃkāraḥ. kṣuṃkārasya vaśa lakṣeṇeti pūrvasevoktā.
[3.2.55] idānīṃ puṣṭim adhikṛtyāha: aṣṭadalasyetyādi. aṣṭāraṃ cakraṃ sanemikaṃ dhyātvā nemyāṃ svarā draṣṭavyāḥ. ata āha: nimnakā svaretyādi.
[3.2.56] yathāvargasya pañcasyeti: pañcamavargasya bakāro grāhyaḥ, tribhir akṣara gṛhyata iti (57d) vakṣyamāṇavacanāt. sa ikārarephābhyāṃ yuktaḥ. upadesād bindunāpi. tataś ca briṃ iti rūpaṃ syāt. sa cakramadhye draṣṭavyaḥ. asya bījasyeti: idaṃ bījam. draṣṭavyam iti śeṣaḥ. kenety āha: jñāninām iti. jñāninety arthaḥ.
[3.2.57] saptanādasyetyādi: saptamo nāda ṛkāraḥ, tena yuktaḥ sa eva bakāraḥ. pañcamasya tu vargasya tṛbhir akṣara gṛhyata iti samatvāt. (?) upadeśād bindunāpi yojyaḥ. tena bṛṃ iti syāt. dalasevam iti: daleṣv areṣu sevā yasya sa dalasevo bṛṃkāraḥ. taṃ dalasevaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. areṣu bṛṃkāro draṣṭavya ity arthaḥ.
[3.2.58] dalasevam ity (57b) asya nirdeśam āha: dale atraivetyādi. saptamanāda ekāra iti kecit. breṃ iti syāt. bhāvayed ityādi: pītavarṇaṃ cakraṃ hṛdi bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.59] kathaṃ bhāvayed ity āha: pūrvoktair ityādi. pūrvoktāṃ śūnyatāṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ. paścāc candramaṇḍale briṃkārapariṇatatadadhiṣṭhitadhānyamañjarīniṣpannaṃ pītam ...
[3.2.60] ... abhayavāmakaraṃ dhānyamañjarīdharadakṣiṇakaraṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaṃ ratnamakuṭaṃ vajrasattvādhiṣṭhitamurdhānaṃ vasudhārārūpam ātmānaṃ vibhāvyātmahṛdi prāguktaṃ cakraṃ bhāvayet. bhāvanākhinno mantraṃ japed ity āha: madhya uktetyādi. oṃ briṃ svāheti japyo mantraḥ. madhye cakramadhye yad uktaṃ bījaṃ tad briṃkāra eva. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.2.61–62] aparaṃ prayogam āha: aparo bhedam ātmānam ityādi. ātmano bhedam aparaṃ vakṣya ity arthaḥ. sarvasaṃsthānam eva ceti: pūrvoktasaṃsthānaṃ cakraṃ sāṣṭāratvāt. pūrvetyādinā śūnyatādipūrvakaJñānaḍākinībhāvanoktā.
[3.2.63] tataś ca jñānaḍākinīyogī hṛdaye padmopari sūryacandropari sāṣṭāracakraṃ tac ca jñānavijñānena hūṃkāreṇa bhāvanāṃ bhāvitaṃ ceṣṭitam ity arthaḥ.
[3.2.64] madhye bījam iti: cakramadhye hūṃkāraṃ bhāvayet. punar nirdiśann āha: veṣṭitam iti. nemyāṃ svarāḥ.
[3.2.65] diśe amṛta catvāra iti: digareṣu catvāry amṛtāni a ā ā3 aṃ ity etāni bījāni vāmāvartena pūrvādiṣu nyasanīyāni. vidigareṣu pañcabuddhābhimaṇḍito hūṃkāraḥ.
[3.2.66] cintayed ātmadehaṃ tv iti: svahṛdaye cakram idaṃ candrasthaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. amṛtebhyo nisṛtya raśmayo daśa diśo gatvā hastyādisaptaratnayuktaṃ jagat kṛtvā ...
[3.2.67] ... punar ātmamanasi svecchayā viśanto dhyeyā iti. bhāvayed iti: atiśayena bhāvayan yogī niṣkampo nirupadravo bhavati.
[3.2.68] durmedhā dūravartitam iti: yad uktaṃ durmedhādikaṃ tena dūrād eva varjitaḥ syāc cakraṃ bhāvayan yogī.
[3.2.69] yadāsamāhito yogī bhāvanāśaktas tadā sitaṃ cakrād reṇuṃ daśasu dikṣu gatvā ...
[3.2.70] ... punar āgatya svacetasi viśantaṃ bhāvayet. tena paracittaṃ jānāti: jñānacetādītyādi. atyarthaṃ cakram idaṃ bhāvayan saṃsārān mokṣaṃ prāpnotīty āha: samāhitaṃ cakra ityādi.
[3.2.71] yogāntaram āha: candretyādi. padmacandropari jñānabījena nirmitam iti: svacittamātram eva bījaṃ tato niṣpannaṃ vairocanaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā: aḥkārabījaṃ jñānabījaṃ, tato niṣpannaṃ pūrvavat.
[3.2.72] pāṇidvayena mudrāliṅganābhinayasthitena hṛdisaktena yuktaṃ vairocanaṃ bhāvayed iti.
[3.2.73] sphurantam ityādi: pañca buddhāḥ svaśarīrāt sphuranto dhyeyāḥ sarvalokadhātuṣv iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.2.74] sphurantaṃ vividhatīkṣṇānām iti: vividhā raśmayaḥ svaśarīrāt sphuranto draṣṭavyā iti bhāvaḥ. etadbhāvanayā kiṃ syād ity āha: prajñā vajra jagottareti. abhedyā prajñā jagaduttāraṇasamarthā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ. asya mantroddhāram āha: svaretyādi. svarapūrvādibījam akāraḥ. sa ca visargeṇa bindudvayena yoktavyaḥ.
[3.2.75] praṇametyādi: oṃ aḥ svāheti mantraḥ. asya bījaṃ tv iti: asmād bījād bhagavato niṣpattir iti bhāvaḥ. samāhito yogī japaṃ bhāvanāṃ ca kurvan sidhyatīti. abhicāras tu ṣoḍaśāracakram eva kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ bhāvayatā sādhyaḥ.
[3.2.col.] ity uktakrameṇa rakṣāprajñādikaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaṃ yogapīṭhe. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe yogapīṭhe dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ.
