[3.3.1] idānīm āveśādikapaṭalam āha: śrutam ityādi. he deva, śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama. kiṃ tad ity āha: adhiṣṭhānakrama kīdṛśam iti. adhiṣṭhānakramo 'dhiṣṭhānavidhānam āveśavidhir ity arthaḥ. adhiṣṭhānakramaḥ kīdṛśa iti bhāvaḥ. ko 'sāv ity āha: yenetyādi. yenādhiṣṭhānakrameṇa darśitena sattvānāṃ vineyānām āveśo bhavati. katham ity āha:
yogabhāvata iti. āveśayogabhāvanayā yogavihitayety arthaḥ. taṃ bhagavan kathayeti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.2] yathātattvam ity aviparītam. samyak saṃsāra-m-uttaram iti: saṃsārād uttārayatīti saṃsārottāram āveśavidhiṃ śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. cikitsā mohitasattvānām iti: mohasāgarapatitānām āveśāc cikitsā kleśacikitsā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ. āveśāc chraddhotpādyate. śraddhayā devatāṃ bhāvayatāṃ cikitsā syād ity ato 'dhiṣṭhānavidhiḥ kartavyaḥ.
adhiṣṭhādharma deśayed iti: adhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇaṃ dharmaṃ darśayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.3] hṛdaya iti svahṛdaye. tatra candramaṇḍale hūṃkāraṃ vīkṣya tadraśmisaṃsparśād viśvaṃ khasamaṃ bhāvayet pūrvalakṣaṇetyādivacanāt (5a). agnītyādi: sādhyahṛdaye 'pi vahnimaṇḍalaṃ rephayuktamadhyaṃ tryaśraṃ dhyātvā ...
[3.3.4] ... tadraśminā sādhyaṃ dagdhaṃ vibhāvayet. sarvasaṃsthānajvālanam iti: sarvasaṃsthānam aṅgapratyaṅgaṃ tad api dagdham iti bhāvaḥ.
jñānaḍākinīyogī svahṛccandrasthahūṃkāraraśmisaṃsparśād viśvaśūnyatāṃ vibhāvya sādhyam api pūrvoktakrameṇa vahnidagdhaṃ bhasmapuñjamātrāvaśeṣaṃ hūṃkārayogāt punar utthāpya, tasya hṛdi vahnimaṇḍalaṃ, tasya madhye padmaṃ, tanmadhye ca sūryaṃ vīkṣya, tatra hūṃkāraṃ pañcabuddhābhidhaṃ cintayed iti.
[3.3.5] pūrvetyādinā śūnyatāṃ smārayati. agnītyādinā cāgnimaṇḍalamātram. tatra padmaṃ tūpadeśāt.
[3.3.6] hūṃkāraṃ lokeśvaretyādinā pratipādayati.
[3.3.7] punar āha: bhāvayed ityādi. asya bījaṃ: idaṃ hūṃbījam.
[3.3.8] jñānaḍākinībhāvanāpūrvakam āveśaḥ kartavya iti darśayann āha: sūryetyādi. padmopari sūrye hūṃkāras tannirmitaṃ jñānaḍākinīrūpam. ata āha: jñāna-m-ātmeti. jñānātmā hūṃkāras tannirmitaṃ jñānaḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.3.9] etadviśeṣaṇam āha: dvibhujetyādi. kirīṭasthāḥ pañca tathāgatāḥ. kirīṭasya pūrve vairocanaḥ, uttare ratnasaṃbhavaḥ, paścime 'mitābhaḥ, dakṣiṇe 'moghasiddhiḥ, madhye 'kṣobhyaḥ, kirīṭāgre pañcaśūkavajram iti kirīṭītyādinoktam.
[3.3.10] vāme yogapātraṃ, dakṣiṇe cordhvakhaṭvāṅgaṃ bhāvayed iti. śvāsaniścalam iti: sthiraṃ cittaṃ yāvat syāt, tāvad bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.11] mantroddhāram āha: yathetyādi subodham.
[3.3.12] jñānaḍākinīyoginā mantraḥ pūrvasevāyāṃ lakṣatrayaṃ japtavyaḥ. oṃ a svāheti. paścād ityādi: pūrvasevānantaraṃ jñānaḍākinīyogī hṛdaye candra saṃcintyetyādyarthaṃ (3a) vidhāyāmunā mantreṇa sarṣapān abhijapya balidānapūrvakaṃ taiḥ sādhyaṃ hanyāt. tata āviṣṭo bhavati. karmam ākṛtyeti: karmāveśakriyā. ākṛtyeti: kartavyam. adaityeti:
baliṃ darśayati.
[3.3.13] aparaṃ prayogam āha: pañcetyādi. madanam ity adhyāhāryam upadeśāt. pañcāmṛtayuktaṃ madanaṃ tenaiva mantreṇāṣṭottaraśatajaptaṃ pūrvoktavidhisaṃpādanapūrvakaṃ sādhyāya dadyāt. tatpānād āviṣṭaḥ syāt. śatam aṣṭam iti: aṣṭottaraṃ śatam. vidhineti: pūrvoktaṃ sarvaṃ vidhānaṃ kṛtvā madanaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ. sa āviṣṭaḥ svayaṃ vajragītaṃ gāyet.
[3.3.14] gītam āha: rama rametyādi. ramasva ramasvety arthaḥ. harṣe dviruktiḥ. ho saṃbodhane. vajjeti: saṃbodhanaṃ vajradharasya. sarvair dharmaiḥ samasthaiḥ pariśuddhai ramasva ramasveti saṃbandhaḥ. khaddhu tuhuṃ: tvaṃ khāda. kiṃ tad ity āha: dīaṇḍa dāaṇḍa iti. dattam apuṇyaṃ dāpitaṃ cāpuṇyaṃ taddvayam api khādeti bhāvaḥ.
apuṇyasya hi nāśa iṣyate. khāditaṃ ca naśyati. tac ca sarvasattvānām iti draṣṭavyam. jovu tuhuṃ: yogas tava. sa māṃ saṃsārād uttārayatu. kṣaṇād ihaiva janmani buddhatvāvāhakatvāt. tava samāpattyā sarvam idaṃ †bhagavata eva prajñāṅge. bhagavantam āha:† mahuṃ saṃsāru uttāru khaṇu padasyārthaḥ.
[3.3.15] anena gītena vajraghaṇṭābhinadanād āviṣṭo nṛtyati.
[3.3.16] tata oṃ tiṣṭhetyādimantreṇa taṃ svasthīkuryāt.
[3.3.17] paścāt saṃtarpaṇārthaṃ taṃ madanaṃ pāyayet. etad evāha: prīṇayed iti. etat sarvam andhapaṭaṃ baddhvā maṇḍalasamīpaṃ nītvā sādhyaṃ samanuṣṭheyam ity ata āha: sarveṣām iti. svasthatānantaraṃ tasmai yoginīcakraṃ darśayed ity āha: yoginī sarva darśayed iti. andhapaṭam udghāṭya darśayed ity arthaḥ.
tadanu puṣpapātaṃ jñātvā mṛdumadhyādhimātrakrameṇa dharmadeśanāṃ kuryāt. etad evāha: tatra dharmāṇi kartavyaṃ hīna madhyama uccakair iti. etac ca sekavidhau vistarato jñeyam.
[3.3.18] idānīṃ mantrapāṭhamātreṇāveśaṃ darśayati: oṃ yogāveśa aḥ ityādi.
[3.3.19] imaṃ mantraṃ pūrvasevārthaṃ lakṣaṃ jñānaḍākinīyogī japtvāveśayet. etad evāha: samāhitam ityādi. āveśam āha: tena pūrvasevāsiddhena mantreṇa sarṣapān abhimantrya balidānapūrvakaṃ sādhyaṃ hanyāt. tata āviṣṭaḥ syāt. ata āha: sarṣapena tv ityādi.
[3.3.20] hūṃkāranādabhīṣaṇam iti: hūṃkāram uccārayan pūrvamantrābhimantritaiḥ sarṣapais tāḍayed iti bhāvaḥ. asya svasthīkaraṇārthaṃ pūrvokto mantra ity āha: asyetyādi. sa ca oṃ tiṣṭhetyādi (16).
[3.3.21] idānīṃ dṛṣṭyāveśam āha: oṃ maṇetyādi. oṃ maṇa ghuru ghuru dṛṣṭi cili 2 hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaḍ iti mantraṃ ...
[3.3.22] ... jñānaḍākinīyogī lakṣaṃ japtvā paścāt sakṛd uccārayan yaṃ paśyati, sa tatkṣaṇād āviṣṭo bhaved ity āha: lakṣam ekam ityādi. dṛṣṭiveśam iti: dṛṣṭyāveśaḥ. yaṃ yaṃ paśyati pūjānām iti: maṇḍalakhādanapānādikāle yaṃ paśyati, sa āviṣṭaḥ san nṛtyati. svasthīkaraṇārthaṃ pūrvoktasvasthīkaraṇamantraḥ (16).
[3.3.23] prayogāntaram āha: oṃ vettālītyādi.
[3.3.24] ayam api jñānaḍākinīyoginā lakṣaṃ pūrvasevārthaṃ japtavyaḥ. tatas tena mantreṇa bhasma sapta vārān abhimantrya tena bhasmanā śavo 'nyad vā kiṃcid yadi spṛśyate, tadā tac chavādikaṃ sapta dināni yāvat na kenāpi bhakṣyata iti. etad evāha: sapta vārān ityādi. śava iti prasiddhaḥ.
[3.3.25] prayogāntaram āha: oṃ phuru 2 matte jaḥ hūṃ iti mantraḥ pūrvavat pūrvasevāyāṃ japtavyaḥ.
[3.3.26] anena mantreṇa raktasūtram aṣṭaśatajaptaṃ yasya gale baddhyate, sa śṛgālo bhavatīty āha: raktasūtretyādi.
[3.3.27] yadā punaḥ sūtrāpagamaḥ syāt, tadā svaṃ rūpaṃ dhārayatīty āha: sūtrāvarjitetyādi. pūrvokto mantro jñānaḍākinīyoginā samāhitena yadi japyate, tadāveśo 'pi sādhyata ity āha: samāhitam ityādi. abhimantritasarṣapatāḍanena, mantroccāreṇa, darśanena vā, āveśo 'pi syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.28] idānīṃ karmāntaram āha: candramaṇḍaletyādi. śūnyatābhāvanādipūrvakaṃ siṃhāsanopari candramaṇḍalopari svabījaniṣpannam uktarūpam ātmānaṃ vajraḍākinīrūpaṃ vibhāvya, ...
[3.3.29] ... paścāt sādhyaṃ vāyumaṇḍalasthaṃ purato dhyātvā tatpādatale vāyubījadvayaṃ tad eva pādayor āsanaṃ pādapīṭham ity arthaḥ.
[3.3.30] hṛdaye vāyuveṣṭasyeti: sādhyahṛdaye vāyumaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ vāyubījam. madhyata iti: tasya sādhyasya ceto 'pi bījamadhyagatam iti bhāvaḥ. ata evāha: bindu nādaṃ tu saṃyuktam iti. binduś cittam. nādaḥ śabdaḥ. sādhyacittaṃ bījamadhyagatam. nādo mantroccāraṇam iti siddham. sādhyaṃ nīlaṃ cintayed ity āha: nīletyādi. ātmaśvāsasyetyādi:
tathābhūtaṃ sādhyaṃ caladrūpaṃ ca nirūpya nijaśvāsāgre vāyubījaṃ dhyātvā, tena śvāsavāyunā sādhyaṃ preryamāṇaṃ dhyāyāt. etad evāha: †...† śaravāyuvegeneva gacchantaṃ taṃ dhyāyād ity arthaḥ.
[3.3.31] tathābhūto mantraṃ ca sāṣṭaśataṃ japet (32): oṃ yuṃ prera vāyuṃ amukasya yuṃ phaṭ.
[3.3.32] jñānaḍākinīyogī pūrvasevāyāṃ lakṣam imaṃ mantraṃ japet. tata uccāṭayati.
[3.3.33] idānīṃ likhanoccāṭanam āha: bhūrjetyādi. ādiśabdād avadhūtādikarpaṭam. rudhirakair iti: tarjanīrudhiraṃ, rājikā lavaṇaṃ ca. ebhir bhūrjādau pūrvoktaṃ savidarbhaṇaṃ mantraṃ likhet. kathaṃ likhed ity āha: agre dhāritayogasyeti. pūrvoktavajraḍākinīyogenety arthaḥ. sarvasaṃsthitam iti: sādhyam api pūrvavad dhyātvā likhed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.34] paścād anena mantreṇa raktasūtram aṣṭaśatajaptaṃ kṛtvā tena tatpatraṃ baddhvā, taru uccādi m-ālambyeti: uccataror agre 'valambayed iti bhāvaḥ. agre 'pi kutrety āha: vātāpīḍyasya bhāvanam iti. yad agraṃ vātena pīḍyate tasminn agre tad avalambayed ity arthaḥ.
sūtrāvalambanānantaram api pūrvavad vāyunā preryamāṇaṃ sādhyaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvanam iti padasyārthaḥ.
[3.3.35] yaṃ prerayatītyādi: yasyāṃ diśi tat patraṃ vātena preryate, tasyāṃ sādhyo 'pi gamanotsuko gacchaty evety arthaḥ. uccāṭanaṃ tu yogīnām iti: devatāyogena japtamantrasyoccāṭanaṃ sidhyati niyameneti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.36] idānīm ākṛṣṭiprayogāntaram āha: candretyādi. pūrvoktaśūnyatādikrameṇa candrasthaṃ vajraḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ vicintya balidānapuraḥsaraṃ pūrvasevāyāṃ kṛtalakṣamantrajapo yogī, tasya madhye tu yuñjitam iti: raktagandhena gocarmapramāṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kāryaṃ raktamaṇḍala kārata iti (38b) vakṣyamāṇavacanāt, tasya madhya iti boddhavyam.
tatra sādhyanāma yoktavyaṃ madhye nāmaṃ tu m-ālikhed iti (38d) vakṣyamāṇavacanāt.
[3.3.37] yathā: oṃ Vvajraḍākini diśam ākarṣayākarṣaya oṃ hrīḥ hūṃ phreṃ phruṃ phuḥ devadattam ākarṣaya svāheti vidarbhaṇam.
[3.3.38] khadiretyādi: tasya gocarmapramāṇasya raktagandhamaṇḍalasyāṣṭasu dikṣu khādirā aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇā ekaśūkavajrākārā aṣṭau kīlakā nikhātavyāḥ. kārata iti maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ. paripūrṇabalisaṃyuktam iti: sarvam idaṃ balidānapūrvakaṃ kartavyam iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.39] raktasūtraṃ tv iti: teṣāṃ kīlakānām agre raktasūtreṇāṣṭaśatābhimantritena sapta veṣṭanāni kuryāt. etad evāha: prākāra sapteti. prākāraśabdena veṣṭanaṃ, veṣṭayed iti kuryāt. vajrāṅkuśena m-ākṛṣyeti:
vajrāṅkuśapāśahastaṃ vajraḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ cintayitvā pāśena galake baddhvāṅkuśena hṛdaye viddhvā samākṛṣyamāṇaṃ sādhyaṃ paśyan mantraṃ japet. etad evāha: mantraṃ tu jāpitam iti.
[3.3.40] prayogamāhātmyam āha: śatetyādi. śatayojanānantarito 'py ākṛṣyate yogenāneneti bhāvaḥ. maṇḍaletyādinā prayogopasaṃhāra uktaḥ.
[3.3.41] idānīṃ stambhanam āha: sūryamaṇḍaletyādi. siṃhāsanopari sūryamaṇḍalasthaṃ śūnyatādikrameṇa ghoraḍākinīrūpam ātmānaṃ vibhāvya vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryāt.
[3.3.42] tad evāha: matsyam ekādi saṃgṛhyetyādi. baliṃ dattvā bhūrjapatre oṃ phruṃ phruṃ devadattasya mukhaṃ bandha bandha hrī hrī hrī phu phaḍ iti (43) mantraṃ vilikhya balālamatsyamukhe prakṣipya, tasya matsyasya śiro vāmapādenākramyāmum eva mantraṃ tathaivāṣṭaśataṃ japet. tato mukhastambhanaṃ syāt.
[3.3.43–44] sarvakāryāṇi stambhitam iti: vidarbhaṇāt kāyacetasor api stambhanaṃ syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.45] aparaṃ stambhanam āha: aparaivetyādi. asya mantrādiyuktasyeti: imam eva mantram ittham eva vidarbhya bhūrjādau vilikhya kaṇikapiṣṭakena saṃveṣṭya jalādhāre mīnebhyo dadyāt.
[3.3.46] kutrety āha: yasyetyādi. yatra hrade cīyamānaṃ vastu gilanto mīnā dṛśyante, tatra gatvā sakṛt sakṛn mantram uccārya mīnebhyo dṛśyamānebhyo 'ṣṭaśatagulikā mantrasahitamadhyāḥ kaṇikasaṃpāditā dadyād iti bhāvaḥ. yadā tā gulikā matsyā gilanti tadā kāyādistambhanaṃ syāt.
[3.3.47] bhagastambhanam āha: vāyv ityādi. pūrvoktanyāyena caṇḍālīrūpam ātmānam āsthāya vāyumaṇḍalamadhyastho vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryāt.
[3.3.48] tad evāha: tasya madhye tu kārayed iti. gomayena padmaṃ dharmodayākāraṃ saṃpādya tasya dharmodayākārasya madhye kārayed iti saṃbandhaḥ. kiṃ tad ity āha: nāma mantraṃ tv iti.
[3.3.49] oṃ stambha stambhaya bhagaṃ mūtraṃ phuṃ phu devadattāyāḥ phaḍ iti mantraṃ nāmavidarbhaṇasahitaṃ bhūrjādau likhitaṃ tasya dharmodayākārasya madhye sthāpayed iti kārayed ity (48b) asya nirdeśaḥ.
[3.3.50] asya mantrasya nāmasyeti: imaṃ mantraṃ gomayadharmodayāntargataṃ nāmavidarbhitaṃ kṛtvā, vāmapādenākramya tam eva mantraṃ tathaivāṣṭaśatasaṃkhyaṃ japet. anena mūtrastambhaḥ syāt.
[3.3.51] duṣṭapatnīdamanam etat. yadā śāntyarthī syāt, tadā kṣīraṃ śāntena cittena pravāhayet. tadā niyataṃ mūtrayati nānyathā. atha vā taṃ mantraṃ kṣīreṇa prakṣālayet. anenāpi śāntiḥ.
[3.3.52] aparaṃ prayogam āha: vāyv ityādi. vāyumaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ: pūrvavad vajrīrūpaṃ sakalaviśeṣaṇānvitam ātmānaṃ vicintya balidānapuraḥsaraṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.53] pretam āsanam āsīnam iti: śavāsanam.
[3.3.54] piṣṭakānām ityādi: piṣṭakena dharmodayākāraṃ niṣpādya ...
[3.3.55] ... kukkuraromabhiḥ pūrayitvā tathaiva vakṣyamāṇaṃ mantraṃ vidarbhaṇayutaṃ tatra prakṣipet. pūrvavan mantraṃ japet. sarvatra pūrvasevā lakṣajāpāt.
[3.3.56] oṃ krodheśvari phuḥ phiṃ devadattāyā bhagaṃ dūṣaya phaḍ iti vidarbhaṇam.
[3.3.57] sādhanakāle sāṣṭaśato japaḥ. vajra guhyābhi nikṣiped iti: abhedyo 'yaṃ mantra ity arthaḥ. asmin karmaṇi kṛte bhagavivartaḥ pīḍā ca mahatī syāt.
[3.3.58] uddhṛte tatra mokṣaḥ.
[3.3.59] nāḍīdrāvaṇaprayogāntaram āha: candretyādi. sādhyāyāḥ kukṣau candropari amṛtākṣaraṃ suṃkāraṃ bhāvayet. kiṃbhūtam ity āha: †...† nijaraśminā coditāt sarvāṅgasthanāḍīcakrād amṛtaṃ śukraṃ srāvayantaṃ suṃkāraṃ bhāvayed ity arthaḥ. amṛtāmbuṣa iti śukram.
[3.3.60] tataḥ kiṃ kuryād ity āha: vikasad ityādi. vikasadbandhūkakusumākāram aṅkuśākṣaraṃ kriṃkāraṃ madhye ceti bhagamadhye gūḍha gūḍhaṃ tv iti khagamukhānāḍīmukhagataṃ cintayet. gūḍha khagamukhānāḍī. tasyāṃ gūḍhaṃ kriṃkāraṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.3.61] tataḥ kiṃ kuryād ity āha: krodhetyādi. krodhāṅguṣṭhasya tarjanyā agre suṃkāraṃ vicintya tayā khagamukhāṃ cālayan bhāvayet. tasya maṅguṣṭham iti: tāṃ khagamukhāṃ cālayed iti bhāvaḥ. piṇḍārthaṃ darśayann āha: tribhir yogaṃ tu cetaneti. triṣu sthāneṣu yogam akṣaranyāsaṃ cetaneti cintayed iti bhāvaḥ. kukṣibhagatarjanyagrāṇi trīṇi sthānāni.
[3.3.62] sravanta iti: sarvāvayavasthitaṃ śukraṃ sravantīṃ bhagam iti bhagavatīṃ nārīm ity arthaḥ. cintayed iti śeṣaḥ. ambuṣa iti śukram. atyantaduṣṭastrībhagākoṭanam.
[3.3.63] bhaiṣajyonmattīkaraṇam āha: jyotiketyādi. jyotikā jyotiṣmatī tailam. gurava iti guñjāphalam. unmattapañca-m-aṅgikam iti: dhuttūrakasya pañcāṅgāni, patrapuṣpaphalamūlavalkalāni. mahāgugguluṃ naramastiṣkam. naratailaṃ narājyam.
[3.3.64] amṛtodakaṃ madyam. jyotikādivastu madye prakṣipya tan madyaṃ yaḥ pibati sa unmatto bhavati. ata āha: jihvendriyaṃ tu svādayed iti. gurubuddhādyapakāriṇe tat kartavyam. ghṛtapānān nivartate.
[3.3.65] añjanam āha: apatitetyādi. yāvat skandhān nāvatāryate bhūmau śavas tāvad eva tasya vastram ādāya vartiḥ kāryā. nāgendraṃ śīśakaṃ tasya mallikā.
[3.3.66] balisthānaṃ śmasānam. ḍākinīhṛdayaṃ jñānaḍākinīmantram.
[3.3.67] śmasānabalikā: āmamatsyādibaliḥ. catvare śmaśāne śmaśāne gatvā kapālatrayopari śīśakamallikāyāṃ naratailena prāguktavartim (65b) ādāya dīpaṃ prajvālya kapālam aparaṃ tadupari dattvā, jñānaḍākinīmantram aṣṭaśatam āvartayan (66d) balidānapuraḥsaraṃ kajjalaṃ pātayet. baliś ca deyaḥ śmaśāne, ekavṛkṣe vā. tenāñjitanayanaḥ sarvā dākinīḥ paśyati.
[3.3.68] kalikaraṇam āha: ucchiṣṭetyādi. ucchiṣṭabhāṇḍadvaye śmaśānāṅgārarājikāviṣarudhirādimasyā kākapakṣalekhanyā ...
[3.3.69] ... prāṇidvayasya nāmasahitaṃ oṃ kirītyādimantraṃ likhitvā ...
[3.3.70] ... krodhacittena tad bhāṇḍadvayaṃ jñānaḍākinīyogī gharṣet. balidānapūrvakam etat kartavyam.
[3.3.71] oṃ kalītyādimantram ...
[3.3.72] ... aṣṭottaraśataṃ japan kaliṃ kārayati. tacchāntim āha: adaityetyādi. balidānapūrvakaṃ jñānaḍākinīyogī śāntacittas tayoḥ svadehanirgataraśmimukhagataiḥ kalaśair amṛtāmbupūrṇair abhiṣekaṃ bhāvayet. tataḥ kaliśāntiḥ syāt.
[3.3.col.] ity uktakrameṇāveśābhakṣyīkaraṇaśṛgālīkaraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itīti prakaraṇaṃ yogapīṭham.
iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe yogapīṭhe tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ.
%
[3.4.1] idānīṃ prajñopāyādinā vajrādiviśuddhiṃ darśayitum āha: kautūhalam ityādi. śrotum āpanneti: śrotuṃ kautūhalam āpanno 'smīti bhāvaḥ. kiṃ tac chrotum ity āha: advayo yogasārata iti. sāram advayayogam ity arthaḥ. saṃjñā advayavākyānāṃ kathaṃ yogaṃ tu m-ārabhed iti:
advayayogasya saṃjñāvākyaṃ saṃjñāvacanaṃ kathaṃ syād iti śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ prāpto 'smi. advayayogaś ca saṃvṛtiparamārthābhedarūpaḥ. taṃ ca yogaṃ katham ārabheteti śrotuṃ kautūhalam āpanno 'smīti saṃbandhaḥ.
[3.4.2] visarjanam āha: śṛṇv ityādi. vajreti vajrapāṇe. samyag aviparītam. kiṃ tad ity āha: advayayogatattvata iti. advayayogatattvam advayayogasvarūpam iti yāvat. tad evāha: vajraghaṇṭāś ca mudrāś ca pratidhāreti tattvata iti. prajñāpāramitānādaviśuddhis tattvam. saṃvṛter abhinnarūpatvaṃ vā paramārthasya. tato vajraghaṇṭā dhārayitavyā.
mudrā prajñā. sā ca ghaṇṭāviśuddhyā dhārayitavyā. bhāvaś cāyam: prajñāpāramitānādaviśuddhyā saṃvṛtyabhinnarūpatvaviśuddhyā vā ghaṇṭā dhāryā. tattvaviśuddhaghaṇṭāviśuddhyā mudrā bahiḥprajñā, antaḥprajñā ca. bahirupāyaprajñayoḥ karuṇāśūnyatālakṣaṇayoś ca saṃvṛtiparamārtharūpayor yogas tatra buddhibhedānavagraha iti.
atha vā vajreti vajram upāyaviśuddhyā, ghaṇṭā ca prajñāviśuddhyā. etayor yogo mudrā. mudaṃ rāto gṛhṇītaḥ prajñopāyāv asyām iti vyutpattyā mudrā prajñopāyayogaḥ. atha ca ghaṇṭāyā upari vajraṃ yat, tat prajñopāyasamāpattiviśuddhyā. anyac ca: tadubhayasamāpattijasukhaviśuddhyā yasya sukhasya sahajam iti vyavasthā.
[3.4.3] etad eva sakalam āha: vajram ityādi. tasyopāyasya bhāvas tattvaṃ, tena vajraṃ, vajraṃ cātmā. prajñāyāḥ parabhūtāyāḥ sakāśād upāyasya śarīram ātmeti. vajratvaṃ cābhedyatvāt. prajñāyāḥ sakāśād abhedasūcanārtham upāyasya vajrasaṃjñeti. tattvetīti tattvam iti kṛtvā vajram iti bhāvaḥ. bahu rajaty anena prajñeti vajram upāyaḥ.
vajraṃ saṃvṛtiḥ, paramārthād abhinnarūpatvenābhedyasādharmyāt. bahavo rajanty aneneti vajram. saṃvriyate paramārtho 'nayeti saṃvṛtir bhāvāṅgaḥ. bhāvāṅga eva vicāryamāṇaḥ paramārthatām upaiti. bhāvāṅgo 'pi prakṛtipariśuddhaḥ paramārthe 'pi. yathoktam:
śūnyataiva hi saṃvṛtir
iti. tataś ca yad vajraṃ yoginā dhriyate, tat prajñādirūpāyā abhedaviśuddhisūcanāya. ghaṇṭā dharmeti devatīti: svasāmānyalakṣaṇadhāraṇād dharmāḥ skandhādayaḥ. kṣaṇikatvādiyogāt paramārthataḥ saṃketato dharmāṇāṃ prakṛtipariśuddhiṃ pratipādayantī dhvanati ghaṇṭā. sā prajñāviśuddhyā dhāryā. ata āha: devatīti. dīvyanty asyāṃ yogina iti devatī prajñā.
dīvyanty asyāṃ sarvadharmā iti. tathatā ca devatī. dharmeti dharmā eva ghaṇṭety arthaḥ. mudrā saṃpuṭayogasyeti: tayor vajraghaṇṭayor upāyaprajñārūpayoḥ saṃvṛtiparamārtharūpayoś ca saṃpuṭayogaḥ samāpattir ekatvaṃ mudrety abhidhīyate. saṃpuṭapaṭayor iva yogaḥ saṃpuṭayogaḥ. etenātyantam abhedaṃ sūcayati. etad evāha: upāyaṃ tasya līyata iti.
tasyāṃ prajñāyām upāyo līyate. śarīreṇa samāpattijena sukhena copāyo 'syāṃ śleṣaṃ gacchati. sahaje hi jñāne saṃvṛtte parātmabhedo na syāt. śūnyatākaruṇābhinnatvāt saṃvṛtiḥ paramārthe līyate. ghaṇṭayā vinā na kevalasya vajrasya dhāraṇaṃ, tatas tasyāṃ vajraṃ līyate tāṃ śliṣyati pratibadhnātīti yāvat.
[3.4.4] advayam idam ittham, asya cādvayatvam iti pratipādyādvayasyaiva devatāyogarūpasaṃvṛtiparamārtharūpasyādau yat kartavyaṃ tad āha: ādau tāvad ityādi. triviśuddham iti: yadā yogī prathamaṃ dhyānam ārabhate, tadā triviśuddhiṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. vajrāñjalītyādi subodham. anye punar anyad vyācakṣate. ādau tāvad idaṃ kathyate:
tāvacchabdaḥ prākārādiviśuddhim apekṣya kathanīyam āha triviśuddhim iti. tribhir mantrapadair viśuddhis triviśuddhiḥ. tatraivaṃ bodhavyam:
[3.4.5] śūnyatāṃ bhāvayitvā oṃ svabhāvetyādimantra uccārayitavyaḥ.
[3.4.6] bījaniṣpanne vajre oṃ vajretyādi.
[3.4.7] devatāyāṃ niṣpannāyāṃ oṃ yogetyādir iti.
[3.4.8] idānīṃ pariśuddhasantānasya yoginaḥ prākārādiviśuddhim āha: †...† sarvatra śūnyatā paramārthaḥ, mantradevatādilakṣaṇā saṃvṛtiḥ. advayam eva kāryavaśān nirmāṇena dvaidham āpadyate. grāhyatvādimuktatvaṃ paramārthaḥ. dānaṃ saṃvṛtiḥ. tadviśuddhaḥ prākāraś ca. kīdṛgdānaviśuddhyā prākāra ity āha: tyāgetyādi.
tyāgasaṃpattir deyadāpakapratigrāhakānupalambhena dānam. tyāgena deyādyanupalambhena dānena saṃpannaṃ cittaṃ yasya sa tathā. sarva iti: arthātyarthāṅgātyaṅgalakṣaṇaṃ sarvaṃ vastv ity arthaḥ. tṛṇādivad iti: ādiśabdāc cuṣacarcitanirmālyādikam. tṛṇādiṣu yathānavagrahas, tathārthādiṣu. tṛṇādyanavagraheṇa dānaṃ yat, tadviśuddhyā prākāra iti śeṣaḥ.
[3.4.9] pañjaraviśuddhim āha: śīletyādi. pañjaram ūrdhvaṃ yat, tac chīlam. śīlam eva kiṃ tad ity āha: abhayam ityādi. abhayaṃ bhītebhyaḥ sarvebhya eva tattvata ity eṣa phalakāṅkṣayā pravṛttatvāt. etena kuśalapravṛttir dānāṅge 'pi sūcitā. akuśalanivṛttiṃ sūcayann āha: ahiṃsetyādi. ahiṃsā janto m-ādīnām iti: ādiśabdād itarākuśalanivṛttiḥ sūcitā.
parārthacaryāṃ sūcayann āha: mā tvaṃ sattvārthapīḍaketi. sattvārthasya pīḍako mā tvaṃ syā iti. akuśalanivṛttikuśalapravṛttiparārthacaryālakṣaṇaṃ trividhaṃ śīlaṃ darśitam.
[3.4.10] kīlakaviśuddhim āha: kṣānti kīlena nimnasyeti. parāpakāramarṣaduḥkhādhivāsanādharmanidhyānalakṣaṇā kṣāntiḥ. tadviśuddhyā kīlakā nimnasya nikhātavyā ity arthaḥ. parapīḍānurodhaketi: kṣāntiviśeṣaṇam. parapīḍā parāpakāraḥ. ādiśabdo 'tra draṣṭavyaḥ. tato duḥkhādhivāsanā labdhā.
parapīḍāduḥkhādhivāsanayor anurodhakaṃ nirākaraṇaṃ yasyāṃ, sā parapīḍānurodhikā kṣāntiḥ. paścād ikārakṣayaḥ. anurodhakaṃ ca dharmanidhyānam. pañjarādikaṃ kimartham ity āha: pañjaram ityādi. dūṣayanti vikṛtaṃ kāyādikaṃ kurvantīti duṣṭāḥ kleśaskandhamṛtyudevaputrāḥ, ta eva mārās teṣām ādicchāraṇaṃ nirākaraṇaṃ tadarthaṃ pañjarādikam.
[3.4.11] pañjarādike cetasā nirmite kathaṃ māranirāsa ity āha: na māra ityādi. na māro nāma kaścid asti. yady evaṃ kathaṃ tarhi māreṇa pīḍā kriyata ity āha: na ca māreṇeti. māreṇa pīḍā ca na kriyata iti bhāvaḥ. pīḍālakṣaṇaṃ mārakāryam anubhūyamānam api nāstīty atisubhāṣitam etad ity āha: ātmacetaṃ tv iti. svacittam idam evety arthaḥ.
pīḍānubhūyata ity api cittavikalpa iti bhāvaḥ. yathā cittena kalpyamānā mārāḥ sidhyanti, tathā pañjarādikaṃ tannirākarttanaṃ kiṃ syād ity etad evāha: bandhavad iti. badhyate bahir nirudhyate 'neneti bandhaḥ pañjarādikam. vad ity apiśabdārthe. yathā mārāḥ svacittena bhāvyamānā anubhūyante, tathā tannirākaraṇaṃ pañjarādikam apy astv iti bhāvaḥ.
tathā cāha:
jattiu païsaï jalehiṃ jalu tattiu samarasa hoi |
dosaguṇāaracittaü veḍhayahi vakkha ṇa ko vi ||
iti. svacittam eva tu yasya dāntaṃ, tasya kuto māra ity etad āha: svacittadamanaṃ yasya kuto māreṇa pīḍyata iti. yataś cittam eva mārān utthāpayati, tatas tad eva damayitavyam. damanaṃ ca kleśāpagamaḥ. kleśaprabhavā itare mārāḥ.
[3.4.12] caturasramaṇḍaladhyānabhūmer viśuddhim āha: samatetyādi. prakṛtiprabhāsvaratayā buddhābuddhasamatā, taccittam eva gandhaḥ. traidhātukasamatācittaviśuddhena gandhena caturasramaṇḍalaṃ dhyānasthānaṃ kāryam iti bhāvaḥ. puṣpaprakaraviśuddhim āha: vāsītyādi. vāsī candanaṃ ca te dve dehe yeṣāṃ, te vāsīca[S 48v ]ndanadehāḥ.
vāsyā yaś chinatti dehaṃ yaś ca candanena lipyati tayor api samatā sthiratarā yeṣāṃ yogināṃ, ...
[3.4.13] ... teṣāṃ kṣamā yā saiva puṣpaprakaram iti bhāvaḥ. dhūpaviśuddhim āha: svabhāvetyādi. svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho 'ham iti samatāviśuddhyā dhūpabhāvaneti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.14] dīpaviśuddhim āha: prajñādīpenetyādi. prajñāviśuddhyā dīpa ity arthaḥ. prajñayā tridhātugatam andhakāraṃ moho hanyate. ālokyeti: ālokaḥ syāt. etad evāha: hanyanta ityādi. āloka patha svecchayeti: pathi sanmārge svecchayāloko bhavatīty arthaḥ. bhāvanārambhe sarvam idam itthaṃ viśuddhaṃ kāryam iti bhāvaḥ.
sādhane ca sarvam idam ekatrīkṛtya darśitam.
[3.4.15] prāguktam abhayaṃ vyācakṣāṇa āha: anāśvāsādim āśvāsya abhayaṃdānacetaneti. cetaneti cittam. balim ityādi: balikāle 'py evaṃvidhā pūjā śreyasīti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.16] bhāvanārthaṃ sthānasaṃpattim āha: manaïtyādi subodham. ayaṃ sāmānyena yogārambha uktaḥ. idānīm advayayogasvarūpaprajñopāyasamāpattijaṃ sahajajñānaṃ yat, tad apy advayayoga ity ucyate. tatpratipattyupāyadvāreṇa vajrasattvayogam āha: vajram ātmetītyādi.
prāgukte sthāne niṣadya, vajrāñjaliṃ hṛdi nidhāya, triśuddhim uccārya, śūnyatāṃ bhāvayitvā, sumerūpari siṃhāsanacandrastham ātmānaṃ hūṃkārajavajrākāreṇa niṣpādya, akārajaghaṇṭākāreṇa prajñāṃ nirmimīta. etad evāha: ghaṇṭā prajñeti nirmitam iti.
[3.4.17] samadṛṣṭiṃ tu m-āsthānam iti: prajñā bhāvyamānā tv ātmano 'grataḥ sthāpayitavyā. etad evāha: āsthānam iti. sthāpayitavyety arthaḥ. pauruṣam ātmakam antaram iti: pauruṣam iti hastamātram. ātmabimbāt purato bhāvyamānāpi hastāntarasthā bhāvayitavyā. hṛdaye hūṃkārabhāvanām iti:
prathamato yoginā śūnyatābhāvanārthaṃ pūjāditriśaraṇagamanādyanantaraṃ candramaṇḍalopari hūṃkārabhāvanā kāryeti. etad evāha: śūnyatetyādi. hṛdi candropari hūṃkāraṃ vicintya tadraśmisaṃsparśāt traidhātukaṃ khasamaṃ nirūpaṇīyam. sarva sarvavid iti: sarvaprakāreṇety arthaḥ.
[3.4.18] śūnyateti (17d) nirdiśann āha: kṣayaṃ kṛtveti. trijāṃ dhātum iti traidhātukam. pūrvetyādinā skandhādiśūnyatāṃ smārayati. śūnyacetaneti śūnyacittam. svaparaṃ jagac chūnyaṃ bhāvyam ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.19] kūṭāgāraṃ darśayann āha: merumūrdhni tv ityādi. merumūrdhni vimānaṃ kūṭāgāraṃ svecchayā kṛtaparimāṇaṃ, yāvadiṣṭaṃ tāvatparimāṇam ity arthaḥ. prāguktaṃ candropari hūṃkārajaṃ vajrabimbaṃ kiṃbhūtam ity āha:
[3.4.20] pūrva uktasya vajrasya aṣṭāviṃśati devateti: pūrvoktaṃ vajram aṣṭāviṃśatidevatāyuktaṃ draṣṭavyam iti bhāvaḥ. hūṃkārabinduniḥsṛtā raśmayo 'pi traidhātukaṃ saṃspṛśya punar atraiva praviṣṭā iti vicintya, tato hūṃkāraniṣpannaṃ vajrabimbaṃ bhāvyam iti darśayann āha: raśmijvālām ityādi. vajrabimbe 'pi hūṃkāro jñeyaḥ.
[3.4.21] vajrasattvādirūpasya tata ātmeti vinirmitam iti: tadvajrabimbapariṇatam ātmānaṃ vajrasattvarūpaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ. varṇabhujādi vakṣyati. aṣṭāṅgakavacanam āha: nyased ityādi. tālucakṣurdvayavaktragrīvāskandhadvayahṛdayanābhiguhyeṣu ...
[3.4.22] ... kundendudhavalo hūṃkāro draṣṭavyaḥ. himakundenduvarṇasyeti: bījasya vajrasattvasya ca viśeṣaṇam. bījaṃ tu śukram eva. vajrasattvaḥ kiṃciddhavalaḥ kiṃcidraktavarṇaḥ, taptakāñcanasamaprabham iti (25b) vakṣyamāṇavacanāt. pañcabuddhābhimaṇḍitam iti: pañcabuddhamakuṭaṃ bhagavantaṃ bhāvayed iti saṃbandhaḥ.
[3.4.23] mahāmudrā hṛdiṃ caiveti: mudrāliṅganābhinayena hṛdinyastavajravajraghaṇṭādharaṃ hastadvayaṃ bhagavantam bhāvayet. kiṃbhūtam ity āha: paramaṃ padam iti. paramaṃ svārthasaṃpattyā, padaṃ parārthasaṃpattyāśrayabhūtatvāt, spharatyaṃ ca tathāgatam. etad evāha: sphurantam ityādi.
[3.4.24] bhāvayed bhāvabhāveneti: tam evaṃvidhaṃ bhagavantaṃ bhāvayet. bhāvabhāvenātiśayena. hūṃkāroccāraṇātmakam atiśayena bhāvayed ity āha: hūṃkāretyādi. yadīcchati japtuṃ, tadā yogī hūṃkāraṃ praṇavādisvāhāntaṃ japed ity āha: jñānabījam ityādi. jñānabījaṃ hūṃkāraḥ. yadepsā ātmacetaneti:yadi cetasyātmecchā syād ity arthaḥ. oṃ hūṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[3.4.25] sattvārtharāgam utpannaṃ taptakāñcanasamaprabham iti: sattvo 'tra prajñā, tasyārthaḥ sukhaṃ, tadartho rāgaḥ sattvārtharāgaḥ. atha vā sattvānām artho nirmokṣaḥ, tatra rāgaḥ saṅgaḥ, tadviśuddhyā taptakāñcanavarṇo bhāgavān. bhāvaś cāyam:
yady api śuklo dharmadhāturūpatvāt, tathāpi sattvārtharāgaviśuddhyā raktavarṇānuviddhaḥ śukla iti kāñcanasya taptatvena raktatvaṃ darśayati. atiśayayīta eva rakto 'bhipretaḥ. idānīṃ prajñāniṣpādanam āha: tato lakṣaṇetyādi. pūrvavat sarvaṃ śūnyaṃ svaparasantānaṃ vibhāvya vakṣyamāṇam bhāvayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.26] etad evāha: candretyādi. candramaṇḍalamadhyasthām aṃkāraniṣpannāṃ tadadhiṣṭhitāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ niṣpādya tanniṣpannāṃ prajñāṃ bhāvayet. kiṃbhūtām ity āha: triketyādi. sarvavid yogī, pūrvavad vajravat, trayoviṃśatidevatāyuktāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ bhāvayet. tanniṣpannāṃ prajñāṃ ca bhāvayed ity arthaḥ, ...
[3.4.27] ... buddhātmakaraśmiyuktāṃ prajñāpāramitātmakadehāṃ, ...
[3.4.28] ... yogaparyaṅkam iti sattvaparyaṅkasthāṃ, varṇaśleṣakagātrāṇām iti raktānuviddhasitām ity arthaḥ, pañcabuddhābhimaṇḍitam iti pañcabuddhamakuṭinīm.
[3.4.29] aṣṭāṅgakalanam āha: aṣṭākṣaretyādi subodham. asyā api bhagavata iva mahāmudrāṃ dvāv ityādināha. etac ca vyākhyātam eva.
[3.4.30] uddhṛta ityādi: asyā bījam akāraḥ, sa ca prajñopāyasvabhāvaḥ. uddhṛta iti prāktanaṃ smārayati. aṃkāra ity arthaḥ. ālikāli tv iti: ukāraḥ kāliḥ. daṇḍa ālir iti kecit. tasya bījaṃ tu jāpata iti: tadbījaṃ japed ity arthaḥ. oṃ aṃ svāheti mantrasamudāyaḥ. rāgetyādipūrvavat.
[3.4.31] sattva-m-uttaretyādi: sattvottaraṇaviśuddhyāliṅganamudrety arthaḥ. vajretyādi: pūrvapaṭaloddiṣṭasya krodhāṅguṣṭhasya agrasya amṛtākṣara cintitam ityāder (3.3.61ab) nirdeśo 'yam. khagamukhāyā vāmapārśvāgraṃ cālayet. kiṃ kṛtvety āha: vajretyādi. tarjanyagre suṃkāram amṛtākṣaraṃ cintayitvā tayā khagamukhāṃ cālayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.32] aṣṭādaśasthānastham amṛtaṃ cālanāc chravatīti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.33] idānīṃ tasyā rāgajananārthaṃ karaṇāny āha: dvandvetyādi. dvandvāliṅganam utsṛjyeti: prajñopāyasamāpattyavasthāṃ tyaktvā pūrvasyāṃ diśi karaṇaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. kutrety āha: pūrvābhimukha sthāpayed iti. pūrvābhimukhīṃ prajñāṃ sthāpayed iti bhāvaḥ. pūrvadiksthitasattvoddharaṇaviśuddhyā pūrvābhimukhatvam.
[3.4.34] karaṇam āha: lalitākṣepetyādi. lalitākṣepo vāmacaraṇanikuñcanam. devyānāṃ vajraṃpādena pādata iti: vajraṃpādena vajrasattvasya pādena, devyāḥ pādata iti devyāḥ pādatalam iti saṃbandhaḥ. vajrasattvasya dakṣiṇacaraṇena devyā vāmacaraṇam ākrāntam iti draṣṭavyam. vāmasya ghaṇṭā ūruṃ tv iti: vajrasattvo 'nuvarttate pādaś ca.
vajrasattvasya vāmapādo ghaṇṭāyāḥ prajñāyā dakṣiṇorūpari draṣṭavyaḥ. kucadvaye hastapāṇināv iti: prajñāyāḥ kucadvayaṃ vajrasattvasya pāṇidvayena grahītavyam iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.35] śṛṅgāro hasitabhāveneti: śṛṅgāreṇa hasanādikriyā kāryeti bhāvaḥ. adho tīkṣṇāni pīḍayed iti: adho 'dharaṃ, tīkṣṇāni dantās, taiḥ pīḍayed iti bhāvaḥ. ayam vajrasattvasya vyāpāraḥ. ghaṇṭā pāṇidvi guhyasyeti: prajñāyāḥ pāṇidvayena vajrasattvasya grīvā grahītavyā. guhyaśabdena grīvocyate. guhyasya vajrasattvasya kaṇṭho grahītavyaḥ.
upadeśata iti kecit. japed bījam advayam iti: svabījam aṃkāraṃ prajñoccārayet. advayam iti vajrasattvaḥ, so 'pi bījākṣaraṃ hūṃkāram uccārayed iti bhāvaḥ. prajñopāyādvayayogaṃ bhāvayed iti vā japed bījam advayaṃ tathety asyārthaḥ.
[3.4.36] pūrvadiksthitān sattvān uttārayituṃ saṃsārapāraṃ netuṃ karaṇam idam ārabdham iti bhāvaḥ. ākṣepeti karaṇam. idānīṃ dakṣiṇadigavasthitasattvottāraṇārthaṃ karaṇam āha: vāmetyādi. vāmākṣepo dakṣiṇajaṅghākuñcanaṃ vāmajaṅghāprasāraṇam. devyā kartavyam etat. dakṣiṇamukham eva ceti: dakṣiṇābhimukhatvaṃ prajñāyāḥ,
tasyāḥ prādhānyaṃ hi yoginītantre, rataviṣaye vā.
[3.4.37] vajraṃ so 'pi vikṣepeti: vajradharasya dakṣiṇapādaprasāraṇaṃ devyā vāmapādopari. talābhyāṃ dvaubhi pīḍayed iti: prajñopāyau parasparaṃ pādataladvayaṃ pādataladvayena pīḍayetām iti bhāvaḥ. prajñāyā dakṣiṇena pādatalenopāyasya vāmapādatalasya pīḍanaṃ yoga iti bhāvaḥ. ghaṇṭā dvaubhi pāṇasya kaṭisandhiṃ tu pīḍayed iti:
prajñayā svaṃ pāṇidvayam upāyasya kaṭidvaye dhartavyam ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.38] vajram ekaṃ tu guhyaṃ tu dakṣiṇaṃ kucam eva ceti: vajrasattva ekena kareṇa vāmena guhyaṃ grīvāṃ prajñāyā dhārayet. dakṣiṇena kucaṃ gṛhnīyāt. ghaṇṭā vajrasya mūrtīnām iti: prajñopāyau parasparamukhaṃ pratikṣanaṃ cumbeyātām iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.39] ubhayarāgajananārthaṃ prajñopāyau svabījaṃ japantau karaṇenānena dakṣiṇadiggatān sattvān uttārayetām iti bhāvaḥ. idānīṃ paścimadiśi karaṇam āha: paścimam ityādi. paścimadiggatā satī samapadaṃ hastāntaraṃ vidhāya, devyā devī paścimadṛṣṭiketi paścimasattvottāraṇārthaṃ paścimadṛṣṭiḥ syāt.
[3.4.40] vajram ekapārśvasthaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ jānu pīḍitam iti: vajraṃ vajrasattva ekapārśvasthaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ devyā jānu vāmena kareṇa pīḍayet. jaghanabāhūparisthena dakṣiṇena saṃveṣṭya pṛṣṭhaṃ grīvāṃ vā saṃyogaṃ kuryād iti. ghaṇṭā jihvena lālayet.
[3.4.41] vajrajihvāṃ tu saṃspṛśyeti: devī svajihvayā vajradharasya jihvāṃ spṛśed ity arthaḥ. etac ca cumbanakāle draṣṭavyam. jāpa yoga dvayaṃ tatheti: samāpattau pūrvavaj japa ity arthaḥ. idānīm uttaradiggatasattvottāraṇārthaṃ karaṇam āha: vajram ityādi. vajraṃ vajrasattvaḥ. sama madhyaṃ tv iti:
prajñāyā jaghanamadhye samenopaviśya bāhubhyāṃ tasyā madhyaṃ pīḍayeta. etad evāha: bāhu dvau madhya pīḍayed iti.
[3.4.42] ghaṇṭā dvaubhi pāṇasya karṇamūle tu dhārayed iti: prajñāpi karābhyām upāyasya karṇamūle nakhakṣataṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. saṃlagnādharau dvau prajñopāyau cumbanakāle 'mṛtam ivādharapānaṃ kuryātām ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.43] pūrvavan mantrasya japaṃ darśayann āha: japed ityādi. vajraghaṇṭābhi krīḍanam iti: vajrasattvadevyoḥ krīḍanam ittham iti bhāvaḥ. asyāḥ krīḍāyāḥ saṃbhūtaṃ yat puṇyaṃ, tena sarve sattvāḥ sukhino bhavantv iti praṇidhānam āha: sarva ityādi.
[3.4.44] yena mārgeṇa buddhatvaṃ labhante, tena gacchantu. kurv iti: svacittakriyānumatau, yena sattvāḥ sukhinaḥ santi nirāmayāś ca, he citta tat tvam api kuru: anutiṣṭhety arthaḥ. prajñopāyasamāpattau jhaṭiti devatāyogaḥ kārya ity āha: jhaṭitākāre tyādi.
[3.4.45] etad evāha: ghaṇṭā devasya yogīnām iti. yadā prajñāyogo devasya vajradharasya syāt, tadā jhaṭiti devatāmudrāmantrādikaṃ kāryam. bhāvayed vicakṣaṇa iti: evaṃprakāreṇa vicakṣaṇo yogī vajrasattvarūpeṇātmānaṃ devīrūpeṇa prajñāṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.46] devyāḥ śarīre 'ṣṭākṣaranyāsam āha: aṣṭākṣarasyetyādi. aṣṭāv akṣarāṇi devatāsvarūpāṇi devyāḥ śarīre bhagavaccharīravan nyastavyānīti bhāvaḥ. eṣa ca pūrvoddiṣṭasya kalanasya nirdeśaḥ. etad evāha: devyā ityādi. devyāḥ śarīre cetanā cittaṃ kalanadvāreṇa kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. punar eveti: kalanam uktam api punar ucyate smaraṇārtham iti bhāvaḥ.
anye tv evaṃ varṇayanti: naṣṭarāgonnayanārthaṃ devyām ātmaceto vyāpārayituṃ yadi syād yogī, tadā punaḥ kalanam iti.
[3.4.47] tata ityādi: tata iti kalanānantaram. vajrasyeti vajrasattvaḥ. m-āliṅgyeti: svayaṃ devīm āliṅgya svayam anuraktaṃ cittaṃ devyām utpādayet. atha vajrasyetyādi: itthaṃ cittam utpādayan ...
[3.4.48] ... vajrasattvo 'ham ity ātmany ahaṃkāraṃ kuryāt. etad evāha: aham ityādi. atha vā tata iti (47a) karaṇāvasthānantaram. vajrasya (47a) vajrasattvasya. m-āliṅgyety (47a) āliṅganādikriyā. svem iti (47b) svayam. anuraktasya ceta neti (47b): anuraktasyety anuraktam. ceteti cittam. nā iti pratiṣedhe. etad uktaṃ syāt:
karaṇāvasthāyām api prajñāyāś cittaṃ nānuraktaṃ bhavatīti lakṣyate, tadā svayaṃ vajrasattvayogī tasyā āliṅganādikaṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ. athety (47c) adhaḥ. kvacid adheti pāṭhaḥ. prajñāyā adhogatam ātmānaṃ yogī kuryāt. kathaṃ kuryād ity āha: cetanā saheti (47d). cittena saha. yadā nānuraktaṃ tasyāś cittaṃ lakṣyate, tadaitad api kartavyam iti bhāvaḥ.
viparītaratam apy anena darśitam. tasyām avasthāyāṃ vajrasattvāhaṃkāraceto 'nyathā mā bhūd ity ahaṃkārādi vajrasyety (48a) uktam. vajrasattvam ahaṃ paṭhed ity (48b) uddiṣṭasya nirdeśaḥ.
[3.4.49] idānīṃ khagamukhāyā nāḍyāḥ svarūpam āha: ambhodbhavasyetyādi. ambhodbhavasya puṣpāṇām iti: padmapuṣpasya dharmodayāyā ity arthaḥ. tasya kim ity āha: antargūḍhasya lakṣaṇam iti. tasya padmasyābhyantare gūḍhaṃ guptaṃ, tac ca khagamukhaiva lakṣyata iti lakṣaṇam. dharmodayāntargūḍhā khagamukhā nāḍīti bhāvaḥ. kim ivety āha:
aṅkurā mūla puṣpāṇām iti. yathāṅkuro mūlaṃ puṣpāṇāṃ, tathā nāḍī dharmodayāyā mūlam. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.4.50] idānīṃ pūrvoktam eva kalanaṃ sthāpanadvāreṇa vyaktīkurvvann āha: uradvayasyetyādi. uradvayaṃ skandhadvayam. bījānām iti: bahuvacananirdeśāt kaṇṭhamūlahṛdayanābhimūlāny api gṛhyante. nyasanīyabāhulyān nyāsasthānabāhulyam. bījaśabdena kūṭabījāny abhimatāni. tataś ca skandhadvaye smryuṃ, kaṇṭhamūle hmryuṃ, hṛdaye ymryuṃ, nābhimūle kṣmryuṃ.
pṛthivyeti hūṃkāraḥ, sa ca cakṣuṣoḥ. vāyur yuṃkāraḥ, sa ca karṇanāsayoḥ. nimna āpasvabhāvasyeti: nimne jihvāyāṃ, apsvabhāvaḥ suṃkāraḥ. aṅkura agnibījavad iti: aṅkure tāluni kṣuṃ. idaṃ cāgnibījam.
[3.4.51] yathetyādi subodham.
[3.4.52] devyā rūpa saṃcintyeti: tenoddhṛtenāṃkāreṇadevyā rūpaṃ niṣpādya, pṛthivyā madhya līyata iti: pṛthivī dharmodayā, tasyā madhye ca sa hūṃkāro līyate śliṣyate. etac ca prāg evoktaṃ smārayati. khagamukhāyām aṃkāro nyasanīya iti bhāvaḥ. kālāgni preta agnisyeti: kālāgnir dakṣiṇa ucyate, pretāgnir vāmaś ca.
tataś ca kālāgninā dakṣiṇena kareṇa prathamam upāya āliṅgati, paścād vāmena kareṇeti bhāvaḥ. idānīṃ prajñāyā āliṅganam āha: pretāgni kālāgnivad iti. pretāgninā vāmena kareṇa prathamaṃ prajñāliṅgati, paścād dakṣiṇena kareṇeti bhāvaḥ. tenopāyasya dakṣiṇabhāgaḥ pradhānaḥ, prajñāyā vāmabhāga iti sūcitaṃ syāt.
[3.4.53] dvayasaṃpuṭapāṇasyeti: dvayoḥ prajñopāyayoḥ saṃpuṭe pāṇayaḥ kīdṛśā ity āha: trisūcyā vajram ucchrita iti. triśūkavajrākāreṇa pāṇayas tayor ucchritā bhavantīti bhāvaḥ. triśūkavajrākāratvam eva katham ity āha: kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭha dvau veṣṭyeti:
vṛddhāṅguṣṭhena kanīyasīṃ samākramya śeṣāṅgulitrayam utthitaṃ kāryaṃ, tatas triśūkavajrākāraḥ syād iti bhāvaḥ. evaṃ saty api kutra te pāṇayo dhāryā ity āha: dvayamudrādi pārśvata iti. prajñopāyayor viniyamena pārśvadvaye dhāryā iti bhāvaḥ. tataś ca triśūkavajramudrā syād iti mudrety asyārthaḥ.
[3.4.54] adharo tīkṣṇa saṃpīḍya amṛtāhāra cetaneti: adharaṃ tīkṣṇair dantaiḥ saṃdaśyādharamadhu pibed iti bhāvaḥ. amṛtam adharamadhu. āhāreti: āharet. ādadītety arthaḥ. cetaneti yogī. aṣṭādaśabhītyādinā bodhicittacodanāṃ smārayati. ghaṇṭā codaneti: prajñāyā amṛtaṃ codayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.55] bandhūka vāmabhāga iti: bandhūkakusumasadṛśī khagamukhā nāḍī, sā ca dharmodayāvāmabhāge. ata āha: kharatāpeti m-ākṛtīti. kharatāpaṃ khagamukhā. m-ākṛtīti: ākṛtā vyaktety arthaḥ. punaḥ kīdṛśīty āha: khagacañcukam ākāram iti. pakṣicañcunibhety arthaḥ. anenākṛtiḥ kathitā. kharatāpeti svarūpākhyānam. tasya mudreṇa codayed iti:
tāṃ nāḍīṃ mudrayā bhāvitakriṃkārayā tarjanyā codayet. atha vā gajahastādinā.
[3.4.56] dāyiṇaṃ vajram ambūnām iti: samāpattau dakṣiṇabalitena vajreṇa padmenāpi vāmabalitena gharṣaṇaṃ prajñopāyau kuryātām iti bhāvaḥ. ambūnām iti: ambujaṃ padmaṃ dharmodayety arthaḥ. vajrasya dakṣiṇatvena padmasya yuktyā vāmatvam. samakārānurūpavad iti: samaṃ prajñopāyayor gharṣaṇam iti bhāvaḥ. pulakaṃ dadyād urasyā sarva saṃsthitam iti:
tarjanyā pulakaṃ dadyād urasi stanayor ity arthaḥ. tena sarvāṅgasaṃsthitaṃ bodhicittaṃ sravatīti kriyā draṣṭavyā. urasyety upalakṣaṇamātratvāt. varāṅgādau ca pulakaṃ dadyāt.
[3.4.57] ata vajrasūcyādimudrāṇāṃ pārśvasthānaṃ tu yojayed iti. ateti: ata ity arthaḥ. ato bodhicittacodanāhetoḥ pūrvaṃ vajrasūcīmudrā prajñopāyayor āliṅganaprastāve darśiteti bhāvaḥ. gharṣaṇam māṃsalayann āha: dvaubhi lagnam ākārau padma vajreṇa codayed iti.
dvau prajñopāyau lagnākārau parasparaṃ padmaṃ vajreṇa dakṣiṇabalitena, vajraṃ padmena vāmabalitena gharṣaṇaṃ kurvāṇau codayātām iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.58] japaṃ smarayann āha: hūṃkāretyādi. hūṃkāradhvaniṃ kurvāṇo vajradharo 'dvayabījam advayajñānam eva japet. svadehe sarvadevatāḥ satsukhena prīṇayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.59] ghaṇṭāpy aṃkāraṃ japed ity āha: ghaṇṭetyādi. vajrāgre jñānabījaṃ tv iti: vajrāgre hūṃkāraṃ vīkṣyātmānaṃ prīṇayet. saṃyogakāle vajrāgre hūṃkāro dhyeya ity arthaḥ. kiṃ ca prajñāyāḥ padme 'py aṃkāro dhyeyaḥ.
[3.4.60] vajretyādi: vajro vajradharaḥ. ghaṇṭā prajñā. itthaṃ tayoḥ svadehasaṃpuṭaṃ mudreti bhāvaḥ. itthaṃ tattvāvabodhena vajravajraghaṇṭāmudrādhāraṇam iti darśayann āha: vajra ghaṇṭāṃ cetyādi. bahirvajraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ mudrāṃ ca tadviśuddhyaiva dhārayed yogīty arthaḥ.
[3.4.61] vajram ityādi. tattveneti: hūṃkāreṇa. hūṃkāroccāraṇenety arthaḥ. hūṃkāram uccārayan vajreṇa gharṣayed ity arthaḥ. evaṃ padmam utthāpya prajñāpy aṃ kāram uccārayantī vajraṃ pratīcched ity āha: ghaṇṭā svecchena nādayed iti. itthaṃ vajraghaṇṭāsaṃyogaparamārthajñānenātmānaṃ saṃtoṣya dehasthā devatāḥ prīṇayed ity etad evāha:
vajraghaṇṭetyādi subodham.
[3.4.62] adhiṣṭhayed ityādi: adhiṣṭhayed bhakṣayed ity arthaḥ. kiṃ tad ity āha: tasya m-ambūnām iti. tadubhayayogajaṃ bodhicittam. tac ca svacittarūpam. dvaubhir ātmakair iti: ubhayātmajam iti bhāvaḥ. etac cobhayabodhicittam ubhayābhyām āsvādyam iti ca jñeyam.
[3.4.63] pūrvaṃ prajñāyāḥ pradhānyena karaṇaṃ darśitam. idānīm upāyasya nāyakatvena karaṇam āha: śṛṅgārākṣepetyādi. śṛṅgārasaṃsthānayukto yogī pradhānam ātmānaṃ gaṇayan sukhāsane niṣadya vajrasattvarūpam āsthāya kiṃ kuryād ity āha: lalitetyādi. lalitapādatvaṃ dakṣiṇacaraṇasaṃkocaḥ. vāma urusya ghaṇṭata iti: prajñāyā vāmacaraṇasaṃkoca iti bhāvaḥ.
etenaitad uktaṃ: vajradharo dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ saṃkocya vāmaṃ prasārayet. prajñā vāmaṃ caraṇaṃ saṃkocya dakṣiṇaṃ prasārayed iti.
[3.4.64] vāmāliṅgaṃ tu kucapāṇāv iti: vāmena kareṇāliṅgya kucaṃ pīḍayed iti bhāvaḥ. dāyiṇaṃ vajra lālitam iti: dakṣiṇena kareṇa vajram upāyena kamale prakṣepaṇīyam iti bhāvaḥ. evaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāya hasitādikriyāṃ kurvaṃś cumben muhur muhuḥ.
[3.4.65] idānīṃ karaṇāntaram āha: ghaṇṭetyādi. prajñāyā dakṣiṇacaraṇakuñcanaṃ lalitam ākāram ity asyārthaḥ. upāyasya vāmacaraṇakuñcanam iti draṣṭavyam. urum āsanam āsinam iti: upāyasyorūpari prajñāsīnety arthaḥ. dakṣiṇena caraṇena prajñā kaṭideśam āveṣṭya dhārayet.
[3.4.66] ekāliṅgana grīvaṃ tv iti: ekena dakṣiṇena kareṇa prajñāyā grīvām āliṅgya veṣṭayet. vāma ghaṇṭābhi nāditam iti: vāmena kareṇa prajñāyāḥ padmaṃ prasārayed iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā cālayed ity āśayaḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham. karaṇadvayaṃ darśitaṃ tāvad anyad apy ūhanīyam.
[3.4.67] vajragītam ityādi: vajram advayaṃ guhyam ity arthaḥ. tadanvitaṃ gītaṃ karaṇādyanantaraṃ gāyed iti bhāvaḥ. paścān madanaṃ pītvā gāyed ity āha: amṛtetyādi. amṛtodakaṃ madanam. etac ca śramāpanayanārthaṃ, prajñāsaṃtoṣaṇārthaṃ ca gānaṃ pānaṃ ca kāryam. ubhayor api hi pānam.
[3.4.68] vajragītam āha: sāretyādi. sāra samuccaa pāviaï iti: sāram apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaṃ taṃ samuccinoti sādhayaty aneneti sārasamuccayaḥ. tādṛśo 'pi śūnya iti viruddhālaṅkāraḥ. tattvato grāhyādinā śūnyo jalādinā ghaṭavat. ko 'sāv evam ity āha: saṃsāru iti. saṃsāraḥ pañca skandhāḥ.
skandhapañcakaṃ hi devatātmakaṃ, niḥsvabhāvaṃ ca bhāvyamānam anāsaṅgād apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaṃ sādhayati. asāru iti: na vidyate sāraḥ sthirāṅgo nityatā lakṣaṇo yasya so 'sāraḥ saṃsāraḥ. yadi grāhyādirahitaḥ saṃsāras, tataḥ katham apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇam api syād ity āha: bhāvetyādi. bhāvaḥ svabhāvo grāhyādilakṣaṇaḥ.
atha vā sad asannityānityādi bhavati pratibhāty asminn iti bhāvo dvayākāraṃ cittaṃ tena vimuktā siddhir api, apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaniṣpattir apīty arthaḥ. sadṛśāt sadṛśaṃ kāryam iti bhāvaḥ. paryāyeṇārthakathanam āha: mokkheti. apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaṃ mokṣa iti paryāyāv iti bhāvaḥ. tat kimartham ity āha: tāraṇeeṇeti. sattvatāraṇārtham ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.69] etad evāha: dondāliṅgaṇajou saala kājjieṇeti. śūnyatākaruṇāsvarūpo devatāyogo dvandvāliṅganayogaḥ sa nikhilajanakāryārtham. ṇāhi saṃsāru asāru iti: dvandvāliṅganayogam abhyasyatāṃ nāsty asāraḥ saṃsāraḥ pariśuddhatvena nirvāṇarūpatvād iti bhāvaḥ. tasmād yoginyo 'pi magnā eva pariśuddhā evety arthaḥ.
sarvatra padānte te nā hūṃ te nā hūṃ te nā te te hūṃ iti draṣṭavyam. ata evoktam: hūṃkāra vividhaṃ mateti (67b).
[3.4.70] ghaṇṭāṃ vādayitvā punar gāyed iti. tad evāha: vajraghaṇṭetyādi. itthaṃ yogī japan bhāvayan sidhyatīti darśayann āha: samāhitam ityādi. evaṃbhūtasya yogino yad yac cintitaṃ kāryaṃ, tat sarvaṃ sidhyatīti darśayann āha: yad ācintitetyādi.
[3.4.71] idānīṃ vaśīkaraṇārthaṃ yogam āha: ākṣepetyādi. ākṣipyate svavaśīkriyate yena, sa ākṣepo bhaiṣajyādi. tasya yoktavyam iti: mukhyatvena prajñāyāṃ tāvad yoktavyam oṣadham iti bhāvaḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.4.72] yogasthāna gṛhyata iti: sarveṣāṃ samo bhāga ity arthaḥ. bhāvayet tasya dravyeṇeti: tena bodhicittena tāni dravyāṇi bhāvayitavyānīti bhāvaḥ. bodhicittaṃ ca dvīndriyasamāpattijaṃ grāhyam. yogasthānaśabdena bodhicittam ity eke.
[3.4.73] indrahastādītyādinā yogāntaram āha. indrahastākṛti mūlaṃ yasya, tad indrahastam. mohanī vaṭaparṇikā. stambhanī śālaparṇī. ātmapañcikam iti pañcāmṛtam. pañcāṅgam iti cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇajihvāvajrāṇāṃ malaḥ pañcāṅgam ity ucyate. samabhāgaṃ kārayed iti: indrahastādīnāṃ samo bhāgo grāhyaḥ. anyeṣāṃ yathālābhaṃ grahaṇam.
[3.4.74] pāna iti madirādipāne. tāmbūlabhakṣasyeti: guvākabhakṣaṇe cūrṇena dātavyam iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.75] rajaḥsphulitayogena vaśakarmādi sidhyatīti: strīrajasā raktadevatāyogena ca vaśyaṃ karma sidhyatīti saṃbandhaḥ. mantralakṣeṇa ca sidhyatīty āha: mantretyādi.
[3.4.76] visarjya bodhicittaṃ tac cātmadehasya, pūrvoktena rajasā svadehajena ca bodhicittena, dvābhyāṃ miśrībhūtābhyām ity arthaḥ. raktadevatāyogī sarvaṃ vaśayatīti bhāvaḥ. ata āha: svecchābhi kārayed iti. rajo bodhicittaṃ ca japtalakṣamantro raktavarṇadevatāyogī yasmai dadāti, sa vaśī syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.77] śāntikādikāle mudrāṇām upayogam āha: vratītyādi. vratīti vratinī kāpālikīty arthaḥ. jñātīnām iti jñāninī, kāṇḍakāriṇī daivajñety arthaḥ. jñānīnām iti pāṭhe 'py evam. rājñīti kṣatriyā. guravaguhyeti brāhmaṇī. guraveti brāhmaṇī. guhyeti naṭī kecit.
[3.4.78] cirmiketi carmakārī. māṃsiketi māṃsavikrayaṇī. khaṭṭikādikanyety arthaḥ. mīneti kaivartī.
[3.4.79] rajakī prasiddhā. rajanīti pāṭhe 'pi rajakī. tasmād ityādi: yogaḥ śāntikādikarma. śāstreṇa mantraśāstreṇa. dhīyatāṃ sādhyatām. mantranaye yataḥ kathito mudrāgrāmaḥ, sa śāntikādisiddhyarthaṃ yatnena sādhyatām iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.80] idānīṃ bandhaprakāram āha: aparaivetyādi. apara ākṣepaḥ kathyata iti jñeyam. bandhena sattvottāraṇaṃ padam iti: sattvottāraṇārthaṃ padaṃ samapadādikam. dvaya saṃlagnam ākṣepa tattvamudreti bandhayed iti: padadvayaṃ samatayā saṃlagnaṃ samapadaṃ syād iti samapadasya lakṣaṇam. tad eva vighnākṣepakatvād ākṣepaḥ. tattvamudreti:
samapadabandho mudrātattvam ity arthaḥ. anye tu dvaya saṃlagnam ākṣepeti: dvayoḥ prajñopāyayoḥ saṃlagnaṃ saṃparko bandhādirūpaḥ. sa evākṣepaḥ pūrvavat. tattvam aviparītam advayajñānam ity arthaḥ. tasya mudrā rūpaṃ yataḥ samapadādikaṃ, tato badhnīyād iti nedaṃ samapadasya lakṣaṇam. samapadaṃ tu prasiddham eva.
[3.4.81] samatvena padadvayaṃ samapadam. dakṣiṇāgram ekaṃ caraṇam aparaṃ vāmāgraṃ dhārayed iti samapadam iti kecit. prajñopāyayos tu parasparapādāṅgulīnāṃ samena saṃsparśaḥ samapadam.
[3.4.82] tathābhūtāyām evāvasthāyāṃ nāryā ekacaraṇa saṃkocād bhinnapadam.
[3.4.83] samapadaṃ kṛtvā jānunī samaṃ vistārayed iti maṇḍalapadam.
[3.4.84] samāpattau nāryā ekasya caraṇasyordhvam utthāpanāt trivikramapadam.
[3.4.85] dakṣiṇacaraṇena vāmasyākramaṇāt kūrmapadam.
[3.4.86] vāmacaraṇakuñcanād dakṣiṇacaraṇaprasāraṇād ālīḍhapadam.
[3.4.87] vāmadakṣiṇacaraṇaprasāraṇasaṃkocābhyāṃ pratyālīḍhapadam.
[3.4.88] caraṇam ekam utkṣipyānyena sthitvā cakrākāraṃ bhramayed ity ekasthānam. samāpattau citrakaraṇam etat.
[3.4.89] niṣadyeti lalitākṣepādaya eva niṣadyam. sukhena niṣīdataḥ prajñopāyāv asminn iti niṣadyam.
[3.4.90] yatheṣṭaṃ pādavikṣepāt pādavikṣepaḥ.
[3.4.91] khedāpanayanārtham adharottarakrameṇa praguṇīkṛtya jaṅghe suptayoḥ prajñopāyayoḥ samāpattir yūpākāreti yūpākāraḥ.
[3.4.92] upaviśyaikaṃ jānu saṃkocya grīvādhāraṇapūrvakaṃ pravartanād añcupīḍanam. jānusaṃkoca ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.93] ekasyoror upari dvitīyam ūrum āropya prajñāyā jaghanayor madhye pravartanān saṃyamīkṛtam. anye tu prajñāyā jaṅghe kakṣayor nikṣipya bāhubhyāṃ saṃveṣṭya grīvāṃ dhṛtvā samāpattiṃ kuryād iti saṃyamīkṛtaṃ vyācakṣate.
[3.4.94] ubhayor apy anatidūram ūrū prasāryopaviśya pravṛtteḥ samasthānam.
[3.4.95] prajñāyā dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ svahṛdayenākramya pravartanāt kālāgniḥ.
[3.4.96] prajñāyā ūrudvayaṃ nijajaṅghayor abhyantarīkṛtya tasyāś caraṇau svahṛdaye samāropya pravṛtter hṛdayasphoṭamudrā hṛdayaśabdenocyate.
[3.4.97] prajñāyā ūrvor upary upaviśya pravṛttir nandāsanam ucyate.
[3.4.98] ekaṃ caraṇaṃ saṃkocya pravṛtter ekasaṃyatam.
[3.4.99] eta ākṣepaprakārā aṣṭādaśa samāpattau prayoktavyāḥ. saṃbhave guṇa sarvatra yogīnāṃ tu vilakṣayed iti: eteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ saṃbhavo yasyāṃ, tasyām eva bandhaviśeṣā yojanīyā iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.100] idānīm abhinayaviśeṣān āha: hṛdītyādi. prajñāṃ bāhubhyāṃ pṛṣṭhe saṃveṣṭya hṛdaye hṛdayena saṃpīḍya pravṛtter hṛdayapīḍanam.
[3.4.101] prajñāyāś caraṇau hṛdaye saṃsthāpya pravṛtteḥ pādasaṃlagnam. atha vā prajñāyāḥ padbhyāṃ svapādadvayaṃ saṃyojya pravṛtteḥ pādasaṃlagnam.
[3.4.102] sragdāmābhinayapūrvakam āliṅganaṃ sragdāmābhinayaḥ.
[3.4.103] kucasparśāt kucābhinayaḥ.
[3.4.104] prajñāyāḥ kamalam upāyo leḍhi, upāyasya ca vajraṃ prajñā. tac cādharottarabhāvena draṣṭavyam iti lagnadvaubhakṣaṇasyārthaḥ. lagnadvibhakṣaṇam iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.105] ardhenāṅgenāliṅganād ardhāliṅganam.
[3.4.106] anyonyaguhya sparśāt pāṇibhyām iti adho dvau pāṇayaṃ guhyasparśanasyārthaḥ. ardha dvau pāṇayaṃ guhyasparśaneti pāṭhasyāpy ayam evārthaḥ.
[3.4.107] tarjanīmadhyamābhyāṃ cibukasyotthāpanaṃ cibutolanam.
[3.4.108] śarīrasparśād aṅgasparśanam.
[3.4.109] śṛṅgāreṇa hasitānanaḥ syād iti śṛṅgāro hasitānanasyārthaḥ. śṛṅgārahasitam abhinaya ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.110] na kevalam ete daśābhinayāḥ kāryāḥ, kiṃ tarhi daśanacchedādayo 'pi kāryā iti darśayann āha: vividhā ityādi. vajrādidhāraṇaniyamam upasaṃharann āha: vajretyādi. vajraghaṇṭā mudrā ca yathoktā yoginā yogānuraktena sadā dhartavyāḥ.
[3.4.111] prajñopāyayor anyonyarahitatvena siddhir na syād ity āha: rahitetyādi. vajraguhyam upāyaḥ. ghaṇṭasyeti prajñā. ghaṇṭārahitasyopāyasya phalaṃ kīdṛśam? na kim api phalam iti bhāvaḥ. upāyarahitāyā ghaṇṭāyā api sādhyaṃ dūrata iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.112] vyatirekam uktvānvayam āha: vajraghaṇṭāś ca mudrāś ca m-ālambya pāram uttareti. prajñopāyayor militayor eva siddhir ity arthaḥ. dṛṣṭāntam āha: rajanyetyādi. rajanī candravikalā kīdṛśī jyotsnāvaikalyāt? svārthaṃ parārthaṃ ca kartum aśaktety arthaḥ. candrasya dina kīdṛśam iti:
divābhāge candro 'pi rajanīvaikalyāt kīdṛśaḥ syāt? jyotsnārahitatvān na kam api svārthaṃ parārthaṃ vā kartuṃ śakta ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.113] dārṣṭāntikam upasaṃharann āha: tata ityādi. yathā rajanīrajanīpatyoḥ samakālabhāvitvaṃ svaparārthakṣamaṃ, tathā vajrasya ghaṇṭāyāś ca samatā samakālabhāvitvaṃ samāpattir iti yāvat. tattvaṃ jyohuye iti: tattvaṃ svarūpaṃ, jyohuye iti jyotsnāyuktaṃ karoti prakāśayatīti bhāvaḥ. svarūpaṃ ca mahāmudrāsiddhir eva. prakāśanaṃ sādhanam.
kvacit sarva jyotsnayed iti pāṭhaḥ. tatra sarvaṃsādhyam ity arthaḥ. ālīkālītyādi: prajñopāyayogaṃ pura iti prathamaṃ dhyātvā mahāyogena pūrvoktaśūnyatāmantracihnādiniṣpattikrameṇa sādhaketi yogī sarvakarmāṇi sādhayed iti saṃbandhaḥ.
[3.4.114] ālikālyor artham āha: ālītyādi subodham.
[3.4.115] sattvārthaṃ sakalaṃ ca prajñopāyayogena devatārūpeṇa sādhayed ity āha: sattvārthetyādi. prajñopāyayogena nirvāṇam apīty āha: prajñā-mupāyabāhyānām ityādi. prajñopāyabahirbhūtānāṃ kuta upāyāt saṃsārottaraṇam? enaṃ prajñopāyasvarūpasyopāyaṃ vihāya saṃsārottāropāyo nānyo 'stīti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.116] samayasya devatādirūpasya yatheṣṭam akathyatvam āha: mūrkhā bāhyādicetasya tasya samaya darśitam. mūrkhasya bahirartharaktasya ca samaya darśitam iti samayo na kathyaḥ. samayo darśitaḥ kim? na darśita iti śiraścālena gamyate. guhyetyādi subodham.
[3.4.117] yato āgametyādi: yaḥ khalv āgamaṃ jñātum icchati, tato yogī sa eva yogīty arthaḥ. śuddhasphaṭikacittasya tasya dharmān deśayed iti: śuddhasphaṭikanirmalaṃ yasya cittaṃ, tasmai dharmaṃ deśayed ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.118] kuta evam ity āha: yadītyādi. yadi mano vicikitsā kim idam uta neti saṃśayaḥ, maṇavicikitsāyaśabdasyārthaḥ. yadi guror bhagavato 'pi vacane saṃśayo yasya syāt, tadā so 'vīcyādau patatīti gurvāgamavacanapratikṣepāt. tataḥ śuddhasphaṭikacittatvena śraddhāprajñāvatām evāsya dharmasya śravaṇādhikāra iti bhāvaḥ.
kāmādisaktāya na deśyo dharma ity āha: na tv ityādi.
[3.4.119] tattvam ālambya: tattvaṃ nairātmyaśūnyatāṅgam iti yāvat. yaḥ pāramitānayaṃ jñātvā sattvārthaṃ kartum icchati, tasmai tantram idaṃ deśyam iti bhāvaḥ. yogino viṣayopabhogam āha: yadītyādi. yadi kāmarato yogī kāmopabhogam icchati, tadā tasyaika evopāya ity upāyam ekalakṣaṇam ity asyārthaḥ.
[3.4.120] upāyam evāha: saṃbhava ityādi. svacchair iti: viṣayāḥ. kṣanikair ity avasaraprāptāḥ. strīviṣayā iti viśeṣeṇa draṣṭavyam. viṣayāṇāṃ saṃbhave sati lābhe siddhe kāmam upabhujyate yadi, tadā ghaṇṭāvajrādiyogata iti
vajrapadmayogenopabhuñjīteti bhāvaḥ. vajrapadmayoga eva kathaṃ sādhyata ity āha: jhaṭitākāretyādi. jhaṭitiniṣpanno vajradharo 'ham ity ahaṃkāram āropayet.
[3.4.121] antardhetyādinā khagamukhāyāṃ aṃkāro dhyātavya iti smāritam. ātmakṣaṇika yogānāṃ hūṃkṛtam iti: kṣaṇikaṃ kuliśam. ātmanaḥ kuliśe hūṃkāraṃ yuñjītety arthaḥ. jāpya tatparam iti: ubhayor japaḥ smāritaḥ.
[3.4.122] etat sarvaṃ prajñopāyādikam upasaṃhāre darśitam. bhāvanālakṣaṇaś cetovyāpāraḥ. tatra kāyavācor vyāpāro līyata iti darśayann āha: yadītyādi. yaḥ kaścit kāyavācor vyāpāraḥ, sa devatāyoge layaṃ gacchati. bhāvaś cāyam: yaḥ sādaranirantaraṃ dīrghakālaṃ ca devatāyogī, sa kiṃ stavādipāṭhacaityavandanādikaṃ kuryād iti.
deśanāmāhātmyam upadarśayann āha: añjalyetyādi. sarvāsu dikṣu sthitā ye yogā yogino vajrapāṇiprabhṛtayaḥ, yogīśvarīti yoginyo ḍākinyaḥ, śrutaṃ śrutvā dharmatām iti śeṣaḥ, añjaliṃ kurvanto ...
[3.4.123] ... bhagavantaṃ prītiprahlādayuktahṛdayāḥ stuvantīti draṣṭavyam. stavam āha: adyetyādi. yo 'yam upāyo bhagavatā deśitas, tenādyetīhaiva janmani mokṣaṃ vrajej, jagad iti jātyādiniyamābhāvaṃ darśayati. aho vismaye. dharma paraṃ mārga iti:
yato 'yaṃ mārgaḥ paraḥ śreṣṭho dharmaś ca, dharmaḥ prakṛtiśūnyatety arthaḥ, tadrūpatvād dharmo 'yaṃ mārgas, tato vismayaḥ. kuta evaṃbhūta ity āha: aho saṃsāracchedaketi. niyamena saṃsāracchedakatvād iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.124] aho muktipadam iti: kutaḥ saṃsāracchedakaḥ? yato mukteḥ padam upāya iti. tato yogināṃ buddhānām api yogeti yuñjīta. vismayeti vismayam. mārgaḥ karttā(?). mārgasya sulabhatvam āha: na tithir ityādi. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.4.125] karmaprasaram āha: karmacakravidhānaṃ tv ityādi. sādhakānāṃ vividhamatavaśāt karmacakravidhānaṃ vakṣyate. karmacakraṃ karmaprasaraḥ. candretyādi. evaṃviśiṣṭāṃ vettālīṃ vibhāvya ...
[3.4.126] ... japel lakṣatriketi: lakṣatrayam.
[3.4.127] mantram āha: dviguṇākṣaretyādi subodham. yady api kṣuṃkāro ghorībījaṃ, tathāpi vettālyāḥ praṇavādiyuto mantro 'yam. tattva karma sādhyetīti: karmatattvaṃ prasādhayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[3.4.128] pretavastretyādi: śavavastre subhaṭavastre prasūtavastre vā siṃghinīṃ citrayet. dvibhujam ityādi subodham.
[3.4.129–132] pṛthvīmadhyādīti: śmaśānamadhye. pūrvokto 'daityābalis taṃ dattvā karma kuryāt.
[3.4.133] śmaśāne pretāgniṃ prajvālya caṇḍālāgniṃ vā, homaṃ kuryād iti śeṣaḥ. samidhāna devadāruṇām iti: devadārusamidhaḥ. pañca kṣīrādīti: aśvatthādikṣīravṛkṣajāḥ pañca samidhaḥ.
[3.4.134] udārāṃ pūjāṃ baliṃ ca kṛtvā yathoktamāṃsenāhutiṃ dadyāt. rudhiramadhum āloḍyeti: madhu mahātailam. rudhiramadhubhyāṃ mrakṣayitvā nṛmāṃsam aṣṭaśatasaṃkhyaṃ juhuyāt.
[3.4.135] oṃ ityādi ...
[3.4.136] ... homamantraḥ.
[3.4.137] sarve yuktādi pūrvavad iti: pūrvakrameṇa hotavyam ity arthaḥ.
[3.4.138] nimittam āha: prathamam ityādi.
[3.4.139] pañcamena sahāyasya m-āveśa siṃgha-m-ākṛtim iti: uttarasādhake siṃghākṛtir dṛśyate. sa siṃghīva dṛśyata ity arthaḥ. siṃghyā āveśāt.
[3.4.140] pratīccha yathāsthāne āsanaṃ kuru m-ātmanam iti: svasthāna āsanaṃ karotu bhagavatīty ukte sāsaneniṣaṇṇā syād iti tasyārthaḥ.
[3.4.141–142] uttarasādhakaṃ hitvā darśanaṃ kuru devīty ukte tataḥ sā bhagavatī vatsa kiṃ mayā kartavyam iti vadati. kimarthaṃ sādhitāsmīti yāvat. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.4.143] oṃ ityādi mantraṃ paṭhitvā ...
[3.4.144] ... śirasi hastaṃ dadyāt.
[3.4.145] pretayāneneti: vāmāvartena. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[3.4.146–149] vyāghrīśivyulūkīnāṃ tu tasya karmeṇa sādhayed iti: siṃghīvad vyāghryādīnāṃ sādhanam iti bhāvaḥ. kiṃ tu vyāghryā oṃ hmyruṃ svāheti mantraḥ. śivīti jambukī. tasyā oṃ ymyruṃ svāheti mantraḥ. ulūkyā oṃ kṣmryuṃ svāheti mantraḥ.
[3.4.150] sarvatra prajñopāyayogena siddhir iti darśayann āha: vajraghaṇṭāś cetyādi subodham.
[3.4.col.] ity uktakrameṇa prajñopāyādvayayogaprākārādiviśuddhiprajñopāyadevatārūpaniṣpādanakhagamukhācālanabandhaviśeṣagānabhāvanānuśaṃsāvaśīkaraṇabhaiṣajyakarmaprasarasiṃghyādisādhanāni prakriyante yasmin paṭale sa itiprakaraṇaḥ, sa ca caturthaḥ. tatra yogapīṭhaṃ samāptam. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe yogapīṭhaṃ samāptam.

[4.1.1] idānīṃ guhyapīṭham āha: śṛṇu vajretyādi. vajreti vajrapāṇe, saṃsārottāralakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇv iti sambandhaḥ. saṃsārād uttaraty aneneti saṃsārottāro gurus, tasya lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpam. tat kathaṃ śrotavyam ity āha: yathāmārgam iti. mṛgyate 'neneti mārgaḥ śāstram. tasyānatikramo yathāmārgaḥ. yathāmārgam iti yathāmārgeṇety arthaḥ.
vajrapāṇer vacanam āha: ācāryaṃ ca grahīṣyāmīti. siddhyupāyaśikṣaṇārthaṃ grahīṣyāmi jñāsyāmīty arthaḥ. kīdṛśaṃ tam ity āha: mantramudrādiyogavid iti. mantreti śāntikādimantrāḥ. yogeti samādhiḥ. ādiśabdān maṇḍalādikam. mudreti kāyādimudrā. kāyamudrā karamudrā. vāṅmudrā vacasā yā kriyate, vākchommakam ity arthaḥ.
cittamudrā bhāvanayā yā dṛśyate varṇabhujādisaṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ. kāyādimudraivaṃ yadā bhāvyate, tadā cittagatatvāc cittamudrā. mantrādivijña ācāryaḥ syāt, tasya lakṣaṇam idānīṃ śrotum icchāmīti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.2] bhagavan kena draṣṭavyeti: kīdṛśa ācāryo draṣṭavya iti bhāvaḥ. guṇa kīdṛśam iti: tasya guṇāḥ kīdṛśā ity arthaḥ. katham ācārya yoktavyam iti: ācāryakaṃ kathaṃ yoktavyam iti bhāvaḥ. gurusaṃjñeti kīdṛśam iti: tasya gurusaṃjñā kīdṛśīti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.3] tattvaṃ sarvasaṃbodhi lakṣaṇam iti. tattvaṃ śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. tac cācāryasya prakṛtatvāt. sarvasaṃbodhi lakṣaṇam iti: lakṣyate 'neneti lakṣaṇaṃ saṃjñā. tasyācāryasya yan nāma, tat sarvāḥ sarvajñatāmārgajñatāsarvākārajñatālakṣaṇāḥ saṃbodhayaḥ. nāma cābhiṣekanāma. sarvāḥ saṃbodhaya eva tasya nāmeti bhāvaḥ. sarvabuddhasya kāyasyeti:
ācāryasya yaḥ kāyaḥ, sa sarvabuddhamayaḥ. sarvabuddho mahāvajradharaḥ. bodhyaṅgāvayavasthitam iti: sapta bodhyaṅgāni tasyāvayavā ity arthaḥ.
[4.1.4] arhantas tasya romāṇīti: arhanto buddhā lakṣakoṭyādisaṃkhyās, te tasya romāṇi. kuletyādi: trīṇi kulāni pañca ṣaḍ vā tasya makuṭam. laukikā laukikyo devatā brahmādayaḥ pādapīṭhasyeti pādau. raśmi yakṣādiguhyakam iti: guhyakam iti gandharvāḥ. tasya kāyaraśmayo yakṣabhūtagandharvāḥ.
[4.1.5] evam ityādi: uktaguṇo deho yoginām ācāryāṇām. guṇa kīdṛśam iti (2b) praśnaṃ visṛjann āha: yasyetyādi. ko 'sāv ācārya evaṃguṇamaya ity āha: yasya lakṣaṇayoktavyam iti. yasya jñānina ācāryasya lakṣaṇayogo jñeyalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇarūpaḥ, tasyaivaṃguṇamayo deha iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ.
[4.1.6] lakṣaṇam āha: vinītetyādi. vinīto jitendriyaḥ. śāntaveśo laukikaveśaparihārāt. mantratantraprayogajñeti: mantraḥ śāntikādimantras, tantraṃ śāntikādicakraṃ, tayoḥ prayogo japalikhanādilakṣaṇas, tajjña ācāryaḥ. yogaśilpakalādiveti: yogo vāyavyādiyogaḥ, śilpaṃ citrakarmādi, kalā vaidyaśāstrādi, ādiśabdāc chabdaśāstrādikam.
yogādikaṃ vāti jānātīti yoga śilpa kalādi veti nirvibhaktikam.
[4.1.7] mādhuryam ityādi: priyavāk sattveṣv ity arthaḥ. sarvasattvaikaputravad iti: maitrīyukta ity arthaḥ. dānetyādi: dānaśīlakṣāntyādiyukta ity arthaḥ. yogaḥ prajñopāyayogaḥ. dhyānaṃ devatābhāvanā.
[4.1.8] samatetyādi: śatrumitrodāsīnābhedena samatayā sattveṣu nāthabhūtaḥ. daśatattvetyādi:
maṇḍalaṃ svasamādhiś ca mudrā karaṇam āsanam |
japahomau tathā pūjā karmayogopasaṃhṛtī ||
iti daśatattvaparijñātā. ete vinayādayaḥ śreyo'rthibhir guṇyante, abhyasyanta iti guṇāḥ. ebhir yogād ācāryakaṃ syād, gurusaṃjñā ca, yathoktaguṇācaraṇāt. yad āha:
yathoktācaraṇāt samyak pareṣām ātmano 'pi vā |
gurur ācāryaśabdena paṇḍitaiḥ parigadyate ||
iti.
[4.1.9] aparair ityādinā śiṣyakarmāha. aparam anyat. tac ca śiṣyāṇāṃ karma. śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. nindām ācāryaguhyānām iti: ācārya eva guhyo mahāvajradharas, tasya nindāṃ nindako 'vīcau patatīti saṃbandhaḥ. āha ca:
bodhicittavajraś cācāryaś cādvayam advaidhīkāram
iti. anyac ca:
nānātvaṃ naiva kurvīta guror Vajradharasya ca |
iti. punaḥ kīdṛśasyety āha: yoginītyādi. yoginyo jñānaḍākinyādayaḥ. yogadevateti: prajñopāyasamāpannā devatāḥ. etatsvarūpasyācāryasyeti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.10] samayācāradevasyeti: pañcāṅkuśapañcāmṛtalakṣaṇaḥ samayaḥ. tadācāreṇa dīvyatīti krīḍatīti samayācāradeva ācāryaḥ. baliguhyādiyaugiketi: balihomāditattve yuktasya nindakaḥ. kadā patatīty āha: śīghram iti. ihaiva janmani maraṇasamanantaram iti bhāvaḥ.
atha vā na kevalam ācāryasya nindako 'vīcau patati, kiṃ tarhi yoginyādīnām api nindaka iti vyākhyeyam. sarvatrāsamayena tv iti: anicchann api prāpte samaye vaimukhyād avīcau patatīti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.11] guroḥ śayyādilaṅghanādināvīcau patatīti darśayann āha: śayyetyādi. niṣīdaty asminn iti niṣadyam āsanam. pādū upānat. guroḥ śayyādīnāṃ laṅghanādikarmaṇāvīcau patatīti bhāvaḥ. chattravastropabhogavān iti: guruṇopayujyamānasya chattrasya vastrasya vā bhogaḥ svayam upayogas, tadvān avīcau patatīti saṃbandhaḥ. chāyollaṅghanakrāntasyeti:
guroś chāyālaṅghanāc, chāyām aspṛṣṭvā tadupari pādanayanād iti yāvat. krāntasyeti: padbhyāṃ guruchāyākramaṇāc ca. praśrabdhā na ca vākyavad iti: guruṇā saha kathālāpādau guruṇājñapte vā kṛtye «nedaṃ syān», «nedaṃ karomi», «na pārayāmī»tyādi neti vākyaṃ vadann avīcau patatīti saṃbandhaḥ. praśrabdheti vacanāt. «idaṃ pādair bhadrakarmādiṣṭam.
kiṃ cāyaṃ ko 'pi vyatireka» ityādinā naṅṅarthaḥ sūcayitavyaḥ.
[4.1.12] itthaṃ tāvad ayaṃ paraloke doṣa iha loke ka ity āha: teṣv ityādi. teṣv ācāryādinindanādiṣu satsu nimittabhūteṣu vā grastā pīḍā bhavet. pīḍā duḥkhaṃ labdhā syād iti bhāvaḥ. kāsau pīḍety āha: tata ityādi. tebhyo gurulaṅghanādibhyo 'rthahāniḥ. śrīḥ puṇyajñānasaṃbhāralakṣaṇā, lakṣmīr vā. sā dūrasthā syāt. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.1.13] svapne siddhīti svapne 'pīty arthaḥ. ācāryānugatatvāt siddheḥ.
[4.1.14–15] śikṣāpūrvakam ityādi: ādau daśakuśalādiśikṣāṃ dattvā triśaraṇagamanādipuraḥsaraṃ maṇḍalapraveśādipūrvaṃgamam abhiṣekaṃ pūrvoktalakṣaṇāya śiṣyāya pūrvoktaguṇa ācāryo dadyād iti bhāvaḥ. makuṭābhiṣekam āha: pūrvākṣaram ityādi. pūrvākṣaraṃ hūṃkāraḥ. ekasyeti: aḥkāraḥ. praṇavasvāhākārau ca draṣṭavyau. oṃ hūṃ aḥ svāheti mantraḥ.
tena pañcabuddhamayamakuṭābhiṣekaṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.16] tasyetyādi: tasya makuṭābhiṣekasya yogena sveṣṭaṃ karma sidhyatīty arthaḥ. mantrābhiṣeketyādi: mantrābhiṣekādyante aṣṭasthāneṣv aṣṭākṣaranyāsaḥ.
[4.1.17] adaityābalīti: paścāc ca baliṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ. abhiṣekaṃ tasya dharmeṇa jñāpayed iti: vajraghaṇṭābhiṣekaḥ sūcitaḥ. yogetyādi: evam abhiṣekayuktasya jñānādi kathayet. paścān nāmābhiṣekānantaram ācāryābhiṣekaṃ dadyāt. sa ca sarveti. vajravajraghaṇṭānvitakarāliṅganaṃ sarvaśabdenocyate. tato guhyeti guhyābhiṣekaḥ. ādīti caturthābhiṣekaḥ.
[4.1.18] abhiṣekakramam āha: śiṣyāṇām ādāv ityādi. maṇḍalaniṣpattipūjābalidānādyanantaraṃ śiṣyam adhivāsayet. tad yathā: puraḥsthitāya hṛdi kṛtāñjalaye śiṣyāya mukhaśuddhyarthaṃ oṃ āḥ jihvāsaṃvartini hūṃ phaḍ iti mantreṇa saptavārābhimantritaṃ dantakāṣṭhodakaṃ dattvā saṃvaraṃ dadyāt.
[4.1.19] guruḥ kīdṛśo dadyād ity āha: gurum ityādi. siṃhāsanasthadevatāyogī san. kiṃ kṛtvā samvaraṃ dadyād ity āha: pāṇāv ityādi. dakṣiṇena pāṇinā hṛdi nyastaṃ baddhāñjaliṃ śiṣyakaradvayaṃ spṛṣṭvā samvaraṃ dadyāt.
[4.1.20] samvaram āha: buddham ityādi ...
[4.1.21–23] ... mahādharmakriyāpadam ityantam imaṃ granthaṃ śiṣyaṃ pāṭhayet.
[4.1.24] kiyato vārān ity āha: dvir api trir api samatācittena uktavān iti. kāyādisamatāviśuddhyā vāratrayaṃ vācayed iti bhāvaḥ. tataḥ prajñāpāramitādigambhīradharmadeśanāpuraḥsaraṃ maṇḍalapraveśa ity adhivāsanāvidhiḥ. tato maṇḍale viṃśatividhāṃ pūjāṃ vidhāya vajraghaṇṭāṃ vādayitvā gītopahāraṃ kṛtvā saṃstutya ca samayaṃ dadyāt.
tato baddhāndhapaṭaṃ dhṛtapuṣpāñjaliṃ maṇḍaladvāre śiṣyam avasthāpya punaḥ saṃvaraṃ dadyāt.
[4.1.25–27] oṃ svabhāvaśuddhā ityādi (25) trikāyaśuddhiṃ vācayitvā, punar virata krodhāhaṃkāretyādinā (42a) maitryādinā pratiṣṭhāpya, tripraṇāmaṃ śiṣyaṃ kārayed anena oṃ pañcetyādi (39) mantrapadatrayeṇa. samayas tvaṃ samaya hor ityādi (44) mantram uccārya, kāyādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ kuryād anena:
akāro jihvāyāṃ (33ab), ikāraḥ kāye (34ab), hūṃkāro hṛdi (33cd). tataḥ pañcāmṛtaṃ dadyāt (35–36ab). paścād āveśaṃ kuryāt. tatas tiṣṭha vajra samaya pratipālaya hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hoḥ hoḥ hoḥ svāheti mantreṇa puṣpaṃ pātayet. tata oṃ jñānacakṣuḥ hūṃ svāheti mukham udghāṭya maṇḍalaṃ śiṣyāya darśayet.
pūrvadvārasthena hṛdi kṛtānjalinā śiṣyeṇa oṃ vajramaṇḍalamahāmaṇḍalapraviṣṭo 'haṃ, yogamaṇḍalamahāmaṇḍalapradarśano 'haṃ, guhyamaṇḍalamahāmaṇḍaladīkṣito 'haṃ, samaya hor iti paṭhitavyam.
tatas tasyāṅgakalanam ācāryaḥ kuryāt. tataḥ kṛtadevatāhaṃkārasya śiṣyasyodakādisekā deyāḥ.
[4.1.28] kāyetyādinā dharmadeśanām āha. samayam eṣo na rakṣitā ita eṣa kāyādisaṃyamalakṣaṇaḥ samayo na rakṣitā na rakṣaṇīyaḥ kim? api tu rakṣaṇīya ity arthaḥ. bhakṣaṇīyas tu pañcāṅkuśapañcāmṛtalakṣaṇaḥ. samayatvaṃ cānullaṅghanīyatvāt. kāyādisamatāyuktāya gambhīrodāradharmadeśanāṃ kuryād ity āha: samatetyādi.
[4.1.29] maṇḍalapraveśāya śiṣyasya mukhabandhaṃ kurvīteti darśayann āha: vastretyādi. balibhūmyeti maṇḍalabhūmau. pāṇītyādi. †...† tata iti maṇḍaladvārasamīpanayanād anantaraṃ punaḥ samvaraṃ dadyād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.30] saṃvaraṃ sūcayann āha: sarvetyādi. pūrvoktaṃ samvaragranthaṃ pāṭhayitvā sarvetyādi pāṭhayitavyaḥ. yogapīṭhaṃ maṇḍalam.
[4.1.31] prajñāsekānte caturthaṃ kathayed ity etad āha: śiṣyaṃ tv ityādi. jñeyeti jñātvā. paramārthadavādisiṃgheti: paramārthaḥ paramārthajñānaṃ, sa eva davo vanaṃ, tatrādiḥ śreṣṭhaḥ. siṃha iva siṃhaḥ, tatrābādhyarūpatvāt. paramārtharūpagambhīratādhimuktaṃ śiṣyaṃ jñātvā sutattvarājaṃ tasmai kathayed iti saṃbandhanīyam.
punaḥ kīdṛśaṃ śiṣyam ity āha: yogavān paṭṭakarpaṭachedanārtham iti: yogavantaṃ yogaratam ity arthaḥ. kimartham ity āha: paṭṭakarpaṭaṃ paṭṭamohas, tasya chedārtham.
[4.1.32] kiṃbhūtaṃ taṃ sutattvarājam ity āha: hanyatv ityādi. yat tattvaṃ kleśaṃ hanti. kiṃbhūtaṃ kleśam ity āha: bhavetyādi. bhavaḥ pañca skandhāḥ. sa eva doṣateti. doṣatā duḥkhatety arthaḥ. tasyā duḥkhatāyā jātabhūmir janmabhūmiḥ kleśas, taṃ hanti. sutattvarājena kim anyac ca syād ity āha: sidhyatv ityādi. yoga paramārtheti:
paramārthayogo mahāmudrāsiddhir ity arthaḥ. etad eva tattvam iti yathā pratipannaṃ tvayā tathā tan nānyatheti yad guruvacasā sphuṭīkṛtaṃ tad eva sutattvarājam iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.33] akāra ityādi subodham.
[4.1.34–35] pañcetyādi pañcāmṛtadānam.
[4.1.36–38] guror ityādinā vandanā.
[4.1.39–45] vajretyādināṅgakalanam. anyetyādinā vyākaraṇam.
[4.1.46–48] śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.1.49] sekādyante 'pi śikṣāṃ dadyād ity āha: svabhāvetyādi. svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ. tava ca svabhāvaḥ prakṛtiḥ, svabhāvakṛt: suṣṭhu grāhyādisvabhāvābhāvaṃ karoti bibhartīti svabhāvakṛt. kvacit svabhāvaṃ te 'bhāvata iti pāṭhaḥ. abhāvata iti lyablope pañcamī. abhāvaṃ śūnyatāṃ sātmīkṛtya tavādisvabhāvaḥ syād iti.
tataś ca na hi bhāva svabhāvasya saṃbodhikrama darśayet: niḥsvabhāveṣu bhāveṣu saṃbodhisaṃbodhyasaṃbodhakābhāvāt, paramārthataḥ kaḥ saṃbodhimārgo darśayitavya iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.50] iti nairātmyaṃ buddhisthīkṛtyākuśalaṃ pariharet kuśalaṃ saṃpādayed ity āha: kṣametyādi. kṣamāmūlo hi dharmaḥ. tataḥ sattvān na pīḍayet. kleśeṣu punar akṣamā kāryeti bhāvaḥ. pāruṣyaṃ parasollāpeti: marmodghāṭanam apriyaṃ vacanaṃ pāruṣyālāpaḥ. sa ca paruṣo niṣṭhuraḥ, paraloke 'pi duḥkhajanakatvāt.
khede sati sa jāyata iti khedo na kārya ity āha: nityetyādi. yadi syād ātmadoṣa eva paruṣālāpaḥ.
[4.1.51] śīlam āha: śīletyādi. yadi śīlahāniḥ svacittādidoṣahānāv eva. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.1.52] parasvādānaviramaṇam āha: paretyādi. yadi paradravyāharaṇaṃ tadā prajñāmbujabodhicittadravyādānam.
[4.1.53] mṛṣāparihāram āha: mṛṣetyādi. gurv iti ratnādikam. sūkṣmaṃ dhūlyādikam. tatrāpi karmaṇi yoginā mṛṣā rakṣyā. kuta ity āha: yogetyādi. yogaḥ samādhiḥ samāhitatvam. tatrācāro yeṣāṃ te yogācārā yoginas teṣāṃ mṛṣā na dṛśyate. mṛṣāvādo hy asamāhitatvaṃ, nityasamāhitāś ca yoginaḥ. yadi mṛṣā sarvadharmeṣv eva.
[4.1.54] sarvatra saṃyamaḥ sarvasiddhihetur ity āha: saṃyamam ityādi.
[4.1.55] sattvārādhanam āha: sattvam ityādi. yata ityādināśvāsam āha.
[4.1.56] śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.1.57] kāmikaṃ dānaṃ viruddham ity etad evāha: dānam ityādi. tasya aṅkuravṛddhasyeti: tasya dānalakṣaṇasya bījasyoptasya vṛddhasyeti rūḍhasyāṅkurabhūtā gatīti ṣaḍ gatayaḥ, rudhyata iti rundhanti, mārgeti buddhatvamārgam. kāmikena dānena dattena kugatiṣv api bhogāḥ syur, dānena bhogāḥ kugatāv api syur iti nyāyāt.
bhogavyāsaṅgād buddhatvamārgo ruddhaḥ syāt. tataś ca gataya eva dānāṅkurā buddhatvamārgaṃ rundhantīti bhāvaḥ. yat punar deyādyanupalambhena dānaṃ, tena buddhatvaṃ labhyata eva.
[4.1.58] prāguktam eva māṃsalayann āha: kṛta ityādi. dānasaṅgāt kleśālayavijñānaṃ svargādau dehaṃ labdhvā bhogādikam īpsitaṃ kṛteti karoti. bhogād ratikrīḍāsaṅgaḥ syāt, tataś ca mohāt punaḥ puṇyācārābhāvād durgatau patanaṃ syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.1.59] dhyānāsaktasya yoginaś caityavandanādiniṣedham āha: caityetyādi. hastamudrān na badhnīyād iti: saṃpuṭayogamudraivātra kāryeti bhāvaḥ. na tu yathākathaṃcid yogayuktasya caityavandanādiniṣedhaḥ. anyenāpi yoginānabhiniviṣṭena tat kāryam.
[4.1.60] sarvatra hy abhyāso na sulabhaḥ, śāstrapāṭhaśravaṇamātraṃ tu sulabham. pāṭhaśravaṇamātrataś ca nārthasiddhiḥ. tathā cāha:
kāyenaiva paṭhiṣyāmi vākpāṭhena tu kiṃ bhavet |
cikitsāpāṭhamātreṇa rogiṇaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati ||
iti. tad evāha: sulabhāni ca śāstrāṇi prayogās tatra durlabhā iti. paṭhituṃ śrotuṃ ca śāstrāṇi sulabhāni śakyānīty arthaḥ. vaktuṃ ko vā na paṇḍita ity abhiprāyaḥ. prayogo 'bhyāsaḥ pāṭhaśravaṇānantaram avagatārthakriyānuṣṭhānaṃ tatra śāstre 'durlabhe 'śakya iti bhāvaḥ. sa ca prayogaḥ svayaṃkāriṇā bhūtvācāryeṇa pratipādayitavya ity āha:
ācāryasya tv ityādi. ācāryo mārgaṃ darśayitvā svayam api mārgābhyāsaṃ śiṣyāyānuṣṭhānārthaṃ kurvan darśayet. ata eva vicakṣaṇaḥ. na hi vāṅmātreṇa kvacit kasyacit pravṛttiḥ. tathā ca: śilpādau kriyām api darśayanti, na tu vāṅmātram. atha vā: ācāryo mārgaṃ darśayed, abhyāsas tu śiṣyeṇa kārya iti.
yuṣmābhir eva kartavyam upadeṣṭā tathāgataḥ |
iti vacanāt.
[4.1.61] abhyāse svacittam evābhyasanīyam iti sūcayann āha: ātmetyādi. ātmā svadehaḥ. sa eva deveti devatā. paraṃ deveti: bahir niṣiktaṃ ghaṭitādikaṃ ca devatā. yathā niṣiktādikam āropitadevatāyogād devatā, tathātmāpi devatāropād devatā. tayor madhye kā sātiśayapuṇyāvāhikety āha: ātmānaṃ devam uttamam iti.
svadeho hi prāg eva varṇabhujādisaṃsthānayogād devataiva, kiṃ tu mohenātadahaṃkāraḥ. tatra ced viśeṣeṇa devataivāropyate, viśiṣṭatvāt viśiṣṭaṃ puṇyam āvahati. ātmaiva devatārūpeṇābhyasanīya iti bhāvaḥ. yady evaṃ kiṃ pratimādikaraṇenety āha: ātmetyādi.
ātmabimbe svadehadevatāyām asaktānām atatparāṇāṃ bahiḥ pratibimbādi pratimādikam, etad api hi puṇyāvāhakam. anāsaṅgād ubhayor api nāsti virodhaḥ. kiṃ ca bahiḥpratimādikaṃ stokapuṇyāvāhakam. tataś ca
svacittam eva yathā vyāsajyate tathaiva puṇyaṃ prasūyate.
[4.1.col.] ity uktakrameṇācāryalakṣaṇārādhanābhiṣekādi prakriyate yasmiṃs tad itiprakaraṇaṃ guhyapīṭham. tatra prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe guhyapīṭhe prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ.
