[4.2.1] saṃkṣiptasiddhyupāyaṃ darśayituṃ dvitīyaṃ paṭalam āha: śṛṇv ityādi. vajraprabhav iti: vajrāṇāṃ yakṣāṇāṃ prabhuḥ svāmī, tasya saṃbodhanaṃ vajraprabho. prayogāḥ kriyās tatra śāstre durlabhāḥ. tattvopadeśo durlabha ity arthaḥ. tattvopadeśaṃ vinā ko doṣa ity āha: adho caryati mantrīṇām atattvī bhavavartanam iti.
adhaś carati sa yo 'tattvī mantrī. saṃsāra eva tiṣṭhatīty arthaḥ. ata evāha: bhavavartanam iti. mukter adhaḥ saṃsāra eva.
[4.2.2] bhagavato vacanād avakāśam āgamya vajrapāṇir adhyeṣaṇām āha: kautuketyādi. he bhagavan, kautukam utpannaṃ mama. dehadharmatattvam idaṃ deśeti diśa. kathayety arthaḥ. kaḥ pramāṇasyetyādi: yogasya samādheḥ kiṃ pramāṇam. kaḥ svabhāva ity arthaḥ. mantramudrādi kīdṛśī: kiṃsvabhāvam ity arthaḥ.
[4.2.3] dehasya jalādipramāṇaṃ kim. yogināṃ balikarma ca kīdṛśam. gatim āsādhya kenātreti: kenopāyena kā gatiḥ sādhyata iti bhāvaḥ. gatīnāṃ ca kiṃ pramāṇaṃ kiṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ceti. katheti kathaya.
[4.2.4] bhagavato deśanām āha: śṛṇu tattva yathālakṣya pramāṇaṃ dehacetaseti. dehacittasya pramāṇaṃ tattvaṃ śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. yathālakṣya yathājñātam. prasiddham ity arthaḥ. anityam ityādi: ātmanaḥ śarīrasya dhātavaḥ pṛthivyaptejovāyavaḥ, anityās te sarve. balikarmādīti: baliḥ samādhiś ca. jāpitam iti mantrasya japaḥ.
[4.2.5] maṇḍalam agnim iti: agnihomaḥ. ambūnām iti: jalahomaḥ. mudrā karādimudrā. dhūpādipūjakair iti: puṣpadhūpadīpagandhanivedyādibahiḥpūjā. etat sarvam anityam iti bhāvaḥ. phalaprāptasyetyādi: asmin yadi grāhyādyabhiniveśena yogī pravartate, bhavārṇave patatīti bhāvaḥ. balikarmādiṣv abhiniveśāt sāṃsārikaphalaprāptaḥ syāt.
stokakālānubandhi ca sukhaṃ sāṃsārikaṃ phalaṃ balikarmādeḥ saṃbhavet.
[4.2.6] gatim āsādhyetyādi (3e) punaḥ pṛcchatīti darśayann āha: vajrapāṇir āhetyādi.
[4.2.7] cetaneti cittasya. pramāṇaṃ svarūpaṃ kīdṛśaṃ draṣṭavyam. yadi tasya ceti: yat tasya pramāṇaṃ tad ity arthaḥ. kutra tad ity āha: pṛthagbhūtabhavādīnām iti. pṛthak pṛthag bhave. rūpadhātau, ārūpyadhātau, kāmadhātāv ity arthaḥ. atra ca manasaḥ saṃśayo jāyate.
[4.2.8] śṛṇv ityādi bhagavato vacanaṃ darśayati. yat tvaṃ pṛcchasi saṃsāre rūpadhātvādau, tac chṛṇu. kiṃ kṛtvety āha: ceta prakṣālya cikitsayeti. ajñānacikitsayā cittaṃ prakṣālya śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. cittaṃ samādhāyety arthaḥ. sveccha jñānasya ārūpasyeti: ārūpe vijñānaṃ caryate carati. utpadyata ity arthaḥ. katham ity āha: sveccheti.
karmakleśavaśāt svecchopajāyate. tataś ca svecchāvaśenety arthaḥ. kiṃbhūtaṃ vijñānam ity āha: khageti. khena gacchatīti khagam. ka ivety āha: vāyūtīti. vāyur iva. atha vaivaṃ yojyam. kadety āha: yadā pitarau vāyuṃ visṛjatas tadety arthaḥ. ārūpyam ārūpyabhavaḥ.
sa cākāśānantāyatanā vijñānānantāyatanā ākiṃcanyāyatanā naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanā devā iti.
[4.2.9] rūpam iti rūpadhātau rūpi vijñānaṃ, tadutpattikakarmakleśasamāgatam. sidhyaty utpadyata ity arthaḥ. rūpadhātuś ca brahmakāyikāḥ, brahmapurohitāḥ, mahābrahmāṇaḥ, parīttābhāḥ, apramāṇābhāḥ, ābhāsvarāḥ, parīttaśubhāḥ, apramāṇaśubhāḥ, śubhakṛtsnāḥ, anabhrakāḥ, puṇyaprasavāḥ, bṛhatphalāḥ, abṛhāḥ, atapāḥ, sudṛśāḥ, sudarśanāḥ, akaniṣṭhāś ceti.
kāmeti kāmadhātau devāḥ. ādiśabdān manuṣyādayaḥ. devādaya eva kāminaḥ. teṣu ca vijñānam utpadyate. etad evāha: icchā bhuktiṃ tu nityaśa iti. tadutpattikakarmakleśavaśād icchā syāt, tadvaśāc ca tatra karmaphalaṃ bhuṅkta iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.10] ete trayo bhavāḥ saṃsāraḥ: etat sāṃsārety asyārthaḥ. mānuṣyair iti: mānuṣyādiṣu karmakleśavaśāt svecchā bhūmyādīti svecchayā sthānādikaṃ sidhyati.
kāmadhātuś ca  saṃjīvakālasūtrasaṃghātarauravamahārauravatapanapratāpanāvīcisaṃjñā aṣṭau mahānarakāḥ, tiryañcaḥ, pretāḥ, jambūdvīpaḥ, pūrvavidehaḥ, uttarakuruḥ, aparagodānīyaḥ, cāturmahārājakāyikāḥ, trāyastriṃśāḥ, yāmāḥ, tuṣitāḥ, nirmāṇaratayaḥ, paranirmitavaśavartinaś ceti. tasya m-uttārya vastūnīti:
tasmāt saṃsārād yenopāyavastunā samyag uttāraḥ syāt, tat saṃkṣepāc chṛṇv iti bhāvaḥ. vajradhṛg iti vajrapāṇe.
[4.2.11] gatim āsādhyeti: karmavāsanāḥ kleśavāsanāṃ sahakāriṇīm āsādya pūrvasmin vijñāne kṣīṇe 'nyad vijñānaṃ tasyāṃ tasyāṃ gatāv āsādhyety āsādayantīty arthaḥ, prāpayantīti yāvat. tad āha:
karmaṇo vāsanā grāhadvayavāsanayā saha |
kṣīṇe pūrvavipāke 'nyad vipākaṃ janayanti tat ||
iti. tatra ca grāhadvayeti grāhyagrāho grāhakagrāha iti grāhadvayam. vipākaśabdenālayavijñānam. etat tāvat sāmānyavijñānasvarūpam. yogināṃ tu vijñānaṃ prakṛtiprabhāsvaram ity āha: yogināṃ vijñāna sahajāyata iti. sahajaṃ prakṛtistham ucyate. sahajāyate sahajaṃ bhavatīty asminn arthe. prakṛtyā sārdhaṃ jāyata iti vā. idānīṃ saṃgītikāra āha:
saṃśayam ityādi. cittasya saṃśayabhavanaṃ bhagavān kathayed apanayet. tata iti vajrapāṇer vacanānantaram.
[4.2.12] kathaṃ kathayed ity āha: pratijñetyādi. jñānaṃ kīdṛśam iti saṃbandhaḥ. kiṃbhūtam ity āha: pratijñeti. prati prati jānātīti pratijñam. keṣām ity āha: bhāveti. bhāvānāṃ padārthānām ity arthaḥ. tad eva na jānātīty ajñānaṃ, tattvato 'grāhakam. kasyety āha: bhāvasyeti. bhāvasya padārthasya cittāntarasya vā.
evaṃbhūtasya vijñānasya svaṃ rūpaṃ kim anyad iti bhāvaḥ. śrotu saṃdeha bhāvasyeti: saṃdehāc chrotuṃ bhāvo 'bhiprāyo me vartata iti bhāvaḥ. vijñānaṃ jñānalakṣaṇam iti: viṣayaṃ jānātīti vijñānam iti pratijñety asya nirdeśaḥ. jñānalakṣaṇam iti: jñāyata iti jñānam avabodhas tallakṣaṇam. yena vijñā bhave bhāva iti pāṭhāntaram.
tatra vijñā vijñānaṃ, bhave bhavet, bhāve padārtheṣu. vijñānaṃ kiṃbhūtam ity āha: ajñāneti. cittāntaraṃ na jānātīti tat tathā. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat.
[4.2.13] pratijñetyādy (12a) adhyeṣaṇāvākyam. praśnavisarjanam āha: sphaṭiketyādi. sphaṭikavan nirmalatvād ākāraḥ svarūpam asyeti tat sphaṭikākāram. kiṃ tad ity āha: cetasyeti. cittaṃ vijñānam. tad bhavadoṣeṇa rañjayed iti saṃbandhaḥ. viṣayaṃ prāpya bhavantīti bhavāḥ kleśāḥ. ta eva doṣā dūṣayanti vikurvantīti kṛtvā bhavadoṣāḥ.
sphaṭikavan nirmalaṃ cittaṃ rañjayanti vikurvanti nānātvaṃ nayanti malinayantīti yāvat. kuta evam ity āha: saṃparka viṣayāṇāṃ tv iti. rūpādīnāṃ saṃyogāc cittaṃ rūpādivijñānarūpeṇa kleśavaśād bhātīti bhāvaḥ. nijadoṣa eva kathaṃ nāsyety āha: guṇa doṣā na hi cetaseti. guṇā doṣā vā na hi cetasi saṃbhavantīti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.14] yady evaṃ bhavo nirvāṇaṃ ca kathaṃ sta ity āha: yenetyādi. yas tasya cittasya bhave bhāva utpādaḥ, sa nirvāṇaviṣaya eva sthitaḥ. ubhayor api prakṛtipariśuddhatvāt. saṃsārān nirvāṇaṃ nāntaram iti bhāvaḥ. tad āha:
saṃsārād ṛte nānyan nirvāṇam iti kathyate ||
iti. anyac ca:
nirvāṇasya ca yā koṭiḥ saiva saṃsaraṇasya ca ||
iti. guṇadoṣaparihārāvasthitasya ca cittasya yad rūpaṃ, tad gurucaraṇārādhanagamyam. yathāsthānaṃ tu yoktavyam iti: rūpādiṣu ṣaṭsu viṣayeṣu cakṣurādibhir indriyaiḥ ṣaḍbhir yathākramaṃ cakṣurādivijñānāni ṣaḍ bhavantīti yathāsthānayogaḥ. etenāṣṭādaśa dhātavo 'tra kathitāḥ. pīḍayec chiṣyaputravad iti:
viṣayebhya indriyeṣv anavagrahaṇanivāriteṣv indriyajñānāny api nivāritāni syuḥ. etad eva pīḍanam. kiṃvat pīḍayed ity āha: śiṣyaputravad iti. guruḥ śiṣyam avinayamārgān nivārayaṃs tajjñānalakṣaṇam arthaṃ sādhayet, tathā yogī viṣayagrāmād anavagṛhnann indriyagrāmaṃ tajjñānaṃ ca nivārayan svasaṃvedyaṃ jñānam āsādayatīti bhāvaḥ.
atha vā vajramaṇimadhye bodhicittaṃ saṃyojya pīḍayed vārayet. yathā śiṣyaputraṃ pīḍayed eva, tathā maṇimadhye bodhicittaṃ pīḍayed eva.
[4.2.15] tasmin sati kiṃ syād ity āha: tasyetyādi. tatra pīḍane sati vijñānaṃ jñātavyam avaboddhavyam. kutra jñātavyam ity āha: madhye tv iti. rāgavirāgayor madhye. na rāgo na virāgo 'tra jñāne sta iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā: maraṇād ūrdhvaṃ nāstīty ucchedāntaḥ. nirhetukān nitya iti śāśvatāntaḥ.
tayor madhye nocchede na śāśvatabhāve 'vasthitaṃ, tadubhayāvikalpakatvāt. kim ivety āha: vijñānaṃ kham iva sadeti. viśiṣṭam asādhāraṇaṃ jñānaṃ vijñānaṃ, svayam eva saṃvedyatvāt. kham iva sadā nirmalatvāt. prāguktam eva sphaṭiketyādinā (13a) avarṇetyādinā punar viśinaṣṭi. na vidyante śuklapītādayo varṇā yasyeti tad avarṇaṃ jñānam ākāśaṃ ca.
sarve rāgā āgantukā yasyeti tat sarvarāgaṃ jñānam ākāśaṃ ca. kevalatvān na kenāpi yujyata ity ayuktaṃ jñānam ākāśaṃ ca. yuktaṃ ca dehibhiḥ padārthaiḥ sarvagatvāj jñānam ākāśaṃ ca. atha vā: ayuktam anyair dharmair anāvṛtatvāt. yuktaṃ cānyān āvṛṇoti yataḥ.
[4.2.16] rūpyate bādhyata iti rūpam. tadanyad arūpaṃ jñānam ākāśaṃ ca. sarvaṃ rūpaṃ bādhyaṃ yasya, tat sarvarūpam. nirvikāratvād vikāriyogāc ca tathābhūtam ity arthaḥ. adīrghahrasvam eva ceti: dīrghaṃ ca tad dhrasvaṃ ceti dīrghahrasvam. na tathādīrghahrasvaṃ jñānam ākāśaṃ ca nirvikāratvāt. etena prajñājñānaṃ sūcitam.
yady evam ṛddhyādikaṃ kathaṃ syād ity āha: abhāvetyādi. na bhāti dvayam asminn ity abhāvo 'dvayaṃ jñānam. tata eva sidhyati ṛddhyādikam iti bhāvaḥ. paramārthaḥ ka ity āha: tattvam eva tu cetaneti. cittam eva tattvaṃ paramārthaḥ, akṛtrimarūpatvāt.
[4.2.17] tataś ca tattvarūpasya cittasyotpādādilakṣaṇaṃ nāstīti. etad evāha: na tu lakṣaṇa cetasyeti. tattvasyeti pāṭhāntaram. sarvalakṣaṇadāyaka iti: sarvāṇi lakṣaṇāni dāyati śodhayatīti sarvalakṣaṇadāyakaṃ tac cittam. evaṃbhūtaṃ cittam avabudhya devatā bhāvayitavyety āha: abhāvetyādi.
abhāve 'dvayākāre citte bhāvanāyā devatākāracetanāyā bhāvaḥ sattā, tasya bhāvanam abhyāsas, tad api lakṣayet. nairātmyānuviddhā devatā bhāvayitavyety arthaḥ.
[4.2.18] rūpetyādi prāg eva. subodham. ālambyetyādi: sarveṇa prakāreṇa yogakāyaṃ śūnyatākaruṇābhinnarūpaṃ nirāmayeti niḥkleśaṃ sarveti devatākāram ālambyety ālambayet. etac ca bhāvanāśikṣaṇam.
[4.2.19] punaḥ kīdṛśam ity āha: gūḍhetyādi. gūḍhānīndriyāṇi tebhyo 'pi gūḍhaṃ tebhyo 'nyad ity arthaḥ. mahāgūḍham iti: viṣayāgocaratvāt. jñānagūḍhata iti: cakṣurādijñānebhyo 'pi grāhyādyabhiniviṣṭebhyo gūḍham iti bhāvaḥ. etenāntarabodharūpaṃ svasaṃvedyam indriyādirahitam eveti darśitam. śāstrapāṭhādigūḍhānām iti:
śṛṇvatāṃ paṭhatāṃ cāviṣayāl lakṣyam advayaṃ jñānaṃ dūraṃ syāt.
[4.2.20] guravo 'pi mārgamātradeśakā iti darśayann āha: lakṣyetyādi. lakṣyatattvasya yal lakṣaṇaṃ, tasya mārgaṃ gurur darśayatīti bhāvaḥ. samacetetyādi: samacittasya dehino na śarīre pīḍety arthaḥ. sukham upādeyaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca heyam. samatayā hi sukham eva.
[4.2.21] evaṃbhūtaṃ cittaṃ sādhayitum upāyam ādarśayitum āha: mana ityādi. evaṃbhūte pradeśe sukhacittaḥ san sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ ...
[4.2.22] ... sattvaparyaṅkastho dakṣiṇakaropari vāmakaraṃ dattvā paryaṅke nidhāya, cakṣurbhyāṃ nāsāgraṃ paśyan, niśvāsaṃ praveśavāyuṃ nābhau dhārayan, ...
[4.2.23] ... samam aṅgaṃ kurvann uraḥ prasārya, cittasamatāṃ svabhāvaśuddhā ityādinā vidhāya, ...
[4.2.24] ... manogandhādinā saṃpūjyāmṛtaṃ karṣed iti saṃbandhaḥ. manogandhenetyādinā manaḥśuddhir eva gandhādikam iti darśitam. bali prīṇātme iti: anena balinā pūjayā sarvaṃ śarīraṃ prīṇayet. bhakṣaṇair ity
amṛtasravaṇaiḥ.
[4.2.25] pītetyādinā navadvāramantranyāsam āha. pūrvoktaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāya hṛdi dvādaśasvaraveṣṭitaṃ hūṃkārasoṃkāramadhyaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ vīkṣya, bhrūmadhye hūṃkāram amṛtena sarvāṅgāni plāvayantaṃ sitaṃ dhyāyatāṃ yathoktaṃ syāt. oṃ hūṃ svāheti ca japtavyo mantraḥ. tatra ca vajrasattvāhaṃkāraḥ kāryaḥ.
[4.2.26–42] amṛtāmūlam ālambyeti: akāro 'mṛtaṃ tad eva mūlaṃ kāraṇaṃ vajradharotpatteḥ. ata āha: aṅkurotpattisaṃbhava iti. aṅkuro vajradharaḥ. vṛddhyante tasya tasyaiva rūpa jñāno mahātmana iti: tasyaiva vajradharasya rūpam akārabījajam ātmano vīkṣya prāg uktāṃ bhāvanāṃ japaṃ ca kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.43] sthānasaṃpattim āha: tasya yogāni m-āśritya śailādisamāśritam iti. śailādau yogam imaṃ sādhayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.44] idānīṃ vajrasattvabhāvanām āha: śrotum icchāmītyādi. subodham.
[4.2.45] śūnyatādikaṃ vibhāvyākārajaṃ bhagavantaṃ bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.46–57] hṛdītyādinā yogāntaram āha. hṛdi candrasthaṃ hūṃkāraṃ vīkṣya, śūnyatāṃ vibhāvya, ...
[4.2.58] ... vāyumaṇḍalopari candramaṇḍale hūṃkārajāṃ ...
[4.2.59] ... jñānaḍākinīṃ pūrvoktabhujādikāṃ vibhāvya, candrasthaṃ hūṃkāraṃ bhāvayan pūrvoktānuśaṃso bhavati.
[4.2.60–62] paretyādinā vajrasattvasya yogāntaram āha. paraṃ raktavarṇaṃ candramaṇḍalamadhyagataṃ svayaṃ ca raktavarṇo vajrasattvarūpo bhāvayet.
[4.2.63] svahṛdayapadmacandramadhyagataṃ raktahūṃkāraṃ vijñānaśabdenoktaṃ bhāvayet. tasya ca parasya hṛdaye svahṛdayān niṣkāśya praveśayet. tato 'py ātmahṛdaye.
[4.2.64] itthaṃ paśyan hūṃkāraṃ raktavarṇaṃ japann aṣṭaśatavārān niyataṃ jānāti paracittam. etad evāha: samatetyādi. subodham.
[4.2.65–67] idānīṃ guhyahomam āha: athāgnītyādi. subodham.
[4.2.68–69] giṇṭhā nābhasyeti: nābhigranthiḥ. sa ca sūtradvayena pāśākāreṇa veṣṭitaḥ paraspareṇādhaūrdhvaṃgatena nābhimadhye draṣṭavyaḥ. sa ca pārśvasthasaptarekhaḥ. nābhigranthau aṃkārasya madhyasthasya bindusakāśān nirgatena sūtreṇa saptadhā veṣṭanāt saptarekho nābhigranthiḥ. nābhigranther abhyantarapārśvasthatvād eva pārśvasthāḥ sapta rekhāḥ.
aṅgulaśabdena rekhāḥ.
[4.2.70] nābhigranthir evāgnikuṇḍam. veṣṭanaṃ viśinaṣṭi: nāgendretyādi. sarpaśarīravat kuṇḍalībhūtarekho nābhigranthir iti bhāvaḥ. aṃkāro vahniḥ. granther adho vāyumaṇḍalam. jvalitā sarvavarṇasyeti: rekhāsu svaravyañjanāni draṣṭavyānīti bhāvaḥ. tasya madhye tu padmavad iti: nābhigranther upari padmam ity arthaḥ.
[4.2.71] padmavaraṭake sūryasthā jñānaḍākinī tadbījaṃ vā. giṇṭhā sitabhi jvālaiva vajraḍākinī nīyatām iti: pūrvadale vajraḍākinī śvetā tadbījaṃ vā.
[4.2.72] dakṣiṇe ghorī pītā tadbījaṃ vā. paścime raktā vettālī tadbījaṃ vā.
[4.2.73] vāme caṇḍālī tadbījaṃ vā. pañca m-agniṃ tu ātmānam iti: ātmā pañcamas tatrāgnau. sa ca jñānaḍākiny eva. gurūpamam iti: gurur aṅguṣṭho buddhiḥ.
[4.2.74] apaḥ pūrve, tejo dakṣiṇe, vāyuḥ paścime, pṛthvī cottare. ākāśaviśuddhyā jñānaḍākinī pañcamī madhye. nābhikuṇḍāgnau devatā bhāvayann āhutīr amṛtadhārā hūṃkārān sravatīr asakṛd bhāvayet.
[4.2.75] amṛtalambakālayam iti: amṛtam aṣṭādaśasthānam avalambamānaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ vahnau patad bhāvayet. gavākṣoraṃ tv iti: bhrūmadhyagatahūṃkāram adhomukham āhuḥ.
[4.2.76] agnītyādinā aṃkāram eva darśayati. sa cāgnyādhāre. tasya cādhovāyunā preryamāṇasya tejasā hūṃkārabinduḥ sravann āhutiḥ syād iti bhāvaḥ. itthaṃ bhāvayatāṃ pauṣṭikaṃ rasāyanaṃ syāt.
[4.2.77] kuta ity āha: yad ityādi. anayā bhāvanayā khādyapeyādikaṃ viśuddhaṃ yataḥ syād iti bhāvaḥ. asyetyādi: anena krameṇa yaś carati bhuṅkte yogī, tasya rṇaparibhogo na syāt. dānapate ṛṇaṃ na syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.2.col.] ity uktakrameṇāmṛtabhāvanādiguhyahomāntaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaṃ guhyapīṭham.
iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe guhyapīṭhe dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ.
%
[4.3.1] idānīṃ vāyvabhyāsaviśeṣabhūtapramāṇādijñānāya tṛtīyapaṭalam āha: śrutam ityādi. he deva, śarīre vāyunā cittayogas tasya lakṣaṇaṃ śrotum icchāmīti bhāvaḥ. cittasamatā kathaṃ yoktavyā? kathaṃ cittena samaṃ vāyoḥ saṃbandha ity arthaḥ. asthitaṃ markaṭopamam iti: yato vāyuś cittaṃ cāsthito 'sthiraḥ, tataḥ kathaṃ tayor yoga iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.2] śṛṇv ityādinā praśnavisarjanam. guhyam iti vāyoḥ praveśaḥ. dolā ceti nirgamaḥ. abhedād dvayaṃ śṛṇv iti saṃbandhaḥ. cittaṃ samatayā sthitam. kiṃ tad ity āha: nirvāsyaṃ kāli vartanam iti. nirvāsyaṃ nirgato vāyuḥ, sa ca kāliḥ prajñā. vartanam iti: praviṣṭo vāyuḥ. sa upāya iti jñeyam. etenaitad uktaṃ syāt:
cittam eva vāyvārūḍhaṃ nirgacchati praviśati ca. ata eva vāyur eva vijñānam ity abhedāt. tataś ca vāyurūpeṇa nirgatya daśadiggatam amṛtaṃ gṛhītvā punaḥ praviśya hṛdi sthitaṃ cittaṃ punas tathaiva nirgacchat praviśac ca bhāvayet. cittaṃ cittam eva harṣasvarūpaṃ gūhatītthaṃ prajñopāyayogaḥ. yathāha:
sukhair harṣaiś ca sidhyante prajñopāyādibhiḥ sadā ||
iti. asminn abhyāsaprakarṣaparyantagamanād daśabalādiguṇāḥ syur ity upadeśaḥ. gurutaś ca sphuṭībhaviṣyati.
[4.3.3] āpa sarve pramāṇasyeti: sarvaśarīre 'pām astitve pramāṇam. kiṃ tad ity āha: aṅkurodbhavam iti. aṅkurā romāṇi. snehād dhi bījāṅkurāḥ prarohanti. na tu āpasya dehānāṃ pramāṇeti: śarīre 'pāṃ pramāṇam iyatā nāsti. kuta ity āha: apa dehakam ityādi. †...†
[4.3.4] sarvadehe vyāpikā āpaḥ. pramāṇā āpa buddhimān ity upasaṃhāraḥ. etat tāvad apām astitve pramāṇaṃ buddhimān jānātv iti bhāvaḥ. vajrī ca jalasvabhāveti darśayann āha: vajrītyādi. pramāṇetyādi: appramāṇā vajrīti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.5] agnir api coṣṇatvāt khāditapītādipākāc cānumīyata iti bhāvaḥ. ata evāha: agnītyādi.
[4.3.6] ghorī cāgnisvabhāvā.
[4.3.7] chidrer antara ṛṇṇivad iti: nāsārandhrādiś chidriśabdena. chidrer evāntara ṛṇṇiḥ. tena śvāsādinirgamapraveśāder vāyur anumīyate.
[4.3.8] vāyusvabhāvā vettālī.
[4.3.9] pṛthivyām ityādi. sarvam āsthānam iti: sarve jalādayaḥ padārthās tiṣṭhanty asminn iti sarvasthānaṃ pṛthvī. anyathā jalādeḥ śarīre sthitir na syāt. rūpāṇi viṣayā indriyāṇi ca prasiddhāni. rūpendriyāṇy eva guṇā āśritatvāt. tair bhūṣitā pradhānīkṛtā pṛthvīti rūpendriyaguṇabhūṣitam ity asyārthaḥ. pramāṇaṃ ālambya bhūtam iti:
śarīre pṛthivyastitve pramāṇam ālayabhāva iti bhāvaḥ. etat pṛthivyā rūpaṃ khakkhaṭatvādikaṃ ca dṛśyate.
[4.3.10] tato 'pi tatra tasyā astitvaṃ, guṇasaṃyogāc ca pṛthvī sundarīti bhaṇyata iti nāmākhyānam. caṇḍālī ca pṛthvīsvabhāvā.
[4.3.11] idānīṃ śarīre vijñānāstitve pramāṇam āha: hartetyādi. hartākarteti: śubhāśubhavyāmiśrakarmakartā. kadācic chubhaṃ karoti kadācid aśubhaṃ kadācid vyāmiśraṃ ceti hartā. akaraṇe sati hi na karmaharaṇam. yadi vijñānaṃ nāsti śubhāśubhādivyāpāro 'pi tadā śarīre na syāt. sa cāsti, tataḥ kartā. bhokteti tatphalasya.
saṃśayaṃ ca chinatti, tataś ca mano 'sti. vijñānam ity arthaḥ. indriyaṃ sarva cetasyeti: indriyāṇi sarvāṇi, cetasyeti: vijñānasya. lakṣya lakṣaṇajñātaya iti: lakṣyeti viṣayā lakṣaṇajñātaya iti vijñānāni. etāni śarīre vijñānāstitve pramāṇam iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.12] viṣayendriyasaṃyogād rājendravad viṣayasukham anubhavatīti dehīty api pramāṇam. itthaṃ tāvad dehe vijñānaṃ rājate pramāṇāt. evaṃ ca yogī budhyate, jñānetyāder ayam arthaḥ.
[4.3.13] idānīṃ pramāṇāntaram āha: vijñānam ityādi. abdhātor astitve śarīre vijñānāstitvam. yadi vijñānābhāve 'pi jalaṃ śarīre syāt, tadā cyutasya mṛtasya kiṃ nāpaḥ syuḥ?
[4.3.14] yadi vijñānābhāve 'pi vahniḥ syāt, tadā gatajñānasyoṣṇatā kathaṃ na syāt?
[4.3.15] yady asati vijñāne śarīre vāyus tadā gatavijñānasya śvāsasphurtiḥ kathaṃ na syāt?
[4.3.16] yadi gatavijñānasya śarīrasya pṛthvīyogas tadā mṛtasya lāghavaṃ kutaḥ? etac ca katham ity āha: udake plavanāyata iti. udake mṛtaṃ śarīraṃ plavate tarati yataḥ.
[4.3.17] pramāṇaṃ pañca dehasya pañcasya m-ekabhāvateti: śarīre vijñānāstitve pramāṇam idam, pañceti prapañcataḥ. prapañcasyāpy ekabhāvataikarūpatā. etad evāha: eketyādi. yogibhir ekatvena vijñānagatir īkṣyate.
[4.3.18] tad evaṃ ca jñātuṃ kalyāṇamitrasya sevā guror upāsanā kāryā.
[4.3.19] indriyam ityādi: viṣayebhyo 'pīndriyāṇi tadvijñānāny api pīḍayatāṃ bandhanaṃ saṃsāraḥ syāt. niyamavratādinā na śarīrapīḍā kāryeti nirdiśati. vijñānabandhanān muktir evety āha: bandhayed ityādi. madhyasthānaṃ tv iti: hṛdaye vijñānaṃ badhnīyāt. aṇḍajāgṛhaśabdenāṇḍam eva.
tatra yathā vijñānam ālīyata ity ālayaṃ karmakleśair badhyate, tathā yogināpi bandhanīyaṃ vijñānam.
[4.3.20] bandhanayogam evāha: calitetyādi. calitā vāyus, tasya veṣṭenābhyāsena badhnīyād iti śeṣaḥ. kathaṃ ca vāyor abhyāsa ity āha: dolādolāya cetaneti. dolānilanirgamaḥ. dolāyeti praveśaḥ. taccintayābhyāsaḥ kārya iti bhāvaḥ. cetaneti vijñānam evaivaṃ bhāvyam iti bhāvaḥ. āsanetyādi: sukhāsane padmāsanāsīno bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.21] hetum ityādi: hetusvabhāvapraṇidhānaśūnyatā sūcitā. yoginām iti yogī. vijñānaṃ vijñānamātraṃ sarvam. rūpādikaṃ na syāt, tat kathaṃ bhāvanā syād ity āha: rūpasaṃjñetyādi. rūpādīnāṃ bhāvaḥ prajñopāyasaṃbandhasiddhaye rūpādyāropa iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.22] dolājapam āha: kadalītyādi. kadalīpuṣpākāraṃ nābhau sthitaṃ bhāvayed iti śeṣaḥ. nābhipadme kadalīpuṣpākāram aṃkāraṃ bhāvayed iti samudāyārthaḥ. ucchrite hṛdayavasthitam iti: upari hṛdaye 'py adhomukhaṃ padmam aṣṭadalam. riktamukham iti: kiṃcidvivṛtamukhapadmam. āvarttam ity adhomukhatvaṃ darśayati. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.3.23] uktakesarachādanam iti: prasiddhakesarānvitam. uparipadmavaraṭakamadhye jñānavijñānaṃ hūṃkāraṃ yojayet.
[4.3.24] nābhisthitasya padmasyopari saṃyoga pūrvabhāgaḥ. tatra vahniraśmayaḥ ṣaḍaṅgulāḥ. nābhipadmasyādhobhāge giṇṭhā paścimabhāgaḥ. tatra pañcāṅgulā raśmayaḥ.
[4.3.25] tasya kṣetrasyeti: yathā kṣetre bījāṅkurās tathā raśmaya iti bhāvaḥ. tasya bījasyetyādi: tatrāpy uparipadme hūṃkārabījam adhomukhaṃ kadalīpuṣpavat. raśmīnāṃ āvartteti: raśmiyuktam ity arthaḥ. kecit kadalīpuṣpā sthitā nābheti (22a): hṛdayāt kadalīpuṣpavat kadalīkalikevādhomukhaṃ padmaṃ nābhyuparisamīpagataṃ kiṃcidvivṛtamukham.
uktakesarachādanam iti (23b): adhaḥ keśarachannaṃ nābhau ca padmaṃ draṣṭavyam. tasyāṣṭadalasya varaṭake aṃkāras, tadraśminā kiṃcidvivṛtamukham ūrdhvapadmam iti. śeṣaṃ pūrvavat.
[4.3.26] aṃkāraraśminā sūkṣmeṇādhomukhaṃ hūṃkāraṃ hṛdayasthitapadmasthaṃ hanyād iti darśayann āha: pañcetyādi. pañcasphoṭikabījaṃ hūṃkāraḥ. nimnam iti: adhobījaraśminā. padmakāntiṃ sphālayed vikāsayet. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.3.27–28] adhaḥpadmasya pūrvādidiggatadalacatuṣṭaye krameṇa vāmāvarttataḥ suṃkṣuṃ-yuṃ-huṃ-bījāni. svaretyādināpy aṃkāra evoktaḥ, sa ca madhya eva.
[4.3.29] aiśānādividigdaleṣu smyruṃ-ymyruṃ-hmyruṃ-kṣmyruṃ-bījāni. nemibhāge ṛ-ṝ-ḷ-ḹ-bījāni sarvato draṣṭavyāni. uparipadme 'py evaṃ bījānāṃ vinyāsaḥ. evaṃ hṛdaye 'pi padmagatam akṣaraṃ hūṃkāro dṛśyo draṣṭavyaḥ.
[4.3.30] aṃkāraraśminā codyamāno hūṃkāro 'mṛtaṃ tathā varṣayati yathā sarvam adho'ṅgaṃ snapayitvā sarvam ūrdhvam api snapatīty ata āha: gavākṣyoraṃ tv ityādi. gavākṣyoraṃ śarīram. navadhārateti: nava dvāraṇāni paripūrya snapayatīti bhāvaḥ. pratiromachidranirgatair amṛtaiḥ sarvāṅgaṃ snapayed dhūṃkāra iti.
atra ca vāyau nirgacchati saty aṃkāraraśminā hūṃkāro dravati. vāyau praviśati pīyūṣaṃ sarvasminn aṅge sravatīti bhāvyam. dolāhārālakṣaṇo japaḥ. aṃ-hūṃ-bījābhyāṃ prajñopāyarūpābhyām anuccāro japaḥ.
[4.3.31] dolājāpaś cocyate: jñānavijñānabījasyeti. hūṃkārasya japo 'yam.
[4.3.32] abhyāsasya sarvakālatvaṃ darśayann āha: caṅkrama ityādi. yāne gamane. nisadye niṣadane. sarvakālata iti: bhojanādāv apīti bhāvaḥ. japabhāvasyeti: bhāvanājapasevaṃ kurvan ...
[4.3.33] ... saṃsāram uttared ity arthaḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.3.34] idānīm utkrāntim āha: śṛṇv ityādi.
[4.3.35–37] binduśabdena bhrūmadhyam. pāneti vajrarandhram. apānasyeti gudamārgaḥ.
[4.3.38] nābhe kāmikasvargasyeti: nābhimārgeṇa yadi vijñānaṃ yāti, tadā kāmadhātau devo bhavati. bhrūmadhyarandhreṇa yadā gacchati, tadā rūpadhātau jāyate. ūrdhveti kanakadvāreṇa yadā gacchati, tadā maraṇād ūrdhvaṃ śīghram eva gater gatyantaraṃ viśiṣṭaṃ gacchati.
[4.3.39–40] bhavadvāraṃ mukham. apānamārgeṇa yadā yāti, tadā śīghram evāṣṭau narakān gacchati.
[4.3.41] viśiṣṭena mārgeṇa viṣiṣṭā gatiś ced utkrāntyā ca vivakṣitadvāreṇa vijñānaṃ netuṃ śakyam ity utkrāntir abhyasanīyeti tasmād ityāder ayam arthaḥ.
[4.3.42] utkrāntiś ca mṛtyucihnaṃ dṛṣṭvā mṛtyusamīpagatena kāryā. etad evāha: mṛtyukālasyetyādi. jarādibhiś ca yadi bhedaḥ syāt, tadā notkrāntiḥ kartuṃ śakyate. tasmān mṛtyukālaṃ ṣaṇmāsādikam ālokya svastha evotkrāntiṃ kuryāt.
[4.3.43] pūrvaṃ kumbhakena dvārastambhanaṃ kāryam. pañcasphoṭiketyādi: pañcasphoṭiko hūṃkāraḥ pītaḥ stūpadvāre bhrūmadhye bhāvyaḥ.
[4.3.44] tato 'dhaḥ suṃkāraḥ śuklas toyaviśuddhaḥ. pāne mūtramārge, apāne ca gudamārge 'gnibījaṃ kṣmryuṃ, kanakadvāre kṣuṃ, cakṣuṣor nīlo huṃkāraḥ, ghrāṇarandhrayo raktaṃ yuṃbījaṃ, mukhe śuklaṃ suṃbījam. tad etat sakalaṃ tato nimnasyetyādinā darśayati.
[4.3.45] vāyuvarṇo nīlaḥ. vāyumaṇḍala cetaseti hūṃkāraḥ.
[4.3.46] vāyubījaṃ yuṃkāraḥ. bindunā nādenokāreṇa ca yogaḥ.
[4.3.47] vajrībījaṃ suṃkāraḥ. ghorāṇām iti ghorībījaṃ kṣuṃkāraḥ. aṅkuśādi tv iti hikśabdaḥ. ghorā aṃkāraḥ. ekaviṃśatisvāsakair iti: abhyāsānantaram ekaviṃśatikṣaṇair utkrāntiḥ syāt.
[4.3.48] pada ityādi: etenāṅgapratyaṅgam uktam. ūrṇetyādinā palitastambhanam uktam.
[4.3.49] ghoranādeneti: aṃkāraraśminā. aṣṭāntabījaṃ hūṃkāraḥ. vargetyādinā aṃkāram eva viśinaṣṭi.
[4.3.50] adhovāyupreryamāṇena aṃkāraraśminā hūṃkāro hantavyaḥ. yukta vātāni bījasya vāyumaṇḍala cetaseti: prathamaṃ śūnyatābhāvanādinā devatāyogī pūrvoktanyāyena hūṃkārādibījaiḥ kṛtanavadvārastambhano hṛdaye vāyumaṇḍalamadhyagato hūṃkāraḥ, vāyumaṇḍalasyādhastād upari ca yuṃbījadvayam;
yadā cordhvavāyupreryamāṇo 'ṃkāraraśmir hṛdgatapadmasthaṃ hūṃkāraṃ spṛṣṭvā kaṇṭhadeśe śirasi ca samaṃ vāyunā gacchati, tadā pūrvoktavāyumaṇḍalasyādhovāyubījasyādho hibījam, ūrdhvavāyubījasyādhaḥ kśabdaḥ; yadā śiraḥparyantaṃ gatvāpy adho gacchati vāyus, tadā viparītaṃ hiketi bījadvayam; itthaṃ gamanāgamanābhyāṃ hikamantram uccārayan bhāvayet.
[4.3.51] ittham ekaviṃśatikṣaṇair abhyāsānte niyatam utkrāntiṃ karoti. yena dvāreṇecchati, tena niṣkrāmayati vijñānam. ūrdhva ūrdhva paraṃ tatheti: ūrdhva śirogataṃ padmaṃ dvātriṃśaddalaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ tatrasthaṃ hūṃkāram adhomukhaṃ tathaivāṃkāraraśminā codyamānam amṛtaṃ varṣantaṃ dhyāyan niyataṃ palitaṃ stabhnāti. ardhamūlaṃ tu bhakṣayed iti:
kheṭaśukrarajāṃsi sarvāṇy ekīkṛtya bhakṣayet. etenāpi palitastambhādikaṃ syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.3.52] utkrānter anuśaṃsām āha: devetyādi. devatāvipraghātī, pañcānantaryakārī, ...
[4.3.53] ... adattādāyī ca, anenotkrāntivijñānagamanena utpāde 'pi tad utkrāntaṃ vijñānaṃ pāpaliptaṃ na syāt ...
[4.3.54] ... yathā paṅkotpannaṃ padmaṃ na paṅkena na ca jalagatena vijjalādidoṣeṇa lipyate.
[4.3.55] mṛtyukāla evotkrāntiḥ kāryā, anyathā devatāghātakaḥ syāt. tato mṛtyucihnaṃ vīkṣyotkrāmayed vijñānam.
[4.3.56–67] tato bāhyādītyādi: uktād yogād anyo yogo bāhya ucyate. anyo yogaḥ kathyata iti bhāvaḥ. dvigu†bhuvane dvayam† iti: bhuvanadvayaṃ nābhir hṛdayaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ. bhuvanadvayagatacitto yogī vakṣyamāṇaṃ cintayed iti bhāvaḥ. vāyumaṇḍala cetaseti: vāyumaṇḍalaṃ nābher adhastād bhāvayet.
[4.3.68] tasya madhye tu agnīnām iti: vāyumaṇḍalopari vahnimaṇḍalam. tasya madhye tu sūryavad iti: vahnimaṇḍalamadhye aṃkāraḥ sūryavat. etad evāha: ālītyādi. ālir alir ity arthaḥ. sa ca hūṃkāro hṛtpadmagataḥ. aṃkāro raktavarṇaḥ, sa ca vahnāv eva.
[4.3.69] jñānabījaṃ hūṃkāraṃ nirgacchadvāyupreryamāṇenāṃkāraraśminā hanyamānaṃ cintayet.
[4.3.70] padmaṃ ca hṛdgataṃ raśminā hanyamānam adhomukham apy ūrdhvamukhaṃ cintayet. itthaṃ ca kaṇṭhaśiraḥparyantaṃ vāyor gatāgataṃ punaḥ punaś cintayet.
[4.3.71–72] itaś ca bhāvanāvidhiḥ siddhiṃ gacchati. utkrāntisiddhiḥ syād ity arthaḥ.
[4.3.73] idānīm ādhyātmikakarmaprasaram āha: tata ityādi. tata utkrāntiyogād bāhyam anyaṃ yogaṃ kathayiṣyāmīti bhāvaḥ. sādhakasya tu nirmitam iti: sādhakārtham ity arthaḥ. paracittānucetasya jñāpayed iti:
vāyudīpitāṃkāraraśminā svadakṣiṇanāsāpuṭanirgatena sādhyasya vāmanāsāpuṭapraviṣṭena tasya hṛdayād vijñānam ādāya taddakṣiṇanāsāpuṭanirgatena svavāmanāsāpuṭapraviṣṭenātmanas tejaḥsthāne nikṣiptaṃ pacyamānaṃ ca punaḥ punaś cintayet. yāvan na mriyate, tāvan mūkavat tiṣṭhatīti paretyāder ayam arthaḥ.
[4.3.74] ittham eva japo bhāvanā ca susamāhitacittānāṃ syād iti bhāvaḥ. yad yac cintyate, sarvaṃ tad avikalaṃ sidhyatīti pūrvayogasyaivānuśaṃsā.
[4.3.col.] ity uktakrameṇa vāyvabhyāsajalādiśarīrasthityanumānādikaṃ prakriyate 'sminn itītiprakaraṇaṃ tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ. sa ca guhyapīṭhe. iti catuṣpīṭhanibandhe guhyapīṭhe tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ.
%
[4.4.1] idānīṃ guhyād guhyaṃ darśayituṃ caturthapaṭalam āha: śrotum ityādi subodham.
[4.4.2–8] jñānaḍākinīyogī bhāvanāṃ kuryād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.4.9–12] maitrītyādi: akṣobhyasya hūṃ, ratneti ratnasambhavasya āṃ.
[4.4.13] amitety amitābhasya hrīṃ. amoghasiddheḥ khaṃ.
[4.4.14] svaretyādi: nābhipadmavaraṭake aṃkāranyāsa uktaḥ.
[4.4.15] madhya ityādinā hṛtpadmamadhye hūṃbījam uktam. sarvam idaṃ pūrvavad bhāvayet.
[4.4.16–18] sarvaṃ cet svabhāvaśūnyatvād asāraṃ kim anena bhāvitena phalam ity āha: asāreṇa tu sārāṇām amṛtārambhamocavad iti. asārāpi kadaly amṛtopamaṃ phalaṃ sāraṃ phalati yathā tathā niḥsāramantradevatābījādikaṃ bhāvyamānaṃ saṃbodhyādilakṣaṇaṃ sāraṃ phalaṃ phalati.
[4.4.19] etad evāha: madhya ityādi. niḥsāre rūpe jñānaṃ sāram ity arthaḥ. jñānakāyasya na tu lakṣaṇam asti paramārthataḥ.
[4.4.20–21] yasyetyādi: yaḥ sarvākārasvarūpaṃ jñātvaivākāraṃ bhāvayet tasya sambodhiḥ śīghram evehaiva janmani syād iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.4.22] śāstreṇa kim ity āha: lakṣaṇam ityādi. śāstreṇa tattvalakṣaṇaṃ deśanāto jñāyata iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.4.23] bhāvanāntaram āha: pañcetyādi.
[4.4.24] hṛtpadme hūṃbījaṃ, bindumadhye bhrūmadhye hūṃbījaṃ, tac cāṅguṣṭhāparimaṇḍala iti ṭhakāraveṣṭitam.
[4.4.25–26] tasya madhye tv iti: nābhipadmamadhye aṃkārabījam. tasya raśminā bālāgraśatāṃśaparimāṇena ...
[4.4.27–28] ... hṛtpadmabhrūmadhyakanakadvāragatena tadbījāmṛtaṃ pradrāvya sarvam aṅgaṃ siñced iti punaḥ punar bhāvayet. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.4.29–31] mana ityādi: mana eva bhāvayed iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.4.32–38] evaṃ bhāvayatāṃ puṃsāṃ nimittaṃ darśayann āha: pradīpetyādi.
[4.4.39] kleśoddhṛtam iti: kleśatānavam.
[4.4.40–43] śūnyatāṃ bhāvayituṃ sarvatra prathamaṃ hūṃkāraṃ hṛdi bhāvayed ity etad evāha: hṛdayetyādi subodham.
[4.4.44] hūṃkāreṇa sarvaśarīraṃ kṣayaṃ nītvā tato hūṃkāraṃ bhasmapuñje dṛṣṭvā tadamṛtamayena raśminā punar utpādayet.
[4.4.45–46] kṣiti kuṭilam ity āhu diśa-r-aṣṭamadhātukair iti: kṣitigarbhavajrapāṇyādayaḥ prathamapīṭhaprathamapaṭaloktās tasyotpāditasya śarīrastūpasya pūrvādidikṣu vimoktavyāḥ. śeṣaṃ subodham.
[4.4.47–57] itthaṃ śarīram eva pūjayed yogī. bahiścaityavandanā caivam abhiprāyeṇa niṣiddhā. dharmadhātuyoga iti: dharmāṇāṃ skandhādīnāṃ dhātur āśrayaḥ śarīraṃ tasya yogaḥ śūnyatayā saṃbandhaḥ. atha vā dharmadhātuḥ śūnyatā tayā śarīrasya yogaḥ. dharmadhātuśabdena caityaṃ śarīraṃ cocyate.
[4.4.58–60] ādāv ityādinā pūrvoktam eva nirdiśati.
[4.4.61–85] asya yogasya bhāvanārthaṃ sthānasampattim āha: vana ityādi. subodham.
[4.4.86] īdṛśe sthāne bhaktādirahite kathaṃ yogaṃ yuñjītety āha: nāgetyādi. nāgeti nāgabalā, gorakṣataṇḍuleti yasya prasiddhiḥ, tasya mūlam. †...† palāśapuṣpam. kuṣṭheti kuṣṭham. tṛtīyabhāgata iti: pratyekaṃ palatrayam. māgadhe palam ekaṃ tv iti: pippalīpalam ekam. sarvam etat pratyekaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇīkartavyam. samabhāgaṃ tv iti: ekīkṛtya.
[4.4.87] tasmāt karṣam ekam ādāya gokṣīreṇa pibeta ṣaṇmāsān yāvat.
[4.4.88] varśaṣataṃ yāvat kṣutpipāsādinā na bādhyate yogī tadbhaiṣajyabhogata iti bhāvaḥ.
[4.4.89] bhaiṣajyaṃ kurvan vinīto bhogavarjitaḥ syāt. tataḥ śarīraṃ rakṣati. atha vā bhaiṣajye kṛte vinayādikaṃ syād iti.
[4.4.90] mantramudrādevatādikaṃ mithyāpratyayena bhāvanājapādinā sākṣātkartavyam ity āha: yathāṅganetyādi. yathā nārībālādijanakathāyāṃ prekṣāvān na yatnaṃ karoti śṛṇoti tacchravaṇāt sukhādikam anubhavati ca tathā tad anumodate taiḥ saha †...† saṃparkaṃ kathāṃ ca kathayati na ca sajyate tatra, tathaiva mārgo deśayitavyaḥ.
tato mārgābhyāsān na ca bandhanaṃ syāt. evaṃvidha eva yogaḥ. ito 'nyathā kumārgo darśyata iti bhāvaḥ. atha vā: yathā bālāṅganādikathāvac chrotum icchati tantram idaṃ tasmai na mārgaḥ kathyata iti bhāvaḥ. tasya bandhanam eva, yataḥ sa kumārgadarśī.
[4.4.91] bauddhena vedādimantrābhimantritaṃ dūrvādikaṃ na śirasi dhāryaṃ, na tīrthikadevatā vandyā, yato 'bhiṣekamantrādipavitratāhāniḥ syāt. etad evāha: bauddhenetyādi. bauddhācāryeṇety arthaḥ. bhiṣekety abhiṣekaḥ.
[4.4.92–93] te nā hūṃ te nā hūṃ te nā te te hūṃ iti: te kāyavākcittātmakāḥ skandhadhātvāyatanalakṣaṇāḥpadārthāḥ. neti pratiṣedhe. grāhyagrāhakādirūpeṇa te na santi prakṛtiprabhāsvaratayā santīti bhāvaḥ. kuta ity āha: hūṃ iti. hūṃ iti: cittamātraṃ sarvaṃ yata ity arthaḥ. ayaṃ mantro kāyādiviśuddhyā vāratrayam uccārayitavyaḥ.
svecchavi satta-uttāru iti: svecchayā sattvottārakaḥ.
[4.4.94–99] akṣaram ityādinā tantrasya puṇyamaheśākhyatvaṃ darśitam.
[4.4.100] likhanādikriyāṃ kurvatāṃ cyutyanantaram arūpadhātāv utpattiḥ syāt. yogābhiratānāṃ tu saṃbodhiḥ śīghram eva.
[4.4.101] kuta ity āha: abhinandann itītyanto 'tra caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ. caturbhiś cāmībhiḥ paṭalair guhyapīṭham. atroktaṃ yat tat sarvaṃ sugopyatvād guhyaṃ tasya pīṭham. anyat subodham iti.
guṇavan matilocanaṃ na me
na ca vāṇī matimanmanoharā |
guṇavīkṣaṇabhāṣaṇakṣamaḥ
katham asmi prasarattamastatiḥ ||
paṭubuddhiśikhānubandhibhis
tamasāṃ nityam ahetubhir janaiḥ |
śucijanmabhir eva dīpavad
vihitālokam idaṃ yathārthadṛk ||
atha mandatayāpi bhaktimān
vyabhajaṃ tantram idaṃ śubhair na kim |
api lāñchanadūṣitaḥ śaśī
jagadarthaṃ sphuṭayed dhi raśmibhiḥ ||
ata eva vidhānato mahān
anumeyo mama puṇyasaṃcayaḥ |
guṇadoṣavido 'py amatsarā
iha yūyaṃ vrajata pramāṇatāṃ ||
adhigamya vineyamānasaṃ
cirasaṃvāsavaśena bhūyasā |
nijam āśayam arpayiṣyati
sthirabuddhau gurur atra buddhimān ||
nijacittam idaṃ jagattrayaṃ
sukhaduḥkhādi tato 'nubhūyate |
prakṛtiś ca nijasya cetasaḥ
kimiva pratyavabudhyate svayam ||
mayā catuṣpīṭham idaṃ vivṛṇvatā
yad arjitaṃ puṇyam ananyabhaktinā |
śaranniśānāthamarīcinirmalāt
tato janāḥ santu sadarthavedinaḥ ||
catuṣpīṭhanibandhaḥ samāptaḥ.
